### 38. Formats, ledger entries, contended files and what W22.2 does not take

- **Formats:** no version changes: the four widgets are version 1 and the members of W22.1's components land while they are unlanded (decision 2); the Ui settings section changes the `.eproj`'s canonical form only; no replay, pak, manifest, SpriteSheet, StringTable or font format changes.
- **Ledger positions taken:** PlaySessionPhases UiInput (after ApplyInput, TextLatch reserved between them for C22.3) and UiEvents (after StartFlush); the ui state-hash block (after physics2d); the Ui settings section (after Localization); the Ui category's four components (appended); `UI_WIDGET_PART_INVALID` (after M22's UI codes); `ui.createWidget` (`RegisterMethods.cpp`, the 67th tool, the scene-target inventory); the Ui module (after `Loc`), the six callbacks and their reserved names (appended); IdSeeds 2010 to 2012.
- **Files without a ledger owner that W22.2 appends to** (a later lander appends its entries after main's): `Scene/RenderExtraction.h`'s request (`UiInteraction` after Quality); `Scripting/ScriptHost.h` (`GetUi`, a defaulted virtual, so no other host changes); `EditorCore/Viewport/EditorViewportState.h` and `EditorCore/Automation/ViewportMethods.{h,cpp}` (`uiPreview`; M21's 2D mode may add an option beside it); `Editor/Panels/SceneHierarchyPanel.cpp` (the create menu's calls); `Tests/Source/Support/ScriptTestFixture.h` (`Ui`).
- **M21's files W22.2 only calls into** (ADR 0028's ledger): `InspectorPanel.cpp` (the UiRect drawer), `EditorHostViewports.cpp` and `SceneViewportPanel.cpp` (the preview and the overlay). The later of M21 and M22 adapts after merging main. No ledger amendment is needed.
- **Not in W22.2:** TextLatch, text input, fallback fonts, shaping and world canvases (C22.3); the combined 2D/UI test (the later lander of M21 and M22; UiInput's pointer mapping is the hook it needs, decision 24); `EngineVersionString` (M23's bump). W22.2 adds no reader of external input (no fuzz target) and no GPU object (no recreation hook).

### 39. Gate and verification (C22.2)

- **The contract checkpoint** runs `Generate.py`, the Debug build of every project, `Lint.py --allow-contract-stubs`, `Format.py --check`, `GenerateDocs.py --config Debug` (the regenerated references staged), `Test.py --suite unit --config Debug --allow-skips` on the touched suites, each skipped case forced with `--no-skip` to show it fails, and the MCP schema tests; not `PreCommit.py` or `CI.py`.
- **Each stream** runs the cases and suites its change touches once at the end (ADR 0023): A the Scene, Session, Project and Automation cases and `test_ui`; B the Scene, Scripting and EditorCore validation cases (the scripting coverage case included) and the validation fixtures' check; C the Scene cases and `Test.py --suite gpu --require-gpu --filter "UiPass*"` (the tints reach the pass unchanged); D the EditorCore and Editor cases and `test_ui_authoring`; E `Scaffold.py`, `Scaffold.py --check`, the feature suite and the golden suite for its golden. A case that needs two streams (the keyboard-and-gamepad case, the drag-and-wheel case and the Ui settings' bindings case need A, B and C, the last because it sets `UiCanvas.DefaultFocus`, stream C's registration, before the play copy; the hover-and-click case, the default-focus case, the phase case, the ui-block hash case, the Ui module's focus case and the Python click test A and B; the Python `ui.createWidget` test A and D, for `ui.tree`'s `widget`) is un-skipped by the last of them or by the integration (amended at W22.2's integration, which un-skipped them all).
- **W22.2's integration** replaces every stub and skip of C22.2, registers what decision 20 left to the streams, regenerates the references and FeatureTest, redoes the recorded upgrade of the audited projects for the Ui section, and runs the feature, export, automation, determinism and games suites (the ui block keeps every committed replay's hash; the Ui replay verifies in the four runs).
- **The milestone end** is `CI.py --require-clang-cl` (ADR 0028 decision 12; M23's gate hardens phase 2).

### 40. What C22.3 freezes, and what it implements

C22.3 is W22.3's contract: R-UI-TEXTINPUT, R-UI-L10N's fallback fonts and CJK, R-UI-L10N-RTL and R-UI-WORLD (plan §3.5 "M22", W22.3's row; ADR 0028's phase-2 ledger), with the injections W22.2's review owed (decision 52) and the lead's notes for it (lessons from C22.2's and C21.2's reviews). The facts it relies on, checked at the base (`5a5da45b`, W22.2 integrated on `fd06249d`, main's `48d68e51` merged):

1. **Committed characters already reach the play input.** The window queues a `CharEvent` per codepoint from GLFW's character callback (`Platform/Window.cpp`); `PlayInput::QueueDeviceEvent` converts it into a `Text` event (`PlayInput.h`'s `PlayInputEventType::Text`), which replays, `input.inject` and `play.step` already carry; `ApplyTick` injects it into `InputState`, which ignores it. Nothing reads text yet, so no replay format changes (the ledger).
2. **The measurement layer has room.** `MeasureText` takes a font chain and a `TextDirection` and names a glyph's cluster by its first byte (`Renderer/TextMeasurement.h`, decision 6); `FontData` is format 1 and `FontImporter` version 4 (the ledger reserves FontData 2 and FontImporter 4 and up).
3. **The canvas left its render mode to C22.3** (decision 2), and `UiCanvasComponent`'s comment says that a World canvas takes the entity's Transform.
4. **The pipelines.** The QuadBatcher draws through Hdr (SceneColor, depth-tested) or Ldr (the scene overlay, no depth test) (`Renderer/QuadBatcher.h`; M21's branch appends HdrLit and Normals); world Text is drawn in the SceneOverlay stage, depth-tested against SceneDepth without depth writes (`Renderer/TextRenderer.h`). M22 owns the TextRenderer and the Ui slot; the QuadBatcher is M21's in phase 2 for a change, and a change M22 needs takes a ledger amendment.
5. **A deterministic camera ray exists.** `Camera:ScreenToWorldRay` builds a ray with `DetMath::Tan` (`Scripting/Bindings/Components.cpp`), at the render pose.
6. **`Test.Inject*`** queues an action, a key, a mouse button (with an optional position) or a gamepad control for the next unapplied tick through the test host's input service (`Scripting/Bindings/Test.cpp`); none sends a pointer move, the wheel or text (decision 36, W22.2's review's decision 5).

What the contract does:

- **Vendored by the contract, self-contained** (decision 41, as C23.1 vendored CMP_Core): HarfBuzz 15.0.0 and SheenBidi v2.9.0 with their `VENDOR.md`, licences, premake projects, `UseHarfBuzz()` and `UseSheenBidi()`, the `CheckBuildConfig.py` families, private defines and precise-model entries with the fixtures `FastMathHarfBuzz` and `FastMathSheenBidi`, `Lint.py`'s ABI-macro prefixes, the `ModuleRules.json` entries and the notices rows. Every executable links them; no first-party file includes them yet, so they change no behaviour.
- **Registered by the contract:** `UiTextInputComponent` (`UiTextInput`), appended to the Ui category after UiScrollView, excluding the four widgets and excluded by each (declared by both sides, decision 45); `PlaySessionPhase::TextLatch` between ApplyInput and UiInput with its name (decision 46); `UiWidgetType::TextInput` and `UiEventKind::TextChanged` and `Submit` with their names and callback names (decision 47; the implemented name functions extended, and the two switches over them that clang's `-Wswitch` would report gain their cases: `UiSystem.cpp`'s press and drag steps leave a text input alone, and `ScriptInstances.cpp`'s dispatch pushes no third argument for the two events until stream T); and the regenerated references.
- **Frozen in the headers and implemented** (inert unless used): `Scene/Components/UiTextInputComponent.h`; `IScriptHost::GetText` (`Scripting/ScriptHost.h`, empty by default; added by the review of C22.3, decision 46); `UiTextKeys`, `UiPointerRay`, `UiCanvasPointer`, `UiPointerHit` and `UiInputFrame`'s members `Text`, `TextKeys`, `GamepadNavigation` and `Ray` (`Scene/UiInteraction.h`); `UiCanvasRenderMode` and UiCanvas's members `RenderMode`, `WorldWidth`, `WorldHeight` and `PixelsPerUnit` (unregistered, below); `UiCanvasLayout::RenderMode`, `SceneSize` and `World`; `UiDrawList::WorldCanvases`, `UiWorldCanvas`, `UiScreenCanvas` and `UiDrawItem::WorldCanvas`, `HasCaret` and `CaretByte` (the list's memory-resource constructor gives `WorldCanvases` its resource); `AppendUiDrawItems`'s `worldOnly` parameter (unread until stream W); `UiPassQuad::WorldCanvas`, `UiPassRun::WorldCanvas`, `UiPassInputs::HasCamera`, `View` and `ViewProjection`, `MaxUiFontChainLength`; `TextGlyphRun::World`; `TextMeasuredCluster`, `TextMeasurement::Clusters` and `LineHeight`, `TextCaret`; `FontGlyph::GlyphIndex`, `FontData::UnitsPerEm`, `IndexedGlyphs`, `ShapingSource` and `Fallbacks`, `MaxFontFallbacks`; `FontImportSettings::Fallbacks` (unregistered, below) and `FontBakeReport::ShapingGlyphs`; `UiTreeEntry::WorldCanvas` (unregistered, below); and the frozen comments of `UiLayout.h`, `UiSystem.h`, `UiWidgets.h`, `UiExtraction.h`, `UiPass.h`, `TextMeasurement.h`, `FontData.h`, `FontImporter.h`, `PlayInput.h` and `PlaySession.h`, and, from the review of C22.3, of `RenderExtraction.h`, `RenderSnapshot.h`'s `Ui`, `UiInteraction.h`'s `ComputeUiPointerRay` and `EditorCore/Ui/UiAuthoring.h`'s `PickUiElement` (decisions 50 and 51).
- **Frozen here and registered by their streams**, with a stated reason each (plan §6: a contract changes a default only with one; C22.2's decision 20 froze its registry entries the same way):
  - UiCanvas's four fields and `UiCanvasRenderMode` (stream W): registering them changes the canonical form of FeatureTest's UI scenes (`Components/Ui.scene`, `Components/UiWidgets.scene`, `GoldenUi/Ui.scene`, `GoldenUiWidgets/UiWidgets.scene`), which only `Scaffold.py` regenerates, at the wave's integration (the lead's note 3).
  - `FontImportSettings::Fallbacks` (stream F): registering it changes every font's `.meta` canonical form, FeatureTest's two font metas among them, which `Scaffold.py` regenerates; and the field is read only with FontImporter version 5.
  - `ui.tree`'s `worldCanvas` (stream W): a registered result member with nothing to fill it; the outline's forms with it.
  - The script surface (decisions 46, 47 and 52): `Input.GetText`, `OnUiTextChanged` and `OnUiSubmit`, and `Test.InjectMouseMove`, `Test.InjectScroll` and `Test.InjectText`: registered with stubs, they would turn the scripting coverage case red ("ScriptApi: every registered function ... has a test", decision 20's fact 6), so their streams register them with their coverage scenarios, as C22.2 left the Ui module to stream B.
  - `UiWidgetKind::TextInput` and its template (stream T): `ui.createWidget`'s handler is implemented, so a registered kind without its template would fail at the call, and the Python test that builds every kind would fail.
- **C++ stubs:** every new function's first statement is `ENGINE_CONTRACT_STUB();`, returning an empty or neutral value (none returns `Result` or `Status`). 11 stubs: `ReadUiTextInputCaret`, `GetUiTextInputCaretByte`, `EditUiTextInput`, `PlayInput::LatchStepText`, `PlayInput::GetText` (T); `ComputeUiPointerRay`, `MapUiPointerToCanvas`, `HitTestUiPointer` (W); `FindTextCaret`, `FindGlyphByIndex` (S); `BuildUiFontChain` (F). Nothing calls a stub outside its skipped tests.
- **Acceptance tests:** final names, marked `doctest::skip(true)` (C++) or `unittest.skip` with a "contract stub" reason (Python): 33 C++ cases (two of them GPU cases; five of them added by the review of C22.3) and 2 Python tests, listed under "Acceptance (W22.3)". Each CPU case was forced with `--no-skip` against the contract tree and fails (a stub's empty result, the unregistered surface, a fixture font stream F adds, or the phases and rules that do not run yet); the two GPU cases render through a Ui pass that draws no World canvas yet, so their pixel checks fail by construction.
- **Live tests changed or added** because they pass against the contract tree: "BuiltinComponents: every listed type is plain component data" (35 types), "... UI defaults follow the component table" (the text input's defaults and UiCanvas's new members'), "... every exclusion is declared by both components" (24 exclusions: the five widgets' 20 ordered pairs), "... registry names are those of the component table" (`UiTextInput` after `UiScrollView`); "UiInteraction: widget kinds and UI events have their names and callbacks" (TextInput, TextChanged, Submit); "PlaySessionPhase: every phase has its enumerator name" (TextLatch); "UiDrawList: a list over a memory resource allocates its items and annotations from it" (`WorldCanvases`); the vendored-library sets of `Tests/Automation/test_vendor_cache.py` (HarfBuzz, SheenBidi); and the new guard "PlaySession: a scene with Screen UI and no text input or World canvas keeps its state hash while text is typed" (decision 54). The registry suite and the script proxies' coverage cover UiTextInput with no further code but one change: "TypeRegistry: every component field round-trips through automation set and get" required that most of 20 seeded values of each component reach it, and skipped a drawn value with a null reference (which an RFC 7386 patch reads as a removal); UiTextInput's two EntityRefs, each null a quarter of the time, left exactly 10 of 20 (the threshold needs 11), as two-reference components (UiCanvas, UiSlider) passed only by their seeds. The case now draws again past such a value, at most four draws per value, and requires every component to write all 20 values (`RoundTripDrawsPerValue`): no component writes fewer than before. From the review of C22.3: "FontImporter: version 3 rasterizes through the vendored stb_truetype.h with the glyph fields of version 2" (renamed from "... with the atlases of version 2": it compares the codepoint glyphs' distance fields wherever they are packed, since version 5 changes Inter's atlas, decision 44) and the new "UiTextInput: a Text of the longest MaxLength fits a script's write, and Caret is registered for any Text" (decision 45). Every existing test is kept.
- **No behaviour changes.** No session runs TextLatch or casts a pointer ray, no scene holds a text input or a World canvas, the new members are not serialized, and the vendored libraries are linked but not called. The FeatureTest coverage gates now name UiTextInput uncovered: the feature, automation and export suites are not part of a contract checkpoint, and W22.3's integration covers it (decision 55).

### 41. HarfBuzz and SheenBidi (R-UI-L10N-RTL; vendored by the contract, used by streams S and F)

- **The pins** (approved in `0001-approvals.md`; official upstream; each kept file byte-identical to its archive's, the tree digests in the `VENDOR.md` files):

  | Library | Version | Commit | Archive | SHA-256 | Bytes | Licence |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | HarfBuzz | `15.0.0` (2026-10-22) | `35f656a1a2bab548ea7f54a6dd3fba2b09680d14` | `https://github.com/harfbuzz/harfbuzz/releases/download/15.0.0/harfbuzz-15.0.0.tar.xz` (the release asset; its SHA-256 is the digest GitHub publishes for it) | `968536e9abd782f07f7954044c0c4020199fcdd39b41d221ec42d2a3be4a44df` | 20,396,372 | MIT-Modern-Variant ("Old MIT"), `COPYING`; MIT (Microsoft's, for the Universal Shaping Engine data upstream generated the compiled `src/hb-ot-shaper-use-table.hh` from), `src/ms-use/COPYING` (amended at W22.3's review) |
  | SheenBidi | `v2.9.0` (2025-06-10) | `83f77108a2873600283f6da4b326a2dca7a3a7a6` | `https://github.com/Tehreer/SheenBidi/archive/refs/tags/v2.9.0.tar.gz` (the tag archive; upstream publishes no asset) | `e90ae142c6fc8b94366f3526f84b349a2c10137f87093db402fe51f6eace6d13` | 2,532,288 | Apache-2.0, `LICENSE` (no `NOTICE` upstream) |

- **Why these versions.** HarfBuzz 15.0.0 is the latest release, with the latest fixes for malformed fonts (fonts are external input, fuzzed through the FontData and FontImporter cases); 14.6.0 differs in API the engine does not call. SheenBidi v2.9.0 rather than the newer v3.0.0 (2026-01-24), reconsidered by the review of C22.3 under the rule below: v3.0.0's process-level state is of the kind the rule allows, so it does not decide the version; v3.0.0's release notes list a text-editing and analysis API, a public allocator abstraction and the concurrency primitives they need, a refinement of its run and bracket queues, performance work and Unicode 17.0 data, and no correction of the bidirectional algorithm or the script locator, the two parts the engine calls (UAX #9 and UAX #24, which both versions implement); its editing API is code the engine would carry and never call (75 source files against v2.9.0's 47); and its build depends on detecting the compiler's C11 threads, thread-local storage and atomics (an `#error` without them unless `SB_CONFIG_DISABLE_SCRATCH_MEMORY`, an `SB_CONFIG_` define the engine keeps out), branches its GCC, Clang and Apple Clang builds would add unverified (ADR 0028 decision 10). Its Unicode 17.0 data differs from v2.9.0's only in characters added since, which no font the engine bakes for its tests draws. `Vendor/SheenBidi/VENDOR.md` says so.
- **Process-level state, one rule for every vendored library** (the review of C22.3, the lead's decision 5): a vendored library may keep internal process-level state that the engine never initializes, sets or tears down when it is thread-safe and no result depends on it; Architecture §3 rule 5 now states the rule beside its list of the state `ProcessContext` initializes. HarfBuzz 15.0.0 meets it, as found in its sources: write-once statics created by thread-safe lazy initialization (the Unicode and OpenType function tables, `hb-ucd.cc` and `hb-ot-font.cc`; the shaper list, `hb-shaper.cc`; the face-loader and font-functions lists, `hb-face.cc` and `hb-font.cc`; the interned language list, `hb-common.cc`, whose entries never change once added); the atomic hint indices of its language-tag lookups (`hb-ot-tag.cc`, checked before every use); and `_hb_CrapPool`, the write sink for writes to its null objects on error paths, which nothing reads for a result. `HB_NO_GETENV` and `HB_NO_SETLOCALE` keep the environment and the C locale out of it, and without upstream's `config.h` (`HAVE_ATEXIT`) it registers no `atexit` handler; its atomic reference counts, those of the shared function tables included, decide no result, and no state is thread-local. SheenBidi v3.0.0's state, read in its tag archive's sources, is of the same kind: one-time initializations, atomic per-object reference counts, a process-wide pool of three 8 KiB scratch buffers that a thread holds through a thread-local slot while it builds a paragraph or a line and returns at its end (the heap when the pool is empty), whose contents never outlive the call, and a default allocator that only `SBAllocatorSetDefault` changes, which the engine would never call. So the rule rejects neither, and v2.9.0 stays on the grounds above, not on its state; v2.9.0 keeps none (its objects are created and released by each call).
- **What is kept:** HarfBuzz's `COPYING`, the 365 files of `src/` that its amalgamated `src/harfbuzz-subset.cc` and the public `hb.h`, `hb-ot.h` and `hb-subset.h` include (the two string tables that `hb-string-array.hh` includes through a macro among them) and `src/ms-use/COPYING` (1,141 bytes), the licence of the data the compiled `hb-ot-shaper-use-table.hh` derives from, which the contract had removed with the rest of `src/ms-use/` and stream S restored from the pinned archive at W22.3 (the review of C22.3's open item): 367 files, 5,588,673 bytes, the tree digest in `Vendor/HarfBuzz/VENDOR.md`, the notices row naming both licence files (`MIT-Modern-Variant AND MIT`); SheenBidi's `LICENSE`, `Headers/` and `Source/`. *Amended at W22.3's review (the lead's decision).*
- **The builds:** HarfBuzz one C++17 translation unit, `harfbuzz-subset.cc` (the OpenType shaper with its own Unicode data, `hb-ucd`, the face, font and buffer objects and the subsetter; no platform backend), with the private defines `HB_NO_GETENV` and `HB_NO_SETLOCALE` (no environment variable or C locale changes shaping), and upstream's own MSVC options (`/bigobj`, `/utf-8`, `_CRT_SECURE_NO_WARNINGS`, `_CRT_NONSTDC_NO_WARNINGS`, and C4244 and C4267 disabled for the vendored project, as upstream's CMake build does; consumers keep every warning); SheenBidi every `Source/*.c` but the unity file, without defines. Both precise floating point without contraction (`PRECISE_VENDOR_PROJECTS`, the lead's note 4), checked by `CheckBuildConfig.py` and its fixtures `FastMathHarfBuzz` and `FastMathSheenBidi`; the `HB_` and `SB_CONFIG_` families stay empty in every project (`VENDOR_ABI_FAMILIES`, `Lint.py`'s `banned-abi-macro`), HarfBuzz's two defines listed in `VENDOR_PRIVATE_DEFINES`. MSVC builds them without warnings in Debug, Release and Dist (Dist recorded by stream S at W22.3; amended at W22.3's review); clang-cl builds them in Release and Dist for the portability stage, HarfBuzz without warnings and SheenBidi with one tolerated vendored warning in Dist (an unused variable only an assertion reads, recorded in its `VENDOR.md`; decision 58's runs). Their GCC, Clang and Apple Clang builds are never compiled (ADR 0028 decision 10).
- **Consumers:** `UseHarfBuzz()` and `UseSheenBidi()` in Engine, EditorCore, Editor, Tests and `UseRuntimeDependencies()` (the Runtime's UI text is measured with them); `ModuleRules.json` gives both to Renderer privately (the measurement layer) and HarfBuzz to AssetPipeline privately (the importer's shaping glyphs and subset), so no public header exposes their types; the notices rows ship with every export.

### 42. Fallback fonts and CJK (R-UI-L10N; stream F)

- **The chain is per font.** `FontImportSettings::Fallbacks` (an array of Font asset references, at most `MaxFontFallbacks`, 8; none null, none the font itself; located Validation errors under `/Fallbacks`) names the fonts a text in that font falls back to, cooked into `FontData::Fallbacks` and recorded as the import's dependencies (M19's pruning rule, the ledger), so a pruned export keeps them. `BuildUiFontChain` (`Renderer/UiPass.h`) gives a text's chain: its font (the canvas's or the default font, decision 25), then its fallbacks depth first in order, each font once, at most `MaxUiFontChainLength` (8) fonts; a fallback that is missing, of another type or fails to load is left out and reported once per manager, as a missing sheet is (decision 7). `MeasureText` already takes a chain (decision 6): a codepoint takes the first font that has it, and the Ui pass splits glyph runs where the font changes.
- **Why per font, not per canvas or project:** fonts are what users pair (Unity's and Godot's fallbacks are per font), the import's dependencies carry them through export pruning without an exporter change (`Exporter.cpp` has no phase-2 owner), and a canvas or project list would need its own pruning rule.
- **Not built in.** The reserved handles 0x01c2 to 0x01c4 stay free: a useful CJK fallback is a font of several megabytes whose atlas would cover tens of thousands of ideographs in every export's `Engine.pak`, while a game bakes only the ranges its texts use. A game that draws CJK, Arabic or Hebrew imports the fonts it needs with `Ranges` and names them in its UI font's `Fallbacks`; the built-in default font has no fallbacks (an accepted limit, Backlog §6). The Text component keeps its frozen `TextLayout` (ADR 0013 decision 11): no chain, no shaping (Backlog §6).
- **The fixture fonts** (approved in `0001-approvals.md`'s Noto row; SIL OFL 1.1; the smallest official files that cover M22's test texts), pinned here for stream F, which adds them to `Tests/Data/Assets/Fonts/` (and stream E their byte-identical copies to FeatureTest's `Scaffold/Sources/Fonts/`, with the golden scaffold that imports them, 2016's, since GoldenScene's live case fails on a source no scaffold imports; amended at W22.3's integration) with their `FIXTURE_FILES` pins in `Scripts/FetchAssets.py` (a new pinned kind for a GitHub release archive member and a raw tagged file), their rows and licence texts in `Tests/Data/LICENSES.md` and `*.otf` and `*.ttf` as binary in `.gitattributes` if not already:

  | Font | Source | Archive or file SHA-256, bytes | Member | Member SHA-256, bytes |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | Noto Sans Arabic Regular | release `NotoSansArabic-v2.013` of `notofonts/arabic` (2025-10-15): `https://github.com/notofonts/arabic/releases/download/NotoSansArabic-v2.013/NotoSansArabic-v2.013.zip` (its SHA-256 is the digest GitHub publishes) | `1301aceaea84c501cf2e6dcfb3182e2328c8eae5725817fcb239672bda7154f1`, 18,777,381 | `NotoSansArabic/unhinted/ttf/NotoSansArabic-Regular.ttf`; licence `OFL.txt` (`a7a5a25eb188bf1cd96982030d53e23c33485c69b1044a562254226857ee13af`, 4,382) | `bd86ca02f087d7f3c3788ba458fb6b73744c7639ed276b8d870dba6def6c40d0`, 142,140 |
  | Noto Sans Hebrew Regular | release `NotoSansHebrew-v3.001` of `notofonts/hebrew` (2024-06-03): `https://github.com/notofonts/hebrew/releases/download/NotoSansHebrew-v3.001/NotoSansHebrew-v3.001.zip` | `df0a71814b4e63644cf40fcc4529111b61266b7a2dafbe95068b29a7520cc3cb`, 3,734,185 | `NotoSansHebrew/unhinted/ttf/NotoSansHebrew-Regular.ttf`; licence `OFL.txt` (`9b9fe028b5ba74d231659a1bbaf0ed09b11e759d1ca6a070999e16d151616b47`, 4,382) | `04272f5600d0ec816d31d0df73b23aa8d3501ea359ebe820da31c11ffcf00853`, 16,836 |
  | Noto Sans CJK JP Regular (the Japanese region subset, the smallest of the five) | tag `Sans2.004` of `notofonts/noto-cjk` (commit `523d033d6cb47f4a80c58a35753646f5c3608a78`): `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/notofonts/noto-cjk/Sans2.004/Sans/SubsetOTF/JP/NotoSansJP-Regular.otf` (its git blob `4af556abb9cc42e585aab777b01857246a110746` matches the repository's) | `dff723ba59d57d136764a04b9b2d03205544f7cd785a711442d6d2d085ac5073`, 4,533,028 | the file itself; licence `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/notofonts/noto-cjk/Sans2.004/LICENSE` (`6a73f9541c2de74158c0e7cf6b0a58ef774f5a780bf191f2d7ec9cc53efe2bf2`, 4,301) | as the file |

- **CJK coverage** is `Ranges` (decision 6) plus the chain: a test text's ideographs and kana are baked by ranges of their codepoints (at most 64 ranges, 65,536 codepoints), so the fixture's atlas stays small.

### 43. Shaping and bidi in the measurement layer (R-UI-L10N-RTL; stream S)

`Renderer/TextMeasurement.h`'s frozen comment is the rule: SheenBidi gives each paragraph's embedding levels from `TextDirection` (Auto: the first strong character's; left to right without one); runs of one level, one script and one font are shaped with HarfBuzz when the font carries shaping data (a face and font from `FontData::ShapingSource`, scale `UnitsPerEm`, so positions are integer font units; the language set explicitly to "und", never the process locale; the shaper's default features; monotone character clusters) and laid out codepoint by codepoint with the kerning pairs when it does not; lines break in logical order at the spaces between clusters, then each line is reordered visually (UAX #9 L1 and L2) and aligned. A glyph's atlas rectangle is `FindGlyphByIndex` of its font; `TextMeasurement::Clusters` lists every cluster with its line and extent in visual order, and `FindTextCaret` places a caret at a cluster's leading edge (decision 48). Positions convert to pixels once (`value / UnitsPerEm * Size`, IEEE-exact), so shaped text is byte-identical across configurations (Roadmap M22's acceptance), checked in the Debug and Release unit runs by "TextMeasurement: shaped Latin, Arabic, Hebrew, mixed-direction and fallback CJK text measures to the same bytes in every configuration" (a pinned digest of every glyph's id, position and atlas rectangle, every cluster and every line, for each text in both base directions; added by the review of C22.3, its major; stream S records the digest), by the other cases' exact values and by the goldens. Left-to-right text in fonts without shaping data measures exactly as W22.1's (W22.1's cases keep passing unchanged; their comment on directions changes with the stream). Every font FontImporter 5 bakes carries shaping data, the built-in default font included, so Inter's text is shaped too (the lead's decision 2 of the review of C22.3): its default features and its GPOS kerning in font units replace W22.1's codepoint layout with `FontKerningPair`, and UI text in the default font may measure differently from W22.1's (decision 44 says what that moves). The default features include `rand`, which draws from the HarfBuzz buffer's own random state (1 in every new buffer), so shaping stays a pure function. HarfBuzz and SheenBidi stay private to `Renderer/Private/` and `TextMeasurement.cpp` (no public header exposes their types); a face per font and call is created from the cooked bytes (pure and thread-safe, as `MeasureText` is), and caching shaping plans is the stream's choice if it keeps that.

### 44. FontData format 2 and FontImporter version 5 (streams S and F)

- **FontData 2** (`Asset/FontData.h`, the ledger's reserved version): each `FontGlyph` gains `GlyphIndex`; `UnitsPerEm`; `IndexedGlyphs`, every baked glyph once by glyph index (the codepoint glyphs with their smallest codepoint, and the glyphs only shaping reaches with Codepoint 0; `FindGlyphByIndex` binary-searches it); `ShapingSource`, the font subset to its baked glyphs by HarfBuzz's subsetter with glyph ids retained and its layout and metrics tables kept; `Fallbacks` (decision 42). Format 1 payloads still load (no shaping data, no fallbacks). The payload layout is stream S's, documented byte for byte in the header as format 1's is, with `ValidateFontData`'s new checks (IndexedGlyphs sorted and unique, every codepoint glyph present in it with equal metrics, at most `MaxFontFallbacks` fallbacks, a source of at most the import's size limit) and the FontData fuzz case extended to it (the ledger: M22's FontData 2 extends the FontData case).
- **FontImporter 5** (`AssetPipeline/Importers/FontImporter.h`'s frozen comment): adds the glyphs shaping can produce from the baked codepoints with the shaper's default features (`hb_ot_shape_glyphs_closure`, one buffer per script, no user features), packed after the codepoint glyphs in the same packer, so a font whose shaping adds no glyph keeps version 4's atlas byte for byte, one whose added glyphs fit keeps every codepoint glyph in place, and every codepoint glyph keeps its distance field; records the glyph indices and units per em; embeds the subset; cooks the fallbacks and records them as dependencies (F). Every cached font is rebuilt once. The FontImporter fuzz case covers version 5 (the ledger).
- **Inter's atlas changes deliberately** (the lead's decision 2 of the review of C22.3, its major): shaping uses HarfBuzz's default features (standard behaviour, which Arabic and Hebrew need), and the atlas holds every glyph the font's ranges reach through them, so Inter, the built-in default font, gains glyphs no codepoint maps to (`calt`'s contextual alternates and the `frac`, `numr` and `dnom` forms among them; about 80 by the review's estimate). The version 5 case checks that Inter gains shaping glyphs, keeps its codepoint glyphs' fields and bakes the atlas whose hash stream S records there (`InterTrueTypeShapingAtlasHash`); "FontImporter: version 3 rasterizes through the vendored stb_truetype.h with the glyph fields of version 2" checks the fields wherever they are packed. Text measurements and the Text, Ui and UiWidgets goldens may move (the Text golden's TextComponent, laid out by the frozen unshaped `TextLayout`, through the atlas; UI text in the default font also through shaping, decision 43): W22.3's integration regenerates exactly the images that move, each reviewed and explained in this record (the ledger's reviewed regeneration of an existing golden). **No replay hash can change, and none may:** UI layout is not hashed (decision 3), text measurement is not on the simulation path (it is Renderer's; Scene's layout reads no font, decision 6), the Text component's layout is unshaped, and `Ui.replay`'s pointer hits depend on element rectangles, which never follow their content (decision 6); so every committed replay verifies at its hash at the integration, `Ui.replay` at `94b2457e1ed7567b` among them, and a moved hash is a defect to fix, not a re-record. *Amended at W22.3's integration (the lead's decision):* shaping and the version 5 atlas move no hash, as above, but a registration that adds serialized fields to a hashed component moves the hash of every scene holding that component, because the state hash covers the runtime scene's serialized form (`PlaySession::ComputeStateHash`); stream W's registration of UiCanvas's `RenderMode`, `WorldWidth`, `WorldHeight` and `PixelsPerUnit`, which the scene serializer writes with their defaults, moves `Ui.replay`'s hash from `94b2457e1ed7567b` to `14057c3734e9a42b` at tick 80 (checkpoint 60: `f680de2d377bccb1`, was `28e5309edc944ed4`), its events, expectations and behaviour unchanged. Such a replay is re-recorded by `Scaffold.py` from the same input, with the reason recorded, as M21 did for `Sprites2D.replay`'s NormalMap field; scenes without the component, the demos among them, keep their hashes.
- **One bump each for the wave:** FormatVersion 2 and Version 5 are set by stream S, and F's fields join them; the integration merges the two streams' edits of `FontData.cpp` and `FontImporter.cpp` by function.

### 45. The text input component (R-UI-TEXTINPUT; registered by the contract, stream T)

`UiTextInput`, version 1, a fifth widget appended after UiScrollView, requiring UiRect and excluding the other four (each declares it): `Interactable`, `Focusable` (the widgets' descriptions), `Text` (string), `MaxLength` (UInt32, 1 to 1,024, `MaxUiTextInputLength`, default 256: a codepoint takes at most four UTF-8 bytes, so the longest typed Text fits the 4,096 bytes a script's write of a component holds, `MaxScriptComponentStringBytes`; the review of C22.3, which lowered it from 16,384), `Caret` (UInt32, at least 0 and without an upper bound, read clamped to Text's codepoint count: a Text a scene, a script or automation writes may be longer than any MaxLength, and is kept), `TextElement` and `Placeholder` (EntityRefs to elements below it). `UiTextInputComponent.h` states the behaviour. Why these fields: the text is the component's own (what scripts read and write, `entity:GetComponent("UiTextInput").Text`), while a child UiText draws it with that UiText's font, size, colour and alignment (no duplicated text styling), as a slider's parts are elements; the caret is simulation state in the scene (as ScrollPosition is), so the ui hash block keeps its layout. The demos hold no UI, so nothing changes a hash (decision 54).

### 46. The text latch (R-UI-TEXTINPUT; stream T)

- **The phase:** `PlaySessionPhase::TextLatch` between ApplyInput and UiInput (the ledger's position), in Play and Simulate: `PlayInput::LatchStepText` makes the tick's committed characters (its applied `Text` events, in order) the step view's text, so the tick's UiInput and scripts read the same characters, none on a tick without text. `LatchFrame` makes the frame's text every step text latched since the previous frame (none in a frame without a step; text is never previewed). `PlayInput.h` states it. No replay format change (the ledger): replays, `input.inject`, `play.step` and the window already carry `Text` events (fact 1).
- **The script read** (registered by stream T with its coverage): `Input.GetText() -> string`, "Returns the characters typed in the latched view as UTF-8: in the fixed step (OnFixedUpdate, tasks, physics and UI callbacks) the tick's committed characters, in the frame phase (OnUpdate, OnLateUpdate) those of every tick since the previous frame; empty when none were typed." Read-only, every mode, Runtime and Test environments. It reads `IScriptHost::GetText(phase)` (`Scripting/ScriptHost.h`; frozen by the review of C22.3, its major, with an implemented default, empty, as C22.2 froze `GetUi`), which the play session's host overrides with `PlayInput::GetText` (stream T); every other host, the edit evaluation and test fixtures without a session among them, reads no text, so `EditorMethodContext.cpp` keeps its one granted line (the lead's decision 1 of the review).
- **Committed characters only:** no IME composition display (the plan's kept limit), no selection, no clipboard (Backlog §6).

### 47. The text input's editing, events and callbacks (stream T)

- **Editing** (`Scene/UiTextInput.h`, `EditUiTextInput`): typed characters without control characters and ill-formed bytes insert at the caret up to MaxLength codepoints; then Backspace, Delete, Left, Right, Home and End, each once, in that order; Text and Caret written; TextChanged when Text changed, then Submit for Enter. Codepoints, not grapheme clusters (Backlog §6). The step view carries no order between a step's characters and keys, so that order is the rule; an editing key acts on its press edge (no repeat while held, as the other UI commands, Backlog §6).
- **In UiInput** (`UiSystem.h`'s C22.3 paragraph): step 7a after the stick: the focused interactable text input takes `UiInputFrame::Text` and `TextKeys`; while it has focus, the step's navigation commands are the gamepad's (`GamepadNavigation`) plus Next, Previous and Cancel, so a key typed into the field (an arrow, Space, Enter) never navigates or activates; step 9's Submit at a focused text input raises Submit (a gamepad's South submits); a click only focuses it (`ActivateUiWidget` leaves it alone). The session fills `TextKeys` from fixed keys (Key.Backspace, Delete, Left, Right, Home, End, Enter and KeypadEnter pressed in the step view) and `GamepadNavigation` from the Ui settings' gamepad bindings, never new settings, so the Ui section is unchanged. *Amended by the milestone review* (the lead's decision 7): a key the input edits with no longer also fires a command bound to it. While a text input has focus, Next, Previous and Cancel navigate only when a control other than its editing keys (Backspace, Delete, Left, Right, Home and End; Enter keeps Submit's documented meaning) pressed them: the session fills `UiInputFrame::NonEditingNavigation` (a frozen-header addition at the milestone review, beside `GamepadNavigation`) with the commands a gamepad button or another key pressed, and step 7a keeps its Next, Previous and Cancel. So a game that binds Backspace to Cancel ("back", FeatureTest's and `AllSettings.eproj`'s setting) only deletes in a field, an arrow or Home bound to Next only moves the caret, and Escape, Tab and the gamepad still navigate ("PlaySession: a key a focused text input edits with fires no Cancel, Next or Previous bound to it").
- **A gamepad does not type** (amended by W22.3's review, the lead's decision: an accepted limit, not new scope): a gamepad focuses a text input (its directions, Next or the default focus), submits it (South) and leaves it (its directions, Next, Previous, Cancel), but enters no text and presses no editing key: characters come only from a text source (the keyboard's committed characters, decision 46; an IME's committed text would come the same way), the editing keys are keyboard keys, and a gamepad on-screen keyboard is out of M22's scope (Backlog §6). This departs from Roadmap M22's acceptance "every widget works by keyboard and gamepad" for the text input's typing alone, with that reason: a text field needs a text source, which a gamepad is not, and an on-screen keyboard is a widget of its own. "PlaySession: a gamepad focuses, submits and leaves a text input, and types nothing into it" states what a gamepad does; Architecture §5.7's UiInput step and the skills (`luau-gameplay`, `skills/game-building/ui`) say it.
- **The events** (appended to `UiEventKind`): TextChanged and Submit, raised at the input, carrying no text, so the ui hash block keeps its layout and every committed replay its hash; the callbacks receive the input's Text as it is when they run.
- **The callbacks** (registered by stream T in `Bindings/Script.cpp` after `OnUiScroll`, their names appended to `ScriptData.cpp`'s reserved names, in landing order; all modes, Runtime and Test; added to `RunBehaviourCallbacksCoverage` in the same change):

  | Callback | Signature | Description |
  |---|---|---|
  | `OnUiTextChanged` | `(self: ScriptInstance, element: Entity, text: string) -> boolean?` | "Called when typing or an editing key changes a UI text input's Text, with its new text; bubbles like OnUiClick." |
  | `OnUiSubmit` | `(self: ScriptInstance, element: Entity, text: string) -> boolean?` | "Called when Enter (or Submit) is pressed while a UI text input has focus, with its text; bubbles like OnUiClick." |

### 48. Drawing the text input (streams T and S)

- **Layout** (`UiLayout.h`): a text input clips its descendants as ClipChildren does, whatever its UiRect says, and its Placeholder's subtree is Hidden while its Text is not empty.
- **Extraction** (`UiExtraction.h`): the TextElement's UiText draws the input's Text in place of its own; while the input has focus its item carries the caret (`HasCaret`, `CaretByte`), an empty Text included; an unfocused empty input draws no text item (its Placeholder shows).
- **The Ui pass** (`UiPass.h`): the caret is a quad of the text's colour, `max(1, floor(FontSize / 16 + 0.5))` scene pixels wide, at `FindTextCaret`'s x, spanning the line's `LineHeight`; a caret item's block is aligned vertically as at least one `LineHeight` high, so an empty field's caret stands where its first line will (the review of C22.3: an empty block has no height); a text that does not wrap moves by the least distance, left or right, that puts the caret's quad inside the rectangle's x range (its left edge at the rectangle's left when the quad is wider), so the caret stays visible in a text wider than its rectangle, whatever its alignment (the review of C22.3: the earlier rule could not bring back a caret left of the rectangle, as a right-aligned text puts it at its start). The caret does not blink (presentation would need a clock that captures and goldens do not have).
- **The caret's byte** (`TextMeasurement.h`, `FindTextCaret`): besides the cluster rules, a byte no cluster begins at, ends at or holds (an ill-formed byte the input keeps, decision 47, or a carriage return) takes the trailing edge of its line's cluster that ends last before it in logical order, or its line's start when none does (the review of C22.3).

### 49. World canvases: the component and the layout (R-UI-WORLD; stream W)

- **The fields** (UiCanvas, after DefaultFocus, registered by stream W with the registry enum `UiCanvasRenderMode {Screen, World}`): `RenderMode` (Screen), `WorldWidth` and `WorldHeight` (UInt32 canvas pixels, 1 to 16,384, defaults 1024 and 576; two fields because the registry has no unsigned vector), `PixelsPerUnit` (0.001 to 1,000,000, default 512). Registered descriptions: "Screen draws the canvas over the scene image; World draws it as a rectangle in the world at the entity's transform, depth-tested against the scene."; the enum "Where a UI canvas is drawn: over the scene image, or in the world.", its entries Screen "Over the scene image, after the scene and its texts, laid out in scene pixels." and World "As a rectangle in the world at the canvas entity's transform, depth-tested against the scene, laid out in its own canvas pixels." (frozen by the review of C22.3, its nit); "A World canvas's width in canvas pixels (its layout's scene resolution); Screen canvases ignore it."; likewise for the height; "A World canvas's canvas pixels per world unit: its rectangle is WorldWidth x WorldHeight over this, centred on the entity."
- **Placement:** the entity's local XY plane, centred on its origin, canvas x right, canvas y down mapped to local -Y, facing +Z like a world Text, transformed by its world matrix (scale included). No billboard (an accepted limit).
- **Layout** (`UiLayout.h`): a World canvas is laid out as a Screen canvas over a scene image of its own WorldWidth x WorldHeight, whatever the session's scene resolution; `UiCanvasLayout` records its mode, size and matrix (from the simulated pose); nesting starts canvases of their own as before. Pure and IEEE-exact, as decision 4 requires; the matrix multiplies the TransformSystem's world matrix, itself IEEE-exact.

### 50. World canvases: the pointer (stream W)

`UiInteraction.h`'s frozen comment and functions are the rule: the session casts a ray only while the current layout holds a World canvas (`UiInputFrame::Ray`, `ComputeUiPointerRay` from the primary camera's simulated pose with `DetMath::Tan`, the same projection as `Camera:ScreenToWorldRay` but through the pointer rather than a pixel's centre); `MapUiPointerToCanvas` meets the canvas's plane from either side; `HitTestUiPointer` lets a Screen canvas's target win, else the nearest World canvas with a target at its point (a World canvas takes the pointer only inside its rectangle, (0, 0) to its size, as its layout's Visible rule: stream W's rule, accepted at W22.3's integration); a slider or scroll view on a World canvas follows the pointer along its plane; directional navigation stays within one render space; Next, Previous and the default focus span every canvas. `HitTestUi` keeps W22.2's rule for the Screen canvases. Without a World canvas no ray is cast and nothing changes (decision 54). *Amended by W22.3's review (the lead's decision):* the ray carries the camera's clip range (`UiPointerRay::MinDistance` and `MaxDistance`, in ray parameters from its origin, as viewport.pick's ray interval does: a perspective camera's from NearClip / c, c the ray's cosine with the camera's -Z, without an end, since its projection's far plane is at infinity, §8.3, and FarClip culls meshes, not canvases; an orthographic camera's from its near plane, where the ray starts, to FarClip - NearClip), and `MapUiPointerToCanvas` maps no point outside it, so a World canvas the camera clips takes no pointer ("UiInteraction: a World canvas takes the pointer only within the camera's clip range"). Scene geometry does not occlude the pointer: UiInteraction makes no depth or physics query, so a World canvas behind a mesh or a collider still takes the pointer where the ray meets it, though the depth test hides it there (an accepted limit, Backlog §6); a game whose canvases can stand behind geometry casts the pointer's ray with `Physics.Raycast` before it acts. The ray is the inverse of the projection render extraction gives the primary camera's view, at the simulated pose (`ComputeUiPointerRay`'s comment, amended by the review of C22.3): on the merged tree, the later lander of M21 and M22 makes it follow M21's PixelPerfectCamera (orthographic with a half height of ReferenceHeight / (2 PixelsPerUnit), at its pixel-snapped position, whatever the Camera says), with a case (risk 11): done at M22's landing ("Landing" c). *Amended by the milestone review:* a pointer outside the scene image casts its ray in vain: UiInput hit-tests nothing there (decision 24's amendment), so a World canvas that reaches past the view's edge takes no press from a letterbox bar; a session-level case clicks a World canvas through a PixelPerfectCamera ("PlaySession: under a PixelPerfectCamera a click on a World canvas's button through the camera clicks it").

### 51. World canvases: extraction and the Ui pass (stream W)

- **Extraction:** every view that draws the world draws the World canvases (the game view, a camera's view and the editor's scene view, `worldOnly` for the Explicit source); Screen canvases stay in the game and camera views only (decision 7). A World canvas's items are in its pixels and name its `UiWorldCanvas`, whose matrix is at the render pose (interpolated, as meshes draw), while the session hit-tests at the simulated pose. *Amended by the milestone review* (the lead's decision 1): "as meshes draw" is now what the code does: `AppendUiDrawItems` takes the extraction's alpha (`RenderExtractionRequest::Alpha`, a parameter appended to its frozen signature, defaulting to 1) and places a World canvas at `ComputeRenderedWorldMatrix(entity, alpha)`, where stream W's choice used the scene's interpolation alpha, which is the frame's only inside the play session's RenderExtraction phase: between frames it is 1, so `PlaySession::ExtractView` of a live session (the editor's Game and Scene views during play, `viewport.screenshot` and `viewport.pick` of the play scene, the Runtime's test screenshots) drew World canvases up to a tick ahead of the meshes and the camera. The game view the session extracts in its own phase, every lockstep, paused or tick-driven capture, the goldens and the replays (alpha 1 everywhere) do not change ("RenderExtraction: a World canvas is placed at the request's interpolation alpha, as the meshes are").
- **The pass:** World canvases' runs first, canvas by canvas from the farthest to the nearest (view-space depth of their centres, ties in list order), clipped on the CPU in canvas pixels to their clip and to the canvas's rectangle, (0, 0) to its size (a scissor is screen-space; the rectangle is stream W's rule, accepted at W22.3's integration, so a World canvas draws only inside it), transformed by their matrix and the view-projection, depth-tested against SceneDepth without depth writes, in the SceneOverlay stage's Ui row (the ledger's slot; no new stage, declaration or stats row), untonemapped like world Text; then the Screen canvases' runs as before. Images through the QuadBatcher's depth-tested LDR target (`QuadBatchTarget::LdrDepth`, decision 57's ledger amendment, granted on main in `1707cf47`), glyphs through the TextRenderer's world pipeline (`TextGlyphRun::World`, M22's file). A view without a camera draws no World canvas. World Text is recorded before, in the Text row, also depth-tested without depth writes, so neither hides the other: a World canvas draws over world Text where they overlap, whichever is nearer (an accepted limit beside risk 16's, Backlog §6; the review of C22.3).
- **World canvases' pixels elsewhere** (the review of C22.3, its major, the lead's decision 3; stream W, each rule with a frozen case): a World canvas's elements are in its own canvas pixels, so every consumer of scene pixels takes a rule.
  - Capture annotations (`AppendUiRenderAnnotations`, `UiExtraction.h`): an element of a World canvas is outlined and labelled at the scene-pixel rectangle that bounds its four corners projected through its canvas's World matrix and the snapshot's camera, and not at all without a camera or when a corner lies on or behind the camera's plane ("RenderAnnotations: a World canvas's element is outlined and labelled where the camera projects it"). *Amended by the milestone review* (the lead's decision 3): "behind the camera's plane" is now decision 50's clip range by view-space depth, not clip-space w, which an orthographic projection (a PixelPerfectCamera's included) keeps at 1: a corner nearer than `NearClip`, or farther than `FarClip` for an orthographic camera, leaves the element unannotated, as the camera draws nothing there ("RenderAnnotations: a World canvas's element is annotated only within the camera's clip range").
  - The editor's UI preview (`PickUiElement`, `EditorCore/Ui/UiAuthoring.h`, and `Editor/Ui/UiAuthoringOverlay.cpp`): World canvases' elements are left out, neither outlined, picked nor given the rect tool's handles, because the rect tool maps scene-pixel drags to canvas units through the canvas's scale, which a projection through the camera does not have; a World canvas is placed by its entity's Transform and its elements are edited in the inspector or with `entity.update`, `ui.tree` reporting their rectangles ("UiAuthoring: the UI preview picks no element of a World canvas" and "SceneViewportPanel: the UI preview neither selects nor resizes an element of a World canvas"; Backlog §6).
  - `Ui.GetRect` (`Bindings/Ui.cpp`): reports an element of a World canvas in that canvas's pixels, and every element's canvas in a new `Canvas` member of `UiElementRect` (decision 56's text; "Ui: GetRect reports a World canvas's element in its canvas pixels, with its canvas").
- **The recreation protocol** (C18.3; the lead's note 3): the depth-tested quad pipeline is the QuadBatcher's (SceneRendererPipelines'), the glyph pipeline the TextRenderer's existing world pipeline, so the placement rule covers them; the loss test "UiPass: a device loss recreates the world canvases' pipeline and a World canvas draws the same image afterwards" runs through `Test::RunDeviceLossTest`. The startup pipeline count grows by the QuadBatcher's one pipeline, and its declaration's `PipelineCount` with it.

### 52. Recording suites that move the pointer, scroll and type (W22.2's review, decision 5; stream I)

Registered by stream I in `Bindings/Test.cpp` after `InjectGamepad`, Test environment, all modes, mutating, each queued like the other `Test.Inject*` events into the next unapplied input tick through the test host's input service (fact 6), validated before the host as `InjectMouse`'s position is:

| Function | Signature | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `InjectMouseMove` | `(position: vector) -> ()` | "Queue a mouse move to a finite XY position in view coordinates for the next unapplied input tick." |
| `InjectScroll` | `(delta: vector) -> ()` | "Queue a scroll of a finite XY delta (wheel steps, y up-positive) for the next unapplied input tick." |
| `InjectText` | `(text: string) -> ()` | "Queue committed text (non-empty UTF-8) for the next unapplied input tick, as typed characters." |

The names follow the family (`InjectMouse` takes the button and an optional position, and the replay events are `MouseMove`, `Scroll` and `Text`) rather than the review's working names. Their service forms are `{type: mouseMove, position}`, `{type: scroll, delta}` and `{type: text, text}`, `input.inject`'s. Coverage: a scenario in `RunInputScenario` for "ScriptApi: every registered function ...", their generated declarations, FeatureTest's recording suites in Editor, Release and Dist (decision 55), and the Python test "test_a_recording_suite_moving_the_pointer_scrolling_and_typing_records_what_input_record_records". When they land, the game-building skill's `replays` page drops its `input_record` workaround for drags and the wheel, and its `ui` page names them (decision 36).

### 53. `ui.tree`, `ui.createWidget` and validation (streams T and W)

- **`ui.tree`:** a text input's element reports `widget` "TextInput", its input's Text as `text` and the outline form `UiTextInput("<text>")` (T); `worldCanvas` (bool, after `widget`, registered by W: "True for a World canvas and its elements, whose rect and clip are in the canvas's own pixels.") with the canvas line `UiCanvas(sort <n>, scale <s>, world <w>x<h>)` (W). The method's flags are unchanged.
- **`ui.createWidget`:** `UiWidgetKind::TextInput`, appended (T): an element 240 x 32 with a dark UiImage `(0.15, 0.15, 0.15, 1)` and a UiTextInput whose TextElement is its child "Text" (UiText, white, Wrap off, VerticalAlignment Middle, stretched with a 6-unit inset) and whose Placeholder is its child "Placeholder" (UiText, grey `(0.6, 0.6, 0.6, 1)`, the same layout), `text` the placeholder's text (default "Enter text"); the Create UI menu lists it with the other kinds.
- **Validation:** `UI_WIDGET_PART_INVALID` covers a UiTextInput's TextElement and Placeholder (T: the code's registered description, both of `ProjectValidator.cpp`'s tables, its skill row and its fixture regenerated; the existing case gains the text input's parts, extended, not weakened).

### 54. Scenes without the new components keep their hash (the lead's note 3)

A scene without a UiTextInput and without a World canvas hashes exactly as before C22.3: text typed while no text input has focus reaches no component; the editing keys and Enter change nothing; no ray is cast without a World canvas; the TextChanged and Submit events exist only with a text input; the ui block's layout is unchanged. The live guard "PlaySession: a scene with Screen UI and no text input or World canvas keeps its state hash while text is typed" (three sessions: one types and presses every editing key, one turns its primary camera, one idles; the typing session's hash equals the idle one's at every tick, and both others' interaction states equal the idle one's) holds on the contract tree and guards streams T and W; every committed replay, the Ui replay at `94b2457e1ed7567b` among them, verifies at its hash in the determinism and games suites at the integration (amended at W22.3's integration: the Ui replay is re-recorded at `14057c3734e9a42b` because its scene holds a UiCanvas, whose registered World-canvas fields it now serializes, decision 44's amendment; every other committed replay keeps its hash).

*Amended by the milestone review* (the lead's decision 9; the gap W22.1's m11 closed for `Ranges`): `FontImportSettings.Fallbacks` had no FeatureTest coverage in any run mode, since only the golden scaffold's copy of Inter names fallbacks and golden scenes render in `Tests.exe` alone. `UiFallback.jsonl` (IdSeed 2092, M22's range after the combined test's 2090 and 2091, listed after `Combined2DUiReplay.jsonl` and before `Suites.jsonl`) imports Noto Sans Hebrew with the Hebrew block's `Ranges` and a copy of Inter whose `Fallbacks` names it into `Assets/Ui/FallbackFonts/`, and writes `Assets/Scenes/Components/UiFallback.scene`, a label mixing Latin and Hebrew in that copy, and the suite `UiFallback` (`Assets/Tests/UiFallback/UiFallback.test.luau`, bound through `Suites.jsonl`'s one patch line in Editor, Release and Dist), whose case reads the label's font and text and renders the scene through `Test.Screenshot`. No script loads the Hebrew font, so an export keeps it through Inter's import dependencies alone. A scene and suite of their own, so no committed replay's hash moves; the six coverage gates stay green in the three modes.

### 57. Formats, ledger entries, contended files, and the ledger amendment granted

- **Formats:** FontData 2 and FontImporter 5 (the ledger's reservations); UiTextInput version 1 (new); UiCanvas's fields land while it is unlanded (decision 2), so no version changes; no replay, pak, manifest, SpriteSheet or StringTable change; no settings section changes.
- **Ledger positions taken:** PlaySessionPhase TextLatch (between ApplyInput and UiInput); the Ui category's fifth widget (appended); the vendored libraries' `Use<Lib>()`, `IncludeDir`, premake includes, `UseRuntimeDependencies` lines, `VENDOR_ABI_FAMILIES` entries, private defines, `PRECISE_VENDOR_PROJECTS` entries, fixtures and Lint prefixes (appended in landing order; M23's CMP_Core and codecs land beside them); `ModuleRules.json`'s `ThirdParty` and private lists (appended); IdSeeds 2013 to 2017. The built-in handles 0x01c2 to 0x01c4 are not taken (decision 42).
- **Files without a ledger owner that C22.3 changes:** `Scripting/Private/ScriptInstances.cpp` (the dispatch's two cases, appended); `Engine/premake5.lua`, `Editor/premake5.lua`, `Tests/premake5.lua`, `premake5.lua` and `Dependencies.lua` (the shared integration files' appends); `Scripts/CheckBuildConfig.py`, `Scripts/Lib/scripts.py` and `Scripts/Lint.py` (the ledger's appends for a new library). Everything else is M22's own.
- **The ledger amendment, granted on main in `1707cf47`** (decision 51; requested by C22.3 and granted by the lead, as C22.1's decision 17 was): M22 appends `QuadBatchTarget::LdrDepth` to the QuadBatcher (`Renderer/QuadBatcher.h`, M18's frozen interface, M21's in phase 2 for a change): Ldr's target (the scene overlay's framebuffer with SceneDepth, `GetOverlayFramebufferInfo`), blending and sRGB encoding, depth-tested GreaterOrEqual against SceneDepth without depth writes; one more pipeline (`PipelineCount` +1, after M21's HdrLit and Normals in landing order) and its startup count; `QuadVertex.h` and the other targets unchanged. Reason: a World canvas's images must be depth-tested in the SceneOverlay stage's Ui row, where M22 records, and no existing target does that (Hdr draws into SceneColor before the tonemap, Ldr without the depth test); a pipeline of M22's own would duplicate the batcher. Main's ledger row (ADR 0028's single-owner files, `QuadBatcher`) holds the granted text: `LdrDepth` and its pipeline appended after M21's targets (`HdrLit`, `Normals`), changing none of M21's, the later of M21 and M22 adapting after merging main. `milestone/m22` has not merged `1707cf47` yet; stream W builds the target on the granted text, and the milestone's merge of main brings the ledger row (the review of C22.3, the lead's decision 6).

### 58. Gate and verification (C22.3)

- **The contract checkpoint** runs `Generate.py`, the Debug build of every project (the vendored libraries through the vendor cache), HarfBuzz and SheenBidi with clang-cl in the portability workspace (Release and Dist), `CheckBuildConfig.py` on the workspace and on the two new fixtures, `Lint.py --allow-contract-stubs`, `Format.py --check`, `GenerateDocs.py --config Debug` (the regenerated references staged), and `Test.py --suite unit --config Debug --allow-skips` on the touched suites, each skipped CPU case forced with `--no-skip` to show it fails; not `PreCommit.py` or `CI.py`.
- **Each stream** runs the cases and suites its change touches once at the end (ADR 0023): T the Scene, Session, Scripting, Automation and EditorCore cases, `test_ui`, `test_ui_authoring` and the validation fixtures' check; S the Renderer, Asset and AssetPipeline cases with the FontData and FontImporter fuzz cases, in Debug and Release (shaped text byte-identical); F the AssetPipeline and Renderer cases, `FetchAssets.py fixture --check`, the font fuzz cases; W the Scene, Session, Renderer and Automation cases, `Test.py --suite gpu --require-gpu --filter "UiPass*"` under both API caps; I the Scripting cases and `test_script_tests`; E `Scaffold.py`, `Scaffold.py --check`, the feature suite and the golden suite for its goldens. A case that needs two streams (the caret case S and T, the shaping cases and the shaped-text digest case S and F, the extraction's world case W with the registration it needs, `test_script_tests`' recording test I and T, since it builds a widget of kind TextInput that only T's `ui.createWidget` kind makes, and the importer's fallback case F and S, since `FontData::Fallbacks` is read only from S's format 2 payload with version 5; the last two added by the review of C22.3) is un-skipped by the later of them or by the integration; stream E's scratch merge and the integration take the streams in an order that holds these pairs.
- **W22.3's integration** replaces every stub and skip of C22.3, registers what decision 40 left to the streams, regenerates the references and FeatureTest, and runs the feature, export, automation, determinism and games suites (every committed replay at its hash; the TextInput and world-canvas replays in the four runs, each failing without its input).
- **The milestone end** is `CI.py --require-clang-cl` (ADR 0028 decision 12; M23's gate hardens phase 2, with the FontData and FontImporter fuzz cases' new formats).

## Data formats

- **UI components in a scene** (decision 2):

  ```json
  "Components": {
  	"UiRect": { "AnchorMin": [0, 0], "AnchorMax": [1, 0], "Pivot": [0.5, 0], "Position": [0, 8], "Size": [-16, 40], "ClipChildren": false },
  	"UiImage": { "Sheet": "5ee7000000000001", "Frame": 2, "Color": [1, 1, 1, 0.9], "Type": "Sliced", "BorderScale": 1, "Filter": "Point" },
  	"UiLayout": { "Kind": "Flex", "Direction": "Horizontal", "Padding": { "Left": 4, "Top": 4, "Right": 4, "Bottom": 4 }, "Spacing": [6, 0],
  		"MainAlignment": "Center", "CrossAlignment": "Stretch", "Columns": 0, "CellSize": [100, 100], "Wrap": false }
  }
  ```

- **A `.stringtable` file** (decision 10):

  ```json
  {
  	"Format": "StringTable",
  	"Version": 1,
  	"Locales": ["en", "fr", "pt-BR"],
  	"Entries": [
  		{ "Key": "Menu.Start", "Values": { "en": "Start", "fr": "Commencer", "pt-BR": "Iniciar" } },
  		{ "Key": "Menu.Quit", "Values": { "en": "Quit" } }
  	]
  }
  ```

- **The project setting** (decision 11): `"Localization": { "Locale": "fr" }` after `"Testing"` in the `.eproj`.
- **A font's import settings** (decision 6): `"Settings": { "PixelSize": 48, "Spread": 8, "Ranges": [{ "First": 913, "Last": 937 }] }`.
- **The widgets in a scene** (C22.2, decision 21; a button with its label, and a slider with its parts):

  ```json
  "Components": {
  	"UiRect": { "AnchorMin": [0.5, 0.5], "AnchorMax": [0.5, 0.5], "Pivot": [0.5, 0.5], "Position": [0, 0], "Size": [200, 20], "ClipChildren": false },
  	"UiImage": { "Sheet": null, "Frame": 0, "Color": [0.3, 0.3, 0.3, 1], "Type": "Simple", "BorderScale": 1, "Filter": "Point", "RaycastTarget": true },
  	"UiSlider": { "Interactable": true, "Focusable": true, "Value": 0.25, "Min": 0, "Max": 1, "WholeNumbers": false, "Step": 0,
  		"Direction": "Horizontal", "Fill": "4c1f0a9e2b7d6e31", "Handle": "55425a047af78945" }
  }
  ```

- **The project setting** (C22.2, decision 30), after `"Localization"` in the `.eproj`:

  ```json
  "Ui": { "Navigation": true, "Submit": ["Key.Enter", "Key.Space", "Gamepad.South"], "Cancel": ["Key.Escape", "Gamepad.East"],
  	"Up": ["Key.Up", "Gamepad.DPadUp"], "Down": ["Key.Down", "Gamepad.DPadDown"], "Left": ["Key.Left", "Gamepad.DPadLeft"],
  	"Right": ["Key.Right", "Gamepad.DPadRight"], "Next": ["Key.Tab"], "StickNavigation": true }
  ```

- **`ui.createWidget`** (C22.2, decision 32): `{"kind": "Button", "parent": "/Canvas", "name": "Play", "text": "Play"}` returns `{"entity": {"id": ..., "name": "Play", "path": "/Canvas/Play"}, "entities": [<Play>, <Play/Label>], "undoIndex": 4}`.
- **A text input in a scene** (C22.3, decision 45; its two parts are child elements with UiTexts):

  ```json
  "Components": {
  	"UiRect": { "AnchorMin": [0.5, 0.5], "AnchorMax": [0.5, 0.5], "Pivot": [0.5, 0.5], "Position": [0, 0], "Size": [240, 32], "ClipChildren": false },
  	"UiImage": { "Sheet": null, "Frame": 0, "Color": [0.15, 0.15, 0.15, 1], "Type": "Simple", "BorderScale": 1, "Filter": "Point", "RaycastTarget": true },
  	"UiTextInput": { "Interactable": true, "Focusable": true, "Text": "Ada", "MaxLength": 32, "Caret": 3,
  		"TextElement": "1b7c2e9a40d35f68", "Placeholder": "6e0d9a2c51b47f13" }
  }
  ```

- **A World canvas** (C22.3, decision 49; UiCanvas's members after DefaultFocus): `"RenderMode": "World", "WorldWidth": 1024, "WorldHeight": 576, "PixelsPerUnit": 512`.
- **A font's fallbacks** (C22.3, decision 42): `"Settings": { "PixelSize": 48, "Spread": 8, "Ranges": [{ "First": 1569, "Last": 1610 }], "Fallbacks": ["a2ab000000000001", "7a9a000000000002"] }`.
- **FontData format 2** (C22.3, decision 44): format 1's payload, then each glyph's glyph index, the units per em, the indexed glyphs, the shaping source's size and bytes and the fallbacks' count and handles; stream S documents it byte for byte in `FontData.h`, as format 1 is.

## Waves, streams and file ownership (W22.1)

W22.1 starts at this contract; its seven streams run in parallel (plan §3.5: stream agents at phase start, M22 four; the rest follow as slots free). Streams own disjoint files; files shared by function say which function. A stream that needs a change in another's file lists it in its result. The shared integration files (`Docs/Reference/*`, `Resources/Scripting/Engine.d.luau`, `Tools/MCP/catalog.json`, `RegisterMethods.cpp`, `RegisterSharedMethods.cpp`, `RegisterBindings.cpp`, `BuiltinComponents.h`, the premake files, `Scripts/ModuleRules.json`) are the lead's at W22.1's integration: each stream lists the regeneration its change needs.

| Stream | Items | Owns |
|---|---|---|
| **A** core and layout pass (1.25 sessions) | R-UI-CORE (decisions 3, 4) | `Engine/Source/Engine/Scene/UiLayout.{h,cpp}`, `Scene/UiSystem.{h,cpp}`, `Scene/Registration/UiRegistration.cpp`, the component headers' comments, `Session/PlaySession.cpp`'s UiLayout and FrameUiLayout phases (by function: the end of `FixedStep`, the FrameUiLayout step of the frame phase, and the layouts that end `Activate` and `ConsumeRequests`' scene replacement; L owns the script engine's creation, K the LatchFrame phase); tests `Tests/Source/Engine/Scene/UiLayoutTests.cpp` (the container case with D), `UiSystemTests.cpp`, `Tests/Source/Engine/Session/PlaySessionTests.cpp`'s phase and layout cases; Architecture §5.3 and §5.7 |
| **B** text measurement, wrapping, character ranges (1.25 sessions) | R-UI-TEXT, R-UI-L10N's ranges (decision 6) | `Engine/Source/Engine/Renderer/TextMeasurement.{h,cpp}`, `AssetPipeline/Importers/FontImporter.{h,cpp}` (Ranges, version 4, the settings' registration); tests `TextMeasurementTests.cpp`, `FontImporterTests.cpp`; Architecture §7.4's FontImporter and §8.10's text |
| **C** draw list, extraction, UI pass, scissor (1.25 sessions) | R-UI-RENDER (decision 7) | `Engine/Source/Engine/Renderer/UiDrawList.{h,cpp}`, `Renderer/UiPass.{h,cpp}`, `Renderer/TextRenderer.{h,cpp}` (glyph runs), `Renderer/SceneRendererOutput.cpp`'s SceneOverlay stage, `Renderer/Private/SceneRendererState.h`'s Ui binding caches, `Scene/UiExtraction.cpp`'s `AppendUiDrawItems`, `Scene/RenderExtraction.cpp`'s UI (after M23's paths); tests `UiPassTests.cpp` (the text case with B), `UiDrawListTests.cpp`, `UiExtractionTests.cpp`'s draw-list cases, `TextRendererTests.cpp`; the UI golden's render with E; Architecture §8.2 and §8.3 |
| **D** layout containers (1 session) | R-UI-LAYOUT (decision 5) | `Engine/Source/Engine/Scene/UiContainerLayout.{h,cpp}`; tests `UiContainerLayoutTests.cpp`, UiLayoutTests' container case (with A) |
| **E** `ui.tree` and static coverage (1.25 sessions) | R-UI-AUTO, R-UI-COVER's static half (decisions 8, 9, 13, 14) | `Engine/Source/Engine/Automation/Methods/UiMethods.{h,cpp}`, `Scene/UiExtraction.cpp`'s `AppendUiRenderAnnotations` and its call in `Scene/RenderAnnotations.cpp` (planned after M23's paths; placed before the camera early return that M23's paths follow, touching none of their lines: W22.1's review), `Editor/Source/EditorCore/Project/ProjectValidator.cpp`'s two UI codes (both tables), `Tests/Data/Generate/MakeValidationFixtures.py` and `Tests/Data/Validation/UI_*`; FeatureTest's `Ui.jsonl` (2001) and `UiGolden.jsonl` (2002), their `Scaffolds.json` entries and the `Suites.jsonl` line; the skills (decision 9); tests `UiMethodsTests.cpp`, UiExtractionTests' annotations case, `Tests/Automation/test_ui.py`; Architecture §13.5, §13.7 and §13.8 |
| **L** string tables (1 session) | R-UI-L10N's string tables (decisions 10, 11) | `Engine/Source/Engine/Asset/StringTableData.{h,cpp}`, `Asset/{AssetTypeRegistration,AssetLoaderRegistry,CookedFormat,SourceFormats}.cpp` (the type's entries), `AssetPipeline/Importers/StringTableImporter.{h,cpp}` and its registration, `Automation/Methods/AutomationTypes.cpp`'s AssetType entry, `Editor/Source/EditorCore/Automation/AssetMethods.{h,cpp}` (`AssetCreateType::StringTable`), `Editor/Source/Editor/Panels/ContentBrowserPanel.cpp`'s create entry, `Scripting/Bindings/Loc.cpp`, `Scripting/ScriptEngine.{h,cpp}`'s locale, `Project/ProjectSettings.{h,cpp}` (Localization), `Session/PlaySession.cpp`'s script engine creation (by function), `EditorMethodContext.cpp`'s edit VM (decision 17's amendment, granted), the reference generator's format entry, `Tests/Data/Project/AllSettings.eproj` with its generator and the canonical templates and fixtures, `Tests/Data/Assets/StringTables/`, FeatureTest's `Localization.jsonl` (2003); tests `StringTableDataTests.cpp` (the fuzz case included), `StringTableImporterTests.cpp`, `LocTests.cpp`, the content browser's case, `Tests/Automation/test_string_tables.py`; Architecture §6, §6.1, §7.4 and §11.5 |
| **K** W-CLOCK (0.5 session) | W-CLOCK (decision 12) | `Engine/Source/Engine/Session/PlayInput.{h,cpp}`, `Session/PlaySession.cpp`'s LatchFrame phase (by function); tests `PlayInputTests.cpp`'s W-CLOCK cases, `PlaySessionTests.cpp`'s preview case (and a recording case of its own); Architecture §4.3 and §5.7's frame step 1 |

**The lead (W22.1's integration):** this record, `Docs/Backlog.md` (§5's input row closed, §7's UI line), the Roadmap if a decision here changes a deliverable's words, the shared integration files and their regeneration, `Scaffold.py`, the recorded `Editor --upgrade` of the audited projects for the Localization section, and the list of M22's textures for M23 (decision 7).

## Acceptance (W22.1)

Roadmap M22's acceptance lines that W22.1 owns, with the cases that state them (the contract's skipped cases; Python tests skipped with "contract stub"):

- **UI layout tables are byte-identical across configurations:** "UiLayout: anchors, pivot, position and size place an element in its parent in scene pixels", "UiLayout: a canvas maps its units to scene pixels by ScaleFactor or by MatchHeight", "UiLayout: PixelSnap rounds every corner to whole scene pixels before the children are placed", "UiLayout: ClipChildren clips the descendants to the intersection of their clipping ancestors, not the element itself", "UiLayout: canvases are in SortOrder, ties in entity order, each with its elements in canonical order", "UiLayout: an effectively disabled element, its subtree and an element without a canvas are not laid out", "UiLayout: a rectangle's clip covers the pixels whose centres it holds", "UiLayout: a container places its children, and an element that ignores the layout or is disabled takes no space", the six "UiContainerLayout: ..." cases, "UiSystem: the current layout is the frame's after a FrameUiLayout and the step's after a UiLayout", "PlaySession: UiLayout ends the fixed step and FrameUiLayout follows FrameTransformUpdate, in Play and Simulate" and "PlaySession: the current UI layout is the scene's from the start and the loaded scene's right after a Scene.Load" (exact values in the Debug and Release unit runs).
- **String lookups are byte-identical across configurations:** the six "StringTableData: ..." cases (the cooked layout written independently, the fuzz case), the two "StringTableImporter: ..." cases and the three "Loc: ..." cases; Python `test_string_tables.StringTableTests`' two tests.
- **Text measurement and character ranges:** the five "TextMeasurement: ..." cases and "FontImporter: a font's Ranges add their codepoints to ASCII and Latin-1".
- **A 1:1 UI image reads back pixel-exact, and the scissor oracle holds:** "UiPass: a 1:1 UI image reads back its source texels pixel-exact" and "UiPass: a clipped element leaves the pixels outside its clip untouched" (GPU), with "UiPass: at scale 3 the UI is upscaled with the scene image, its row between Text and Upscale", the loss test "UiPass: a device loss recreates nothing of the pass's own and the UI draws the same image afterwards", and the six CPU "UiPass: ..." cases; the extraction cases "RenderExtraction: a canvas's visible images and texts reach the Ui list in draw order with their scaled lengths" and "RenderExtraction: the editor's scene view draws no Screen canvas, and a view without a primary camera draws it" (renamed by W22.3's review from "... draws no game UI, ...", since the scene view draws World canvases, decision 51).
- **`ui.tree` passes gate 5 and works in the Runtime:** "UiMethods: ui.tree lists every canvas and element with its scene-pixel rectangle in draw order", "UiMethods: ui.tree of the play scene reads the session's current layout", "UiMethods: ui.tree refuses a canvas reference without a UiCanvas, and one of width and height alone", Python `test_ui.UiTreeTests.test_ui_tree_lists_the_edit_and_play_scene_ui_in_scene_pixels` and `test_ui_tree_works_in_the_runtime`; the annotations: "RenderAnnotations: bounds outline every drawn UI element and labels follow the label mode".
- **A frame that runs no step sees new input while the step view is unchanged:** "PlayInput: a frame that runs no step sees new device input while the step view is unchanged", "PlayInput: a previewed press is reported once by the frame view", "PlayInput: injected events are never previewed, and without the preview the frame view follows the ticks" and "PlaySession: the frame preview is on in live play and off in lockstep".
- **Scenes without UI keep their hash:** every committed replay, unchanged (no hash block in W22.1; decision 3), through the determinism and games suites at the milestone end; "RenderExtraction: a scene without a canvas has an empty Ui list" (live).
