## Review of C22.1

One review of the frozen interfaces (ADR 0028 decision 11), read-only, of the staged contract against `111c1c9`, with every new file read in full: 11 findings, none a blocker or a major; 8 minor and 3 nits, each checked against the code, all confirmed and fixed in this checkpoint, none rejected. The review confirmed the ledger's positions (AssetType StringTable 12; IdSeeds 2001 to 2003; UiLayout after PostStepTransformUpdate and FrameUiLayout after FrameTransformUpdate; Ui after Text in SceneOverlay with no pipeline of its own, 58 startup pipelines and 26 stats rows; `UiDrawList.h` in SnapshotHeaders and Scene's Headers, the measurement header left out; `RenderSnapshot::Ui` after `Output`; the Ui category after Scripting and Localization after Testing; the `Targets.json` row sorted by Reader), the stub and skip counts, that no public header exposes a third-party type, and the skipped cases' expected values against the frozen rules. The lead granted decision 17's ledger amendment (amended on main 2026-10-29) and accepted the `StringTableData` fuzz target's move to stream L (decision 10).

**Minor**

- **m1. `UiRect` did not declare its exclusion of `UiCanvas`** (`UiRegistration.cpp`). Confirmed: `Entity:AddComponent` (`Scripting/Bindings/Entity.cpp`), `component.list` and `component.schema` (`ComponentMethods.cpp`) and the reference generator read only the added component's own `GetExcludes`, so a script could add a UiRect to a canvas and `Components.md` showed UiRect with "Excludes: none". Fixed: UiRect declares `.Excludes<UiCanvasComponent>()`, as RigidBody and CharacterController declare each other (decision 2's table); the references are regenerated; the new live case "BuiltinComponents: every exclusion is declared by both components" fails without the fix.
- **m2. `LayoutUiContainer`'s rules divided by 0 for valid values** (`UiContainerLayout.h`). Confirmed: a Flex line whose total of Shrink times size is 0 (every Shrink 0, or zero sizes) gave 0/0, and a Grid with Columns 0 and a main-axis CellSize plus Spacing of 0 divided by 0 before an integer conversion (and a tiny cell gave a count beyond any integer). Fixed: such a Flex line does not shrink and overflows; the fitted Grid count is clamped to 1 to 1024 (Columns' own range), 1024 when the cell plus spacing is 0, never computed from a division by 0; the header and decision 5 say that no rule divides by 0 and every placed size is at least 0. The Flex and Grid cases of `UiContainerLayoutTests.cpp` gain both inputs.
- **m3. `ComputeUiClipRect` converted unbounded floats to int32, and `UiRegistration.cpp`'s comment about the length range was wrong.** Confirmed: MatchHeight scales a unit to up to the scene's height in pixels, so registry-valid values pass int32's range and float's exact integers (2^24). Fixed: each converted coordinate is clamped to ±2^30 (`UiClipCoordinateLimit`, `UiLayout.h`; a NaN to -2^30), decision 4 says so, and the comment now says that such values are inexact but deterministic; "UiLayout: a rectangle's clip covers the pixels whose centres it holds" checks rectangles beyond int32's range.
- **m4. Two frozen comments disagreed on which elements a container places.** Confirmed: `UiLayoutComponent.h` said "the entity's direct children with a UiRect" while decision 2, `UiRectComponent.h` and `UiLayout.h` make an entity without a UiRect transparent. Fixed: one rule, the container places the elements whose parent element it is, in canonical order, stated alike in `UiLayoutComponent.h`, `UiRectComponent.h`, `UiLayoutElementComponent.h`, `UiLayout.h`, `UiContainerLayout.h`, decisions 2 and 5 and Architecture §5.3; UiLayoutTests' container case adds a grouping entity whose element the column stacks.
- **m5. The session's current layout was empty before the first tick and described the replaced scene after a `Scene.Load`.** Confirmed: the scene is replaced at the end of a frame, after FrameUiLayout ran, and nothing laid the new scene out before the next step's UiInput. Fixed: decision 3's new rule, frozen in `UiSystem.h` and `PlaySession.h` and stated in Architecture §5.7: `Activate` and a scene replacement each end with `LayoutStep` of the current scene, outside the phase list (stream A owns both calls). New skipped case "PlaySession: the current UI layout is the scene's from the start and the loaded scene's right after a Scene.Load" (54 skipped C++ cases, 50 of them CPU cases).
- **m6. `ui.tree`'s JSON entries lacked a canvas's sort order and scale, which its text outline prints.** Confirmed. Fixed: `UiTreeEntry` gains `sortOrder` and `scale` (the scene pixels per canvas unit), a canvas's, 0 for an element, registered with descriptions (decision 8, Architecture §13.5); `Methods.json` is regenerated (the MCP catalogue holds no result types, so it is unchanged); "UiMethods: ui.tree lists every canvas and element ..." and the Python test check them.
- **m7. The damaged-payload case put two faults in one payload.** Confirmed: entries out of order and a Present byte of 2 together, so either check alone passed it. Fixed: two payloads, each with one fault ("StringTableData: a damaged payload is refused, never trusted").
- **m8. The FontImporter `Ranges` limits were frozen but untested.** Confirmed. Fixed: "FontImporter: a font's Ranges add their codepoints to ASCII and Latin-1" refuses 65 ranges and 65,537 codepoints with a Validation error located under `/Ranges`, and accepts exactly 65,536 in two ranges (codepoints Inter lacks, so the bake stays ASCII and Latin-1).

**Nits**

- **n1. `TextMeasurement.h`'s line formulas left out the em size.** Confirmed: the metrics are in em. Fixed: `Top`, the block height and `LineSpacing`'s comment multiply by `Size`.
- **n2. `UiMethodsTests.cpp`'s comment named `test_runtime.py`.** Confirmed. Fixed: it names `Tests/Automation/test_ui.py` (`UiTreeTests.test_ui_tree_works_in_the_runtime`).
- **n3. `Loc.Has` did not say what a table that fails to load does.** Confirmed. Fixed: it raises a located error as `Get` does, in the registration (regenerated into `ScriptAPI.md` and `Engine.d.luau`), decision 11 and Architecture §11.5; the Loc case, renamed "Loc: Get of a key the table lacks, and Get or Has of an asset that is not a string table or fails to load, raise a located error", removes the table while a chunk that holds it runs and expects both lookups to raise. Fixing it showed that the case's refused lookups log script errors it did not declare, which would fail it once stream L un-skips it: it now declares them (`Test::ExpectLog` of "Loc.", the binding's prefix of every Loc error).

**Runs** (contract mode, ADR 0028 decision 11): `Build.py --config Debug --jobs 12` of every project (passed, 0 warnings); `GenerateDocs.py --config Debug` (four references updated: `Components.md`, `Methods.json`, `ScriptAPI.md`, `Engine.d.luau`; `--check` passed afterwards); `Lint.py --allow-contract-stubs` (passed: 35 stubs and 58 skips allowed, 54 C++ and 4 Python); `Format.py --check` (passed); `Test.py --suite unit --config Debug --allow-skips` on the touched suites (BuiltinComponents, TypeRegistry, ComponentInfo, ComponentMethods, RegisterMethods, RegisterBindings, ScriptApiRegistry, ReferenceGenerator, UiClipRect, UiDrawList, UiLayout, UiContainerLayout, UiSystem, UiMethods, PlaySession, PlaySessionPhase, StringTableData, StringTableImporter, FontImporter, Loc, TextMeasurement, RenderExtraction, RenderAnnotations, SceneTarget, RuntimeAutomationServer: 193 passed, 41 skipped); m1's regression case alone without the fix (failed) and with it (passed); and the nine skipped cases this review changed or added, forced with `--no-skip`, each failing against the stubs as decision 1 says.

## Integration (W22.1)

W22.1's seven stream branches were merged onto `milestone/m22` at `1e29dd4` (the contract with main merged in: ADR 0039's CPU-time script budgets and the phase-2 ledger's amendments) in the order A, B, C, D, E, L, K, then staged as one change.

  - `Scene/UiExtraction.cpp` (C and E): the union, C's `AppendUiDrawItems` with its enum mappings and E's `AppendUiRenderAnnotations` with its colours and label rule, both streams' includes, one file comment.
  - Architecture §7.4's importer table (B and L): L's `StringTableImporter` row and B's `FontImporter` row with `Ranges` and version 4.
  - `Scaffolds.json` (E and L): `Ui.jsonl` (2001), `UiGolden.jsonl` (2002), then `Localization.jsonl` (2003), after main's scaffolds and before `Suites.jsonl`.
  - K's frame step 1 of Architecture §5.7 merged beside A's UiLayout and FrameUiLayout lines without a conflict, as did the three streams' functions of `PlaySession.cpp` (A's phases and layouts, L's locale, K's preview policy).
- **Un-skipped** (each needed two streams): "UiLayout: a container places its children, and an element that ignores the layout or is disabled takes no space" (A with D); "UiPass: a text lays out in its rectangle by its alignments and wraps at its width", "UiPass: runs split where the kind, the clip, the font or the colour changes, in draw order" and "UiPass: annotations draw after the items as one-pixel outlines and labels" (C with B); "RenderExtraction: a canvas's visible images and texts reach the Ui list in draw order with their scaled lengths" and "RenderExtraction: the editor's scene view draws no game UI, and a view without a primary camera draws it" (C with A). E's three UiMethods cases, the UI annotations case and `test_ui.py`, and L's cases and `test_string_tables.py`, were un-skipped by their streams. Every C22.1 stub is replaced and no skip of the contract remains (`Lint.py` strict).
- **Finished from the streams' open items** (files outside their ownership):
  - `Scripts/ModuleRules.json`'s `Json` extensions gain `.stringtable` (L), as `AuthoredFilesTests.cpp`'s list already had it.
  - `project.upgrade` rewrites `.stringtable` files canonically (`ProjectMethods.cpp`'s `MakeCanonicalFile` and upgraded extensions, appended after the sprite sheet's by the ledger's type-keyed rule), read with unknown members refused as their importer reads them: "ProjectMethods: project.upgrade rewrites .stringtable files canonically and fails at an unknown member of one". The method's description, its header, Architecture §13.5 and §13.12 and the game-debugging skill name string tables.
  - `Suites.jsonl`'s one `Testing.Suites` patch gains the Localization suite (no scene, every mode) after MultiScript's, so `Loc` is covered in Editor, Release and Dist (L).
  - `Field.Asset`'s description names `StringTable` (L), and the game-building skill's `asset_create` types (L); its `ui` page says that a previewed press is reported once in `OnUpdate` by `Input.IsKeyPressed`, `IsMouseButtonPressed` and `IsActionPressed`, possibly a frame before the tick that applies it (K).
  - `GoldenSceneTests.cpp` checks `Components/Ui.scene`'s strict load and byte-identical re-save and `UiGolden.jsonl`'s imported `Rgba.png` against its fixture (E's suggestion).
  - The validation fixtures were regenerated: E's two UI fixtures take L's Localization section.
  - FeatureTest's `Scaffold/Baseline/FeatureTest.eproj` holds the Localization section, as M19 added its export setting there.
- **The lead's decisions:**
  - C's two lines in `SceneRenderer.cpp` (the UI quad binding cache's `Clear` and `ReleaseUnused`, beside every view cache's) are accepted: M22's Ui slot of that file by the ledger.
  - K's `InputState::SetFrameView`, with "InputState: SetFrameView takes another state's frame view and leaves the live state and step side unchanged" in `InputStateTests.cpp`, is accepted: no other stream or milestone owns `InputState`, and one state's single live view cannot hold a preview without changing the step view (decision 12).
  - D's cross-axis rule is accepted as specified (decision 5): a Stack's or Flex's child larger than its line across follows CrossAlignment's formula, so Center overflows both sides equally and End overflows the start; `UiContainerLayout.h`'s comment now says so (a comment-only change of the frozen header, by its owner).
  - B's rules beside `TextMeasurement.h` stay in `TextMeasurement.cpp`'s file comment and Architecture §8.10 (decision 6 amended). `MeasureText` counts each ill-formed UTF-8 byte as one skipped codepoint while `TextLayout` counts maximal subparts; both are deterministic and differ only on truncated multi-byte sequences.
  - **Accepted limits** (Backlog §6): the glyphs a font's `Ranges` add are not kerned, kerning staying among ASCII and Latin-1 (B: the TrueType kerning reader's dense pair table would take about 17 GB for 65,536 glyphs); automation input queued for a tick ahead of a device event the frame view already previewed reaches the frame view only as the state the tick leaves (K: live play only; the step view is unaffected). Backlog §5's row about a frame without a step seeing device input only at the next tick is closed, and §7's UI line names the static UI as delivered.
- **Generated files:** `GenerateDocs.py --config Debug` updated `Automation.md`, `FileFormats.md` (the Font rows at importer version 4, the StringTable format), `Methods.json`, `ScriptAPI.md`, `Validation.md` (the two UI codes), the `AssetMeta` and `Project` schemas and the new `StringTable` schema, `Engine.d.luau` and the MCP catalogue. `Scaffold.py` regenerated FeatureTest: `Ui.jsonl`'s `Components/Ui.scene` and `Api/Ui` suite, `UiGolden.jsonl`'s `GoldenUi` scene, texture and sheet, `Localization.jsonl`'s `Menu.stringtable`, suite and `Localization.Locale` `fr-CA`, and the two fonts' metas at `ImporterVersion` 4 with `Ranges: []` (B); every replay re-recorded byte for byte, so no hash changed; `Scaffold.py --check` reproduces the project.

## Review of W22.1

One review pass of the integrated wave diff (ADR 0028 decision 11), read-only, of the staged change against `1e29dd4`, in three lenses (correctness and robustness; determinism, formats and integration; tests, parity and documentation), each reading every new file in full: 16 findings, none a blocker or a major, 11 minor and 5 nits (one minor, the parity of `FontImportSettings.Ranges`, raised by the correctness lens outside its lens and taken up by the lead). Each was checked against the code and confirmed, and every one is fixed in this checkpoint; none is rejected. The lenses found sound, among others: W-CLOCK's preview and catch-up across device and injected interleavings, the session's preview policy, the layout's and the containers' arithmetic (no division by 0, no unbounded loop, IEEE-exact operations in canonical order), `MeasureText`'s wrapping, the Ui pass's scissors, 9-slice checks and binding lifetimes, the cooked string-table reader, the ledger positions (AssetType StringTable 12, FontImporter 4, the IdSeeds, the phases, the validation codes, the create list, `UiQuadBindings`), and that no frozen header changed beyond the lead-approved comments.

**The lead's decisions on the review** (binding):

1. The UI annotation call's place (m3) is accepted as placed, with no code move (below).
2. Decision 12's sentence about the hosts (n1) is reworded as the reviewer proposed.
3. A UI image whose sprite sheet is missing (m1) is reported in code, through the asset manager's diagnostic path, with a regression test.
4. The font baker (m2) measures and packs before it rasterizes, with a test that fails fast and asserts a bound the code exposes, never wall time.
5. FeatureTest covers a font with a non-empty `Ranges` (m11), in the Ui scaffold's scene, not the UiGolden scene, so no golden changes.
6. The skill page's `WasPressed` (m9) names the real functions, here and in the Integration section.
7. Main's `4535c203` (the ImGui font atlas's border sampler, with the editor goldens it regenerated) is not merged in this step: the lead merges main after this checkpoint.

**Minor**

- **m1. A `UiImage` whose sheet is unknown or of another type drew nothing with no log or diagnostic** (`Renderer/UiPass.cpp`, correctness). Confirmed: `AssetManager::Load` of an unregistered handle records nothing, the sheet has no placeholder, so `GetOrPlaceholder`'s report never ran, and `SkippedItems` reached no reader; `UiPass.h` said that the manager records it. Fixed (the lead's decision 3): `PlanUiPass` reports an unusable sheet once per plan through `AssetManager::ReportDiagnostic`, as `GetOrPlaceholder` reports a missing mesh or font (`ASSET_MISSING` for an unknown handle, `ASSET_TYPE_MISMATCH` for another type or a handle that cannot load on its own, `ASSET_IMPORT_FAILED` for a failed load; each saying that the image draws nothing, logged once per manager and, as reference diagnostics, never blocking play or export). `UiPass.h`'s comments, decision 7 and Architecture §8.3's Ui row say so. "UiPass: an image whose sheet is missing or lacks its frame draws nothing and is counted" now plans twice and checks one `ASSET_MISSING` and one `ASSET_TYPE_MISMATCH` recorded and logged once; without the fix it failed (no diagnostic, no log).
- **m2. `FontImporter` rasterized every glyph before it learned that the atlas could not hold them** (`AssetPipeline/Importers/FontImporter.cpp`, correctness). Confirmed: the `Ranges` limits bound the codepoints, not each glyph's field, so a CJK-sized range at `PixelSize` 128 and `Spread` 32 rasterized gigabytes of fields before `PackAtlas` failed. Fixed (the lead's decision 4): `TrueTypeFont::GetGlyphSdfBox` measures a glyph's padded box without computing a field (the 2,048-pixel refusal moves there), `RenderGlyphSdf` rasterizes over a measured box, and the new `FontImporter::Bake` (which `Import` calls) measures every box and packs them before it rasterizes any field, so glyphs that cannot fit `MaxFontAtlasSize` (16,384, now public) fail with ImportFailed and no field computed; its `FontBakeReport` exposes the bound (the glyphs, the atlas side, the fields rasterized). Atlases are byte-identical (the pinned Inter hashes and the version-4 cooked-byte cases pass). New case "FontImporter: Ranges whose glyphs cannot fit the atlas fail before any glyph is rasterized": `Glyphs.ttf` with a format 13 cmap mapping U+4E00 to U+9FFF to one glyph and a 16,384-codepoint range at the largest settings fails with `RasterizedGlyphs` 0, the import fails alike, and a 64-codepoint range bakes 65 fields into a 2048 atlas. Without the fix it failed (16,385 fields rasterized before the failure, 68 s in Debug). Architecture §7.4 says so.
- **m3. The UI annotation call sits before the existing annotation paths, which the ledger and stream E's row placed it after** (`Scene/RenderAnnotations.cpp`, determinism). Confirmed. Accepted as placed (the lead's decision 1): `AppendUiRenderAnnotations` precedes the `HasCamera` early return because UI annotations need no camera (decision 7: a view without a primary camera draws its UI), and it moves and changes no line of M23's existing mesh and light paths, so the ledger's intent, no reordering of the owner's paths, holds and no ledger amendment on main is needed. Stream E's row above and the call's comment record it, so a later lander keeps the call before the early return.
- **m4. `CheckUi`'s two validation codes had no unit test** (`ProjectValidator.cpp`, tests). Confirmed: the gate-6 fixtures check only which codes a project reports. Fixed: "ProjectValidator: UI_ELEMENT_WITHOUT_CANVAS and UI_IMAGE_FRAME_OUT_OF_RANGE name the element and stay quiet for valid UI" (an element through a grouping entity, one below a nested canvas, an image at its sheet's last frame and a plain image stay quiet; the stray element and the image past the last frame are each named once at their entity, component and field).
- **m5. `ui.tree`'s text outline, clip and visibility were untested** (`UiMethodsTests.cpp`, tests). Confirmed. Fixed: "UiMethods: ui.tree reports clips, hidden elements, sheets and containers in its JSON and its text outline" checks `clipped`, `clip` and `visible` of elements inside and outside a clipping panel and off the scene image, a stack container's placement, and the whole outline line by line: the header, the canvas line, the `├─ `, `└─ ` and `│  ` prefixes to depth 3, the sheet and container short forms and the `clipped` and `hidden` markers.
- **m6. The play-scene `ui.tree` tests could not tell the session's layout from a fresh one** (`UiMethodsTests.cpp`, tests). Confirmed: in lockstep the view is the window size. Fixed: "UiMethods: ui.tree of the play scene reads the session's current layout" moves the panel with `entity.update {target: play}` without stepping and expects the old rectangle, the new one from `ui.tree` with an explicit size, and the new one after the next step.
- **m7. Most string-table limits and the cooked entry-count checks had no test** (`StringTableDataTests.cpp`, tests). Confirmed. Fixed: "StringTableData: the locale and entry counts, the key length and the text rules are checked at their limits" (65 locales and 65,537 entries refused, 64 and 65,536 accepted; a 257-byte key refused and a 256-byte one accepted; a key with a tab, a C1 control or an ill-formed byte and a value with a truncated sequence or a C1 control refused; a 16,384-byte value with a line feed and a tab accepted) and "StringTableData: the cooked reader holds the entry count to its limit and to the bytes that follow" (65,537 written entries refused as Parse before any is read, where without the limit they would be read and refused as Validation; 65,536 and 65 entries read; a count beyond the bytes that follow refused).
- **m8. `Loc.Get`'s closest-keys hint was never asserted** (`LocTests.cpp`, tests). Confirmed. Fixed: the Loc error case checks that a misspelt key's error names `Menu.Start` and not `Menu.Quit`, that a key like none names neither, and that a 257-byte key and one with an ill-formed byte are named by their byte count in valid UTF-8 without the key's bytes.
- **m9. The game-building skill's `ui` page named a script function that does not exist** (`WasPressed`, tests). Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 6): the page and the Integration section above name `Input.IsKeyPressed`, `IsMouseButtonPressed` and `IsActionPressed`.
- **m10. Architecture §15.5 did not list the scene-less Localization suite or `Localization.Locale`** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: §15.5's introduction covers a suite without a scene, its table gains the Localization suite's row, and its settings coverage names `Localization.Locale`.
- **m11. `FontImportSettings.Ranges` had no FeatureTest coverage** (correctness, outside its lens: Roadmap rule 5). Confirmed: the tests lens found it allowed by §15.6 for import settings, and the lead decided to cover it (decision 5). Fixed: `Ui.jsonl` imports Inter (`Scaffold/Sources/Fonts/Inter-Regular.otf`) into `Assets/Ui/Fonts/` with `Ranges` [U+0100, U+017F] (Latin Extended-A), and the Ui scene's second canvas gains `Accent`, a text in that font whose ligatures and Polish letters only the range bakes ("Œuvre, cœur, Łódź"); the `Api.Ui` suite reads its font and text back, and the scene renders it through `Test.Screenshot` in Editor, Release and Dist. The UiGolden scene is unchanged, so no golden changes. Decision 14's row, §15.5's Ui row and FeatureTest's `AGENTS.md` say so.

**Nits**

- **n1. Decision 12 justified the preview's determinism with a false premise about the hosts** (determinism). Confirmed: the Runtime queues window input in lockstep and the editor's Game view while recording. Fixed (the lead's decision 2): the policy, not the hosts, keeps the preview out of lockstep and recording sessions, which may still receive device input, while replay and test sessions receive none.
- **n2. The Localization suite and decision 14 attributed `Loc` calls to gate 3** (tests). Confirmed: gate 3 is lifecycle callbacks. Fixed: `Localization.jsonl`'s comment says gate 2 (regenerated into the suite and `Provenance.json`) and decision 14's row gates 2 and 9.
- **n3. A FontImporter case named for version 3 asserted version 4** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: the case checks no version number ("FontImporter: a font's Ranges add their codepoints to ASCII and Latin-1" keeps the check), and its comment says that every later version keeps the version-2 atlases for a font without `Ranges`; its name stays, since ADR 0030 decision 23's acceptance table names it.
- **n4. Comment lines edited in place were left past the comment width** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: `ProjectMethods.cpp`'s upgrade comment, `AssetMethods.h`'s `asset.create` and `asset.setProperties` comments and `ReferenceGenerator.h`'s schema row are re-filled to their neighbours' width.
- **n5. Decision 18 said that W22.1 changes no editor panel** (tests). Confirmed: the content browser's Create popup gains "String table". Fixed: decision 18 says that no view a golden captures changes, and what the popup gains.

**Runs** (a checkpoint's targeted runs, strict, ADR 0028 decision 11): `Build.py --jobs 12` of Debug, Release and Dist (every project, 0 warnings); m1's and m2's regression cases alone without their fixes (both failed: no diagnostic and no log; 16,385 fields rasterized before the failure) and with them (passed); `Scaffold.py`, then `Scaffold.py --check` (passed; every replay re-recorded byte for byte, so no replay or hash changed and the determinism suite has nothing new to verify); `GenerateDocs.py --check --config Debug` (21 references verified: the fixes change no registered surface); `Lint.py` strict (0 findings); `Format.py --check` (passed); `Test.py --suite unit --config Debug` (4,229 cases, 4,228 passed: "TestRunner: a suite registers a bounded number of cases", which no fix touches, exceeded its suite's 1,000 ms script budget while the machine ran this step's GPU suites and other worktrees' runs, and passed alone); `Test.py --suite unit --config Release` on the touched cases (156 passed: FontImporter, UiPass, UiMethods, StringTableData, Loc, ProjectValidator, RenderAnnotations, EngineAssetBaker, DocExamples, FeatureTest, GoldenScene, AuthoredFiles, TextMeasurement, UiLayout and UiContainerLayout); `Test.py --suite gpu --config Debug --require-gpu --filter "UiPass*"` under both API caps (5 cases each); `--suite golden --config Release` (26 matched, `Ui` included); `--suite feature --config Debug,Release` (the six gates green, FeatureTest unchanged); `--suite export --config Release,Dist` (the six gates green in each mode, the coverage merge green); `--suite automation --filter test_ui,test_string_tables` (4 tests passed), all with `--require-gpu`, all passed.

## Waves, streams and file ownership (W22.2)

W22.2 starts at C22.2; its five streams run in parallel (plan §3.5: A interaction and hash 1.5 sessions, B events and widgets 1.5, C ScrollView and theme 1.25, D minimal authoring 1.25, E `Ui.replay` and widget goldens 1). Streams own disjoint files; files shared by function say which function. A stream that needs a change in another's file lists it in its result. The shared integration files (`Docs/Reference/*`, `Resources/Scripting/Engine.d.luau`, `Tools/MCP/catalog.json`, `RegisterMethods.cpp`, `RegisterBindings.cpp`, `BuiltinComponents.h`, the premake files, `Scripts/ModuleRules.json`) are the lead's at W22.2's integration: B's one `RegisterUi` line in `RegisterBindings.cpp` is taken there, and each stream lists the regeneration its change needs.

| Stream | Items | Owns |
|---|---|---|
| **A** interaction and hash (1.5 sessions) | R-UI-INPUT, R-UI-DET, R-UI-AUTO's fields (decisions 22, 23, 24, 29, 30, 31) | `Engine/Source/Engine/Scene/UiInteraction.{h,cpp}`, `Scene/UiSystem.{h,cpp}`, `Scene/Components/Ui{Image,Text}Component.h`'s RaycastTarget and its registration in `Scene/Registration/UiRegistration.cpp` (by function), `Session/PlaySession.cpp` (by function: the UiInput phase, the input frame from the step view and `Project.Ui`, the Ui settings' check at Prepare, the ui block of `ComputeStateHash`, `ResetInteraction` at a scene replacement, `RenderExtractionRequest::UiInteraction` in the RenderExtraction phase and `ExtractView`), `Project/ProjectSettings.{h,cpp}` (the Ui section), `Tests/Data/Project/AllSettings.eproj` with its generator and the canonical templates and fixtures, `Automation/Methods/UiMethods.{h,cpp}`; tests `UiInteractionTests.cpp`, `UiSystemTests.cpp`'s C22.2 cases (the keyboard-and-gamepad and drag-and-wheel cases with B and C, the default-focus case with B), `PlaySessionTests.cpp`'s UI cases (the phase case with B) and the extended step-order cases, `ProjectSettingsTests.cpp`'s Ui case, `UiMethodsTests.cpp`'s interaction case, `Tests/Automation/test_ui.py`'s interaction tests (the click test with B, the Runtime's); Architecture §4.3's UI input, §5.1's state-hash paragraph (the ui block after physics2d, what it holds and when it is empty, and that it brings the UI interaction, an input-driven state, into the hash: the lead's decision on the review of C22.2), §5.7, §6.1's Ui section and §13.5's ui.tree |

**The lead (W22.2's integration):** this record, `Docs/Backlog.md` (§7's UI line: the interaction delivered), the Roadmap if a decision here changes a deliverable's words, the shared integration files and their regeneration, `Scaffold.py`, the recorded `Editor --upgrade` of the audited projects for the Ui section, the list of M22's textures for M23 (decision 36), and the editor-UI goldens if a stream changed a view they capture (decisions 32 to 34 keep every change in popups and in the UI preview, which no golden shows).

## Acceptance (W22.2)

Roadmap M22's acceptance lines that W22.2 owns, with the cases that state them (the contract's skipped cases; Python tests skipped with "contract stub"):

- **Every widget works by keyboard and gamepad:** "UiSystem: every widget works by keyboard and gamepad", "UiSystem: with nothing focused, navigation focuses only a canvas's DefaultFocus, and a press focuses only a focusable widget", "PlaySession: the Ui settings' bindings drive navigation, and a binding that is not a key or gamepad button fails the start", "UiInteraction: directional navigation picks the nearest focusable element along the direction", "UiInteraction: Next and Previous cycle the focusable elements in draw order", "UiInteraction: the stick's direction is its larger axis at one half or more", "UiWidgets: focusing an element inside a scroll view's content reveals it", and the Ui replay's navigation (decision 36).
- **Scenes without UI keep their hash:** "PlaySession: the state hash holds the ui block only while the UI interaction state or event queue is not empty", "UiSystem: the ui block is empty while the interaction state and the event queue are, and holds them otherwise", "UiSystem: without a canvas the interaction state stays empty whatever the input", "PlaySession: a scene without a canvas keeps its state hash while a gamepad stick is held" (live), and every committed replay at its hash through the determinism and games suites at the milestone end.
- **The Ui replay is identical in all four runs and fails without its input:** FeatureTest's `Ui.replay` (2011) through the determinism suite and its input check.
- **The interaction** (R-UI-INPUT, R-UI-WIDGETS, R-UI-SCROLL): "UiInteraction: the topmost pointer target over a point reaches its nearest widget", "UiSystem: hover follows the pointer, and a press and release over a button clicks it", "UiSystem: the pointer drags a slider and a scroll view, and the wheel scrolls the scroll view under it", "UiSystem: interaction state for an element that can no longer take it is cleared", "UiSystem: SetFocus focuses a focusable element and refuses any other", "UiWidgets: activating a button raises Click, and a toggle flips IsOn and raises ValueChanged", "UiWidgets: a slider reads its value clamped, follows the pointer and steps along its direction", "UiWidgets: a scroll view stays within its content's overflow and scrolls by the wheel, a drag and its steps", and the layout tables' exact values in "UiLayout: a slider's value places its Fill and Handle, and an off toggle hides its Graphic", "UiLayout: a scroll view clips its descendants and moves its content by the clamped scroll position" and "UiLayout: a widget part that is not an element below its widget in the same canvas is ignored" (live) (Debug and Release unit runs: UI layout tables byte-identical across configurations).
- **Events and the module** (R-UI-EVENTS): "PlaySession: UiInput follows ApplyInput and UiEvents follows StartFlush, and Simulate drops the UI events", "UiEvents: an event bubbles from its element per behaviour and stops after an entity where a callback returns true", "UiEvents: each callback receives its element and value in order, and an event whose element is gone is dropped", "Ui: the module reads focus, hover, press and rectangles, and SetFocus refuses an element that cannot take focus", and Python `test_ui.UiInteractionTests.test_a_click_through_play_step_input_reaches_on_ui_click_and_ui_tree_reports_the_state`.
- **Theme** (R-UI-THEME): "UiExtraction: a widget's image takes its canvas's tint for its state, and a text without a font takes its canvas's", and the `UiWidgets` golden.
- **`ui.tree`'s interaction fields** (R-UI-AUTO): "UiMethods: ui.tree reports each element's widget and the play session's focus, hover and press", the Python click test and `test_ui.UiInteractionTests.test_ui_tree_reports_the_interaction_in_the_runtime`.
- **Validation** (decision 35): "ProjectValidator: UI_WIDGET_PART_INVALID names each widget part that is not an element below its widget and stays quiet for valid parts" and the code's fixture.
- **Settings:** "ProjectSettings: the Ui section's binding lists hold at most 16 unique names, located at /Ui", and `AllSettings.eproj`'s coverage (gate 8).
- **Authoring** (R-UI-EDITOR): "UiAuthoringMethods: ui.createWidget builds each kind from its template in one undo step", "UiAuthoringMethods: ui.createWidget puts a widget without a canvas above it below a new canvas, and refuses a label on a kind without one", "UiAuthoring: anchor presets set the anchors and keep the rectangle unless asked to move it", "UiAuthoring: the rect tool moves an edge and keeps the opposite one in place", "UiAuthoring: the UI preview fits the game frame in the panel and selects the topmost element at a point", and Python `test_ui_authoring.UiAuthoringTests.test_create_widget_builds_each_kind_below_a_canvas_in_one_undo_step` and `test_the_ui_preview_option_round_trips_through_viewport_set_options`.
- **Coverage gates in every mode:** decision 36's scaffolds, through the feature and export suites at W22.2's integration; `ui.createWidget` by gate 5.

## Review of C22.2

One review of the frozen interfaces (ADR 0028 decision 11), read-only, of the staged contract against `3c0cc112`, with every new file read in full and every hunk with its context: 19 findings, 1 major, 13 minor and 5 nits, and one design point that was no finding. The major was verified against the code before it was fixed, and so were the others: each is confirmed and fixed in this checkpoint, none rejected. The review found sound, among others: the ledger positions (UiInput after ApplyInput with TextLatch reserved between them, UiEvents after StartFlush, the ui block after native and physics2d, the Ui section after Localization, the four widgets appended to M22's own Ui category, `UI_WIDGET_PART_INVALID` after M22's UI codes, IdSeeds 2010 to 2012 inside 2000 to 2099, no AssetType, built-in handle, render pass, `Shaders.json` program or GPU object); the single-owner files only called; no public header exposing a third-party type; the counts of decision 20; its reasons for leaving fields, the Ui section and the Ui module to their streams; and the skipped cases' expected values, checked by hand.

**The lead's decisions on the review** (binding):

1. The major (a held stick made a canvas-less scene's hash change) is fixed as the reviewer proposed: the UI interaction state stays empty, so no ui block is appended, while the scene has no canvas, and the frozen documents say exactly that, with a contract test that a canvas-less scene with a held stick keeps its block absent (live, since it holds on the contract tree).
2. The reviewer's design point (with the default bindings an arrow key focused the first HUD button and Space then clicked it, so a game whose play uses the arrows and Space, Tetris among them, would have its HUD take its input) changes the frozen navigation rule now, not in M25: navigation commands move focus only from a widget that has focus, or, with nothing focused, to a canvas's new `DefaultFocus` (an entity reference, null by default); Submit acts only on the focused widget; a pointer press focuses only a Focusable widget; `Ui.SetFocus` stays the script's way in (decision 23, "Where navigation starts"). Checked against the Roadmap's M22 section and the plan's R-UI-* rows: no acceptance line asks for navigation without a starting focus, so the reading is recorded in decision 23 (a game opts in through `DefaultFocus`) and nothing goes to the Backlog.
3. The settings case checks the ledger's relative order, Ui right after Localization, so it holds whichever of M21 and M22 lands first.
4. Architecture §5.1's state-hash paragraph goes to stream A, which implements the ui block, in the ownership table.
5. Every other finding is fixed as proposed unless the code proves it wrong; none did.

**The design point** (decision 2 above): `UiCanvasComponent::DefaultFocus` is frozen in the header, registered by stream C after Theme with Font and Theme (decisions 20, 21 and 37; the registration changes FeatureTest's UI scenes, decision 20's reason), read by stream A; `UiInteraction.h`'s focus paragraph and `FindUiNavigationTarget`'s and `FindNextUiFocus`'s comments give the default focus (the `DefaultFocus` of the last canvas in draw order whose `DefaultFocus` is focusable) where they gave the first focusable element; `UiSystem.h`'s steps 3, 8 and 9 say that a press focuses only a focusable widget, that navigation without a focus goes only to the default focus and that Submit needs a focus; `UiButtonComponent.h`'s Focusable and the four widgets' registered Focusable description say so (regenerated into `Components.md`, the Scene and Prefab schemas, `ScriptAPI.md` and `Engine.d.luau`). The skipped cases' expectations follow: "UiSystem: every widget works by keyboard and gamepad" and "UiSystem: interaction state for an element that can no longer take it is cleared" start from the canvas's `DefaultFocus`; the directional and Next and Previous cases expect nothing without a focus, then the `DefaultFocus`, a menu canvas's over the first, a not focusable one passed over; the bindings case starts from it; and the new skipped case "UiSystem: with nothing focused, navigation focuses only a canvas's DefaultFocus, and a press focuses only a focusable widget" checks that the commands and Submit do nothing without one, that a click on a HUD button that is not Focusable clicks it without focus, and that the first command then focuses the default. "BuiltinComponents: ... UI defaults follow the component table" checks the null default. The skills hold no navigation text yet: stream E's row now names the rule for the ui page (a canvas's `DefaultFocus` or `Ui.SetFocus`, HUD buttons left not Focusable).

**Major**

- **M1. A held gamepad stick made the ui block non-empty in a scene without a canvas** (`Scene/UiSystem.h`). Confirmed: UiInput ran with or without a canvas, the session fills the stick whenever Navigation and StickNavigation are on (their defaults), step 7 stored the stick's direction unconditionally and `IsEmpty` compares every member, so FeatureTest's `Input.replay` (no canvas, LeftY at 0.75 from tick 14 to 18) would have hashed the block at ticks 14 to 17, against decision 29, three frozen comments, Architecture §5.1 and Roadmap M22's "scenes without UI keep their hash"; a gamepad game whose checkpoint fell while the stick was held would have changed its hash. Fixed (the lead's decision 1): decision 24's "Without a canvas" rule and `UiSystem.h`'s step 1: when the layout holds no canvas, the stale-state step clears the ids (raising Focus or Hover false at an entity the scene still holds), the rest of the state returns to its default and the phase ends without reading input. Decision 29, `UiSystem.h`'s `AppendStateHash`, `UiInteraction.h`'s state comment, `PlaySession.h`'s UiInput row and its `GetUi` and `ComputeStateHash` comments, and Architecture §5.7's step 1b say exactly that; the related wording defect is fixed too: a scene with UI may hold state without input (the pointer rests at (0, 0) over whatever target lies there), so no document says that such a scene appends nothing. New cases: "UiSystem: without a canvas the interaction state stays empty whatever the input" (skipped: its second half, a canvas removed under a focused and hovered button, needs stream A's `SetFocus` and `ProcessInput`) and "PlaySession: a scene without a canvas keeps its state hash while a gamepad stick is held" (live: no session runs UiInput on the contract tree, so it passes now and guards stream A's implementation; the fix changes frozen text only, so there is no code without the fix to fail against).

**Minor**

- **m1. The event queue crossed tick boundaries unhashed** (`Scene/UiSystem.h`). Confirmed: `Ui.SetFocus` called after the tick's UiEvents phase (from `OnFixedUpdate`, a task, `OnUpdate`, `OnLateUpdate` or an `OnUi*` callback) leaves its events queued at the tick boundary, so a focus set and cleared again at tick 59 hashed like no call at tick 60 although tick 60's callbacks differ. Fixed by the reviewer's first option: the ui block holds the queue after the state (its count, then each event's Kind, Target, Flag, HasNumber, Number, Vector.x and Vector.y), and is empty only while the state and the queue both are (decision 29, `UiSystem.h`, `PlaySession.h`). The case, renamed "UiSystem: the ui block is empty while the interaction state and the event queue are, and holds them otherwise", now takes the hover's event first and adds a focus set and cleared (an empty state with two queued events, hashed, then taken); the PlaySession case is renamed "PlaySession: the state hash holds the ui block only while the UI interaction state or event queue is not empty".
- **m2. DragOrigin and DragStart were never reset** (`Scene/UiSystem.h`). Confirmed: steps 1 and 5 cleared only Dragged and Pressed, so after any drag the state never became empty again, and a slider's DragOrigin was unspecified. Fixed: a slider's press sets DragOrigin to the pointer and DragStart to zero, and whenever Dragged is cleared both return to zero (`UiSystem.h`'s steps 3 and 5, `UiInteraction.h`'s members, decision 24's "Drags"); the new drag-and-wheel case (m13) releases both drags, moves the pointer away and expects `IsEmpty`.
- **m3. A WholeNumbers slider read outside its range, and a Step below 1 never moved it** (`UiSliderComponent.h`). Confirmed both: rounding after clamping read 11 for a Value of 10.5 in [0.5, 10.5] (a position of 1.05), and "at least 1" applied to Step 0 only, so Step 0.4 was rounded away. Fixed: with WholeNumbers the value as read is rounded, then kept to the range's whole numbers ([ceil(Min), floor(Max)], or Min when the range holds none), and the step is rounded to a whole number of at least 1 whatever Step says (`UiSliderComponent.h`, `UiWidgets.h`, the Step's registered description, regenerated, and decision 21). The slider case gains Value 10.5 and 0.5 in [0.5, 10.5] (10 at position 0.95, and 1), a range without a whole number (its Min), and Steps of 0.4, 0.5 and 2.6.
- **m4. Decision 27 and `ScriptEngine.h` disagreed on an event whose element is pending destruction.** Confirmed. Fixed with the header's rule, which matches step 1's: an event whose element the scene no longer holds, or holds pending destruction, when its turn comes is dropped, stated alike in `ScriptEngine.h` and decision 27. The decision's "passed over" survives for the one case it can reach: an ancestor that a callback of the same event destroyed, since a pending entity's subtree is pending with it, so a live element's ancestors are live when its walk starts. The dispatch case gains a pending element (its event dropped) and a click whose callback destroys its element's parent: the walk passes the parent over and reaches the root, and the hover queued behind the click is dropped.
- **m5. An anchor preset with SetPosition collapsed an element stretched on that axis** (`EditorCore/Ui/UiAuthoring.h`). Confirmed: the axis kept its Size, an inset. Fixed: an axis that the preset does not stretch takes the element's laid-out size before the change, max(0, parentSize × (AnchorMax − AnchorMin) + Size), with SetPosition or without (`UiAuthoring.h`, decision 34); the case gains a header stretched with an inset of 16 in a 400-unit parent, moved to the centre: 384 wide.
- **m6. `ui.createWidget` below a parent outside any canvas built UI that never lays out, and `index` without `parent` was unspecified** (`EditorCore/Automation/UiAuthoringMethods.h`). Confirmed. Fixed: a kind other than Canvas below a parent without a UiCanvas at or above it goes below a new canvas created below the parent in the same command, and without `parent`, `index` is the created root's place among the scene's roots, refused past them at `/index` (the header, the `parent` and `index` registered descriptions, regenerated into `Methods.json`, `Automation.md` and the MCP catalogue, and decision 32). The C++ case, renamed "UiAuthoringMethods: ui.createWidget puts a widget without a canvas above it below a new canvas, and refuses a label on a kind without one", gains a plain parent, `index` 0 and `index` 99.
- **m7. The Python undo test undid one command too many** (`Tests/Automation/test_ui_authoring.py`). Confirmed: seven commands, eight undos, and `edit.undo` reports fewer undone instead of failing. Fixed: one undo and five more, each reporting `undone` 1, leave only the first command's Canvas, Button and Label, and a seventh removes all three; the C++ root case also undoes the parentless button once and finds the entity count back at its start.
- **m8. The theme case never tested its precedence** (`UiExtractionTests.cpp`). Confirmed: each state was on a different element. Fixed: one widget pressed, focused and hovered at once takes Pressed, focused and hovered takes Focused, and a disabled one in all three states takes Disabled.
- **m9. `UI_WIDGET_PART_INVALID` and the layout's "an invalid part is ignored" had no case.** Confirmed. Fixed: new cases, the skipped "ProjectValidator: UI_WIDGET_PART_INVALID names each widget part that is not an element below its widget and stays quiet for valid parts" (a Graphic in another canvas, a Fill that is the slider's parent element and a Handle that is no element, each named once at its widget's entity, component and field; children as parts and a null Content quiet) and "UiLayout: a widget part that is not an element below its widget in the same canvas is ignored" (a sibling as Fill and Graphic, a Handle in another canvas, a toggle that is its own Graphic and a scroll view whose Content is its parent element keep their placement and stay shown), both in stream B's tests. The layout case is live: forced on the contract tree it passed, since the layout applies no widget rule yet, so it guards streams B and C instead of waiting for them (the lead's rule for the major's test, applied alike).
- **m10. The Ui settings case required Ui to be the last section.** Confirmed: the ledger appends M21's sections after M22's when M22 lands first. Fixed (the lead's decision 3): Ui is the field right after Localization.
- **m11. Decision 36 claimed `test_ui.py` covered the Runtime's `ui.tree` interaction fields.** Confirmed. Fixed: new skipped Python test `test_ui.UiInteractionTests.test_ui_tree_reports_the_interaction_in_the_runtime` (stream A's): the exported Runtime holds a button pressed through `input.inject` and `play.step`, and its `ui.tree` reports it a Button, interactable, hovered, focused and pressed, then released; decision 36 and the acceptance name it.
- **m12. Architecture §5.1's state-hash paragraph had no owner.** Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 4): stream A's documents in the W22.2 ownership table include it (the ui block after physics2d, what it holds and when it is empty, and that the hash now covers the UI interaction).
- **m13. ProcessInput's wheel and drag steps and the session's input frame had no case.** Confirmed. Fixed: new skipped case "UiSystem: the pointer drags a slider and a scroll view, and the wheel scrolls the scroll view under it" (a wheel step down scrolls down by ScrollStep and one up back, through the content's image to its scroll view; a press on the content drags the scroll view from its DragOrigin; a press on a slider sets and drags its value past its end; neither widget focusable, so the state ends empty); the bindings case now drives Shift+N as Previous and gamepad 1's DPadDown and South (Submit flipping a toggle), from the canvas's `DefaultFocus`, its taps two ticks apart.

**Nits**

- **n1. W-CLOCK's comment headed the new UiInput case** (`PlaySessionTests.cpp`). Confirmed. Fixed: it is back above "PlaySession: the frame preview is on in live play and off in lockstep".
- **n2. A `UiAuthoringMethodsTests.cpp` comment named the wrong widgets.** Confirmed. Fixed: undoing the last four leaves the button's, the toggle's and the slider's entities.
- **n3. Decision 25's "no per-widget override" limit was missing from Backlog §6.** Confirmed. Fixed: the widgets line adds one theme per canvas.
- **n4. `UiToggleComponent.h` named `UiLayoutNode::Visible` for the hidden Graphic.** Confirmed. Fixed: Hidden (`UiLayoutNode::Hidden`): laid out, neither drawn, hit nor focusable.
- **n5. The Ui module's case was not in the binding's test file.** Confirmed. Fixed: it moved to `Tests/Source/Engine/Scripting/Bindings/UiTests.cpp`, which stream B's tests list; `UiEventTests.cpp` keeps the dispatch cases.

The edited Python tests are contract-skipped and pass `Lint.py`'s Python check; they run when their streams un-skip them.

W22.2's five stream branches were merged onto `milestone/m22` at `fd06249d` (C22.2, `b24f6ddf`, with main `48d68e51` merged in: ADR 0039's CPU-time budgets and decision 6, ADR 0040's session-file sharing and 300 s launch bound, the logarithmic `Test.Case` registration, the ImGui atlas border sampler with its three regenerated editor goldens and the ledger amendment for M21) in the order A (`446318cb`), B (`0c865a30`), C (`baf46dcf`), D (`01c5f12b`), E (`f2aab7dd`), then staged as one change. Stream E had built its scaffolds on a local scratch merge of A to D; its resolutions are the ones below, and its regenerated FeatureTest is what `Scaffold.py` reproduces here.

- **Textual conflicts:**
  - `PlaySessionTests.cpp`'s "step order matches the documented sequence" (A and B): the comment names both phases (UiInput after ApplyInput, UiEvents after StartFlush) and the fixed step's slice is `expected.begin() + 14` (A and B each had 13). The Simulate case and `NativeWorldTests.cpp`'s NativeStep phase list merged without a conflict, both phases in the ledger's order.
  - `Scene/UiLayout.cpp` (B and C): the union of the includes (UiScrollView, UiSlider and UiToggle), B's `FindUiWidgetPartRules`, then C's `GetUiScrollOffset`; `LayoutUiCanvas` merged without a conflict (the part rules, the scroll offset with its snap, Hidden, and the scroll view's clip whatever ClipChildren says).
  - `Scene/UiWidgets.cpp` (B and C, the whole file): the union of the includes, B's file comment with C's sentence, B's `Utils` block and Button, Toggle and Slider functions, then C's ScrollView section unchanged.
  - D and E merged without a conflict; the `UI_WIDGET_PART_INVALID` row of the game-debugging skill's `validation-codes` page, which B and E both added with the same text, merged into one row.
- **Un-skipped** (each needed two or three streams; decision 39's list, amended above): "UiSystem: hover follows the pointer, and a press and release over a button clicks it", "UiSystem: every widget works by keyboard and gamepad", "UiSystem: with nothing focused, navigation focuses only a canvas's DefaultFocus, and a press focuses only a focusable widget", "UiSystem: the pointer drags a slider and a scroll view, and the wheel scrolls the scroll view under it", "PlaySession: UiInput follows ApplyInput and UiEvents follows StartFlush, and Simulate drops the UI events", "PlaySession: the state hash holds the ui block only while the UI interaction state or event queue is not empty", "PlaySession: the Ui settings' bindings drive navigation, and a binding that is not a key or gamepad button fails the start", "Ui: the module reads focus, hover, press and rectangles, and SetFocus refuses an element that cannot take focus", and the Python `test_ui.UiInteractionTests.test_a_click_through_play_step_input_reaches_on_ui_click_and_ui_tree_reports_the_state` and `test_ui_authoring.UiAuthoringTests.test_create_widget_builds_each_kind_below_a_canvas_in_one_undo_step`. Every C22.2 stub is replaced and no skip of the contract remains (`Lint.py` strict).

New guard case "UiSystem: a scroll view whose Content is not below it starts its drag at zero and scrolls nothing" (a sibling as Content, ScrollPosition 30: DragStart zero, no Scroll from the drag or the wheel, the position kept); it holds with A's helper too, so it guards the shared rule rather than a fix.

## Review of W22.2

One review pass of the integrated wave diff (ADR 0028 decision 11), read-only, of the staged change against `fd06249d`, in three lenses (correctness and robustness; determinism, formats and integration; tests, parity and documentation), each reading every new file in full and every hunk with its context: 21 findings, 16 once the lenses' duplicates are merged (both majors' first finding was raised by two lenses, the scroll reveal by two and `UiMethods.cpp`'s reader by all three), 2 major, 10 minor and 4 nits. Both majors were verified against the code before they were fixed, and so was every other finding: each is confirmed and fixed in this checkpoint, none rejected (m10's proposed Backlog line gives way to the lead's decision 5, which fixes the finding otherwise). The lenses found sound, among others: the stale-state step and the canvas-less early return; the release and press rule of one step; drag origins and starts reset; Cancel's last-canvas fallback; the stick's selection; Shift+Next as Previous; the binding parse at the session's start with located Validation issues; the ui hash block's marker, fields and order after native; the slider's and scroll view's reads (NaN, inverted ranges, WholeNumbers); the widget parts' rules; the snapshot walk and the boolean-`true` stop of the dispatch; `InvokeUi`'s eligibility and failure publishing; `ui.createWidget`'s atomicity; the overlay's drag arithmetic; no new GPU object; the simulation path's IEEE-exact operations in canonical order; the phase positions; the formats (no version change), the registries' appends, the IdSeeds 2010 to 2012 and the one `Suites.jsonl` line; `Ui.replay`'s header and hash; and that no frozen header changed beyond the lead's comment in `ScriptEngine.h`.

**The lead's decisions on the review** (binding):

1. The major (M1, both the correctness and the determinism lens): the scene's UI is laid out before its startup callbacks as well, keeping the layout after them, in `Activate` and in `ConsumeRequests`' load and reload paths, so the Ui module never reads an empty or a replaced scene's layout (decision 3), with regression tests that fail before the fix: `Ui.SetFocus` in `OnStart` focuses the menu's first button, and after a `Scene.Reload` `Ui.GetRect` in `OnStart` returns the new scene's rectangle.
2. A contract change (m1): `Ui.SetFocus` checks and reveals against a layout that is current at the call, laying the UI out first (layout is a pure function of the scene, so determinism holds), which also fixes the reveal's overshoot and its stale layout (m2): the reveal measures from the fresh layout and applies its shift to the scroll position that layout used. The decisions and headers that state the old rule are amended and the refusal's message corrected, with a regression test for each scenario (a menu shown and focused in one callback; a wheel scroll then `Ui.SetFocus` with no overshoot; a ScrollPosition reset then `Ui.SetFocus` keeping the item in view).
3. The tests major (M2): a test drives a play session (hover, press and focus) and checks that the tints reach the session's game-view extraction and a play-scene screenshot's (`ExtractView`), so dropping that wiring at a merge fails a test.
4. The determinism minor (m3): the UI preview's logic in `SceneViewportPanel.cpp`, an M21-owned file, moves into M22's own files, the panel keeping one-line calls, as the ledger requires; decision 33 lists the remaining call sites for the later lander.
5. `Test.Inject*` cannot send the wheel or a pointer move (m10): no Backlog limit. C22.3 adds `Test.InjectPointerMove` and `Test.InjectWheel`, with their coverage, so a game with a slider or a scroll list records its UI replay from a recording suite; until then the game-building skill's `replays` page says that such a replay is recorded with `input_record` (decision 36). The lead passes this to C22.3.
6. Every other finding (minors and nits of all three lenses) is fixed as proposed unless the code proves it wrong (only m8's read-only variant was, below), the game-building `SKILL.md`'s hash bullet, `ReplayData.h`'s hash text, `UiMethods.cpp`'s content size and the long lines (CodeStyle §3.5's soft limit and the comments' width) included.
7. Main is not merged in this step.

**Major**

- **M1. The Ui module read an empty or the replaced scene's layout during a scene's startup callbacks** (`Session/PlaySession.cpp`; correctness and determinism). Confirmed: `Activate` ran `StartScripts` (`OnCreate`, then `OnStart`) before its `LayoutStep`, and `ConsumeRequests` ran `ResetInteraction`, `StartScripts`, then `LayoutStep`; W22.2's Ui module reads `UiSystem::GetLayout` from exactly those callbacks, so `Ui.SetFocus` in a start scene's `OnStart` raised ("not laid out"), fault-disabling the menu's instance (§11.7), `Ui.GetRect` returned nil there, and after a `Scene.Reload` (same entity ids) both read the replaced scene's rectangles, against decision 3 and `UiSystem.h`. Fixed (the lead's decision 1): `Activate` lays the scene out before `StartScripts` and again after it, and `ConsumeRequests` lays the new scene out right after `ResetInteraction`, before its scripts start, keeping the layout after its startup; layouts are never hashed, so no committed hash moves. Decision 3, `UiSystem.h`'s file comment, `PlaySession.h` (`Activate`, `GetUi`) and Architecture §5.7 say so. New case "Ui: a scene's startup callbacks read its own layout and take focus, at the session's start and after a Scene.Reload": a menu canvas's behaviour focuses its Play button and records its rectangle in `OnStart`; at the session's start the focus is set, no script error is raised, the rectangle is (10, 20) and `OnUiFocus` hears the Focus event at the first UiEvents phase; with the button moved to (200, 100) in the running scene and the scene reloaded, `OnStart` reads (10, 20) again and takes focus. Without the fix it failed (at the start: no focus, the `OnStart` fault and no trace; after the reload: the replaced scene's (200, 100)).

- **M2. The session's wiring of its interaction state into its game view and `ExtractView` had no test** (`PlaySession.cpp`; tests). Confirmed: only hand-built requests reached `AppendUiDrawItems`, the UiWidgets golden renders the edit scene at rest, and nothing observed a non-Normal tint through a `PlaySession`. Fixed (the lead's decision 3): new case "PlaySession: the game view and ExtractView tint the widgets by the session's interaction state": with a canvas theme of distinct tints, the pointer away, then over button A, then pressing it, then released away (A keeps the focus the press gave it), the session's last game-view extraction and an `ExtractView` of the play scene (what `viewport.screenshot {target: play}` captures) both give A's image colour times Normal, Hovered, Pressed, then Focused, and B's times Normal throughout; dropping either `.UiInteraction` assignment would leave A at Normal (an extraction without the session's state draws Normal, decision 25) and fail it.

**Minor**

- **m1. `Ui.SetFocus` on a widget that the same callback had enabled or created was refused, with a message that was false at the call** (`Scene/UiSystem.cpp`; correctness). Confirmed: `SetFocus` checked focusability in `GetLayout()`, a snapshot of the last step or frame, and the skills recommend calling it when a menu opens. Fixed (the lead's decision 2, a contract change recorded in decision 28's amendment): `UiSystem::SetFocus` checks and reveals against `ComputeUiLayout(scene, GetLayout().SceneSize)`, a layout of the scene as it is at the call, which it does not store (UiInput still hit-tests, and `Ui.GetRect` still reads, the current layout: what was drawn); the new const `UiSystem::CheckFocus` answers the same check without a change, and the Ui module asks it before `PrepareHostMutation`, so decision 28's no-mutation rule keeps one check. The refusal names why at the call, which is now true when it is raised ("has no area" says "a zero width or height"). `UiSystem.h`, decision 28, Architecture §11.5's Ui row and the luau-gameplay skill's UI section say so. Before the first layout, which gives no scene size, nothing is laid out and `SetFocus` refuses (`ComputeUiLayout` needs a scene resolution of at least 1 × 1; a session lays its scene out before any script runs, M1). New cases "UiSystem: SetFocus focuses a menu a callback has just shown or created, before any layout" (a pause canvas enabled, then a button created, each focused before any layout; the current layout still without them) and "Ui: SetFocus focuses a menu the same script has just shown" (through the module: refused before `SetActive(true)`, focused after it in the same evaluation, `Ui.GetRect` still nil until the next layout); "UiSystem: SetFocus focuses a focusable element and refuses any other" also refuses an element before the first layout. Without the fix both new cases failed (refused as not laid out).
- **m2. The focus reveal measured from the layout's rectangles but moved from the current ScrollPosition, so a reveal after a scroll in the same step over-scrolled or left the element hidden** (`Scene/UiWidgets.cpp`; correctness and tests). Confirmed both scenarios (a wheel, then a reveal of an item already in view, scrolled 20 more and clipped half of it; a ScrollPosition reset by a script, then a reveal, stopped at 100 with the item still below the view), and the same inside one UiInput (the wheel, then a navigation; two navigation commands). Fixed (the lead's decision 2): `SetFocus` reveals against its fresh layout, and UiInput's step 8 reveals each new focus against a layout of the scene as it is then (`ComputeUiLayout` at the table's scene size), so the shift applies to the position that layout used; `RevealInUiScrollViews`' comment (`UiWidgets.h`, the contract owner's change) states that its table is a layout of the scene as it is, `UiSystem.h`'s step 8 and decision 23's Reveal say so. New cases "UiSystem: SetFocus reveals from the scroll position the scene has at the call" (a wheel scroll to 80, then `SetFocus(Item2)` with no further scroll; a list laid out at 80 and reset to 0 by a script, then `SetFocus(Item6)` scrolling 180, the item at 60 to 100 after the next layout) and "UiSystem: navigation reveals the new focus from the scroll position of its own step": the wheel to 80 then Down in one UiInput reveals Item1 back at 40, and Next then Down in one UiInput reveal Item2 by 20, then Item3 by 40 more, to 60. Without the fix they failed (100, 100 with Item6 hidden, 80 and 80). Interaction never changed a committed replay's hash: the determinism suite verifies `Ui.replay` at `94b2457e1ed7567b` (below).
- **m3. `SceneViewportPanel.cpp`, an M21-owned file, held the UI preview's logic at five places, recorded as calls** (determinism). Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 4): `Editor/Ui/UiPreview.{h,cpp}` gain `DrawUiPreviewOption` (the Overlays popup's checkbox and tooltip) and `FitUiPreviewImage` (the letterboxed frame applied to the panel's rectangle); the panel keeps one-line calls and conditions, and its image is drawn and hovered over its rectangle (generic: the rectangle is the panel's without the preview), while the overlay's item is the first branch of the image item's choice, so the focus and the asset drop after it serve both. Decision 33's "The call sites" list the remaining touches of `SceneViewportPanel.{h,cpp}`, `EditorHostViewports.cpp`, `InspectorPanel.cpp` and `SceneHierarchyPanel.{h,cpp}` for the later lander. No behaviour changes: the panel's preview case passes unchanged, with the new m9 case.
- **m4. The game-building skill's "What the state hash covers" said that the hash never covers input state, and `ReplayData.h`'s paragraph lacked the native and ui blocks** (determinism). Confirmed. Fixed: the skill's bullet adds a native module's state and, in a scene with a `UiCanvas`, the UI interaction state with the queued UI events (empty without a canvas), and says that a pointer moved over a widget can change the hash; `ReplayData.h` names the native block (M19) and the ui block (M22). The skill stays inline (`DocExamples`).
- **m5. `ui.tree`'s new widget forms `UiSlider(<value>)`, `UiScrollView(<x>,<y>)` and `UiToggle(off)` were untested** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: "UiMethods: ui.tree reports each element's widget and the play session's focus, hover and press" gains an off toggle, a WholeNumbers slider whose stored 12.4 lies above its Max of 10 (`UiSlider(10)`) and a scroll view whose stored position 300 exceeds its content's overflow of 100 (`UiScrollView(0,100)`), with their `widget` kinds.
- **m6. Stream A's two accepted rules (decision 24) had no test** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: new case "UiSystem: a tap within a step clicks, a held press outlives its step's release edge, and a destroyed element hears nothing": a press and a release in one step with the pointer up give Hover, Focus and Click; a press held, then a step with both edges and the pointer down keeps it pressed with no Click until the release; a focused and hovered button destroyed in a runtime scene (pending until the flush) leaves the layout, and the next UiInput drops it with no Focus or Hover event and an empty state.
- **m7. Hidden elements were never tested as not hit, focusable, `SetFocus`-able or picked** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: new case "UiInteraction: an element hidden in an off toggle's Graphic is neither hit nor focusable" (a button inside an off toggle's Graphic is passed over by Next, Previous and Down, is not focusable and leaves the hit to the toggle below; with the toggle on, it takes part again); "UiSystem: SetFocus focuses a focusable element and refuses any other" refuses that hidden button and a button without an area too, and checks `CheckFocus` alike; "UiAuthoring: the UI preview fits the game frame in the panel and selects the topmost element at a point" picks the toggle under its hidden Graphic, a scroll view's content inside its view, and the panel below the content's clipped part.
- **m8. The Ui module's two accepted rules (decision 28) were untested** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: new cases "Ui: SetFocus reports a mutation only when focus changes, and the reads return nil for an element pending destruction" (an evaluation that focuses reports once, one with the current focus, nil while nothing is focused or a refused element reports nothing; a focused, hovered and pressed button that a script destroys reads nil from `GetFocus`, `GetHovered` and `GetPressed` while the state still names it) and "Ui: a read-only evaluation reads the UI and refuses every SetFocus". The reviewer's read-only variant does not hold as proposed: `Ui.SetFocus` is registered as mutating (decision 28's table), so a read-only VM refuses every call before the binding runs, the current focus and nil included; the case states that rule (the reads work; every call raises; nothing changes, is queued or is reported), and the no-mutation rule is the reports of a writable evaluation, which the first case checks.
- **m9. The UI authoring overlay's cancel paths, body drag and `ifRevision` refusal were untested** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: new case "SceneViewportPanel: the UI preview moves the selected element with a body drag, and a click, Escape or a changed scene writes nothing": a click on the selected element's body keeps it selected with no undo step; a body drag of (30, 20) scene pixels moves its Position by (30, 20) canvas units, within the panel pixel ImGui rounds the pointer to, in one undo step, its size kept; an edge drag cancelled by Escape writes nothing; and an edge drag during which another edit changes the scene is refused at the release (`Conflict`, from its `ifRevision`), only that edit recorded.
- **m10. The accepted limit that `Test.Inject*` cannot drive a UI drag or the wheel was in neither the Backlog nor the replays skill** (tests). Confirmed as a gap in the skills. Fixed by the lead's decision 5 instead of the proposed Backlog line: it is no accepted limit, since C22.3 adds `Test.InjectPointerMove` and `Test.InjectWheel` (decision 36 says so, and the lead passes it to C22.3); the game-building skill's `replays` page says that a UI replay that drags or scrolls is recorded with `input_record` and planned `play_step` input until then, and that a recording suite still drives clicks and navigation (`Test.InjectMouse`, `Test.InjectKey`, `Test.InjectGamepad`).

**Nits**

- **n1. `UiMethods.cpp`'s scroll-position reader took a zero content size, not the rule the integration recorded as shared** (all three lenses). Confirmed (no output differed: both leave no overflow). Fixed: `ReadUiOutlineScrollPosition` takes the view's size for a Content that is not below the scroll view, as `UiSystem.cpp`'s and `UiWidgets.cpp`'s readers do, and the Integration (W22.2) section's "Not done" bullet says when the three were aligned. No regression test: the change alters no output.
- **n2. `ProjectValidator.h`'s file comment had a 171-column line** (determinism and tests), **and new lines ran past CodeStyle §3.5's soft limit** (the determinism lens's note, the lead's decision 6). Confirmed. Fixed: the paragraph is re-wrapped at the comments' width, and the wave's added lines past about 150 columns (`UiAuthoringMethods.cpp`'s templates, `PlaySession.cpp`'s binding issues, `UiLayout.cpp`'s part rules, the binding's hints and the tests' long statements among them) are wrapped by hand as §3.5 says, a long designated-initializer list one designator per line, the form clang-format keeps (it joins a list broken anywhere else back into one line, as `Format.py` showed); test names stay whole, and one test line that repeats its file's existing idiom ten times over (`EditorHostViewportsTests.cpp`'s cube mesh) is left as its neighbours are. `Format.py --check` passes.
- **n3. `UiWidgetsTests.cpp`'s file comment said that the ScrollView cases stay skipped** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: it names the streams' cases, as the other files' comments do.
- **n4. The Status line did not record W22.2's integration** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: the Status line says that W22.2's integration and its review are committed as one checkpoint, and the Date line names them.

**Runs** (a checkpoint's targeted runs, strict, ADR 0028 decision 11): `Build.py --jobs 12` of Debug, Release and Dist (18, 18 and 15 projects, 0 warnings); the regression cases alone without their fixes, each in a Tests build with only its fix reverted: M1's case failed (at the start no focus, the `OnStart` fault and no trace; after the reload the replaced scene's (200, 100)), and decision 2's four cases failed (the menu and the script's menu refused as not laid out; the reveal at 100 instead of 80 and Item6 left at 140 to 180; the navigation reveals at 80 instead of 40 and 60), then all passed with the fixes; `Format.py` on the eleven files the wrapping left to it, then `Format.py --check` (passed); `Lint.py` strict (0 findings, the 36 child-process targets' skips only); `GenerateDocs.py --check --config Debug` (21 references verified: the fixes change no registered text, so nothing was regenerated); `Test.py --suite unit --config Debug --junit` (4,301 cases passed, the 36 child-process targets skipped); `Test.py --suite unit --config Release` on the wave's cases (391 passed); then, each with `--require-gpu`: gpu Debug on `UiPass*`, `EditorHostViewports*`, `RenderExtraction*` and `SceneViewportPanel*` under both API caps (24 cases each), golden Release (27 matched on nvidia-61x, `UiWidgets` and the editor-UI goldens included), feature Debug and Release (235 cases and 65 suites and replays, the six editor-mode gates green, FeatureTest unchanged), export Release and Dist (60 suites and 5 replays in each mode, the six gates green in each, the coverage merge green), determinism Debug, Release and Dist (FeatureTest's 5 replays at their committed hashes in the four runs, `Ui.replay` at `94b2457e1ed7567b`, each failing without its input), automation (761 tests, method coverage 101/101, `Scaffold.py --check` included) and games Debug, Release and Dist (Tetris, Breakout and NativeTest: the audit, both editor halves and the Dist half, every replay at its committed hash and failing without its input), all passed.

## Waves, streams and file ownership (W22.3)

W22.3 starts at C22.3; its six streams run in parallel (plan §3.5: T text input 1.5 sessions, F fallback fonts and CJK 1, S shaping and bidi 1.5, W world-space canvases 1, E goldens and replays 0.5; and I, the injections W22.2's review owed, 0.5), E last, on a scratch merge of the other five (the lead's note 5). Streams own disjoint files; files shared by function say which function. A stream that needs a change in another's file lists it in its result. The shared integration files (`Docs/Reference/*`, `Resources/Scripting/Engine.d.luau`, `Tools/MCP/catalog.json`, `RegisterMethods.cpp`, `RegisterBindings.cpp`, `BuiltinComponents.h`, the premake files, `Scripts/ModuleRules.json`) are the lead's at W22.3's integration; each stream lists the regeneration its change needs.
