## Acceptance (W22.3)

Roadmap M22's acceptance lines that W22.3 owns, with the cases that state them (the contract's skipped cases; Python tests skipped with "contract stub"):

- **Shaped text is byte-identical across configurations; a missing glyph falls back through the chain:** "TextMeasurement: Arabic is shaped into its joined forms and laid out right to left", "TextMeasurement: a mixed-direction line is reordered by the bidirectional algorithm in either base direction, and Alignment places it" (renamed by the review of C22.3, which added its alignment checks), "TextMeasurement: shaped Latin, Arabic, Hebrew, mixed-direction and fallback CJK text measures to the same bytes in every configuration" (the review of C22.3), "TextMeasurement: the caret of a byte offset is its cluster's leading edge in visual order", "FontData: a format 2 payload round-trips glyph indices, indexed glyphs, the shaping source and fallbacks", "FontImporter: version 5 bakes the glyphs shaping reaches, their indices and the font's shaping subset", "FontImporter: a font's Fallbacks are cooked in order and recorded as its dependencies, and invalid ones are refused under /Fallbacks", "UiPass: a text's font chain follows its fallbacks depth first, each font once, and leaves out a missing one" and "UiPass: a codepoint the text's font lacks falls back to the first font of its chain that has it" (exact values in the Debug and Release unit runs).
- **The CJK, Arabic and Hebrew goldens pass:** stream E's golden scaffold (2016) through the golden suite.
- **The TextInput and world-canvas replays are identical in all four runs and each fails without its input:** stream E's replays (2014, 2015) through the determinism suite and its input check; the text input's behaviour: "PlayInput: the text latch holds the tick's committed characters in the step view and the frame's since the last frame", "UiTextInput: typed characters insert at the caret up to MaxLength, and the editing keys remove and move by codepoint", "UiSystem: a focused text input takes the step's text and editing keys, and keyboard navigation pauses while it has focus", "PlaySession: TextLatch follows ApplyInput and precedes UiInput, in Play and Simulate", "UiLayout: a text input clips its descendants and hides its Placeholder while its Text is not empty", "UiExtraction: a text input's TextElement draws its Text, with the caret while the input is focused", "UiPass: a focused text input's caret stands before its byte and keeps inside the rectangle", "UiEvents: OnUiTextChanged and OnUiSubmit receive the input's text and bubble, and their names are reserved", "InputBindings: GetText reads the tick's committed characters in the step and the frame's in OnUpdate", "UiMethods: ui.tree reports a text input as TextInput with its text", "UiAuthoringMethods: ui.createWidget builds a text input with its text and placeholder elements" and Python `test_ui.UiInteractionTests.test_typed_text_reaches_on_ui_text_changed_and_ui_tree_reports_it`; the world canvases': "UiLayout: a World canvas lays out over its own size and carries its world matrix", "UiInteraction: the pointer's ray goes through the primary camera at its simulated pose", "UiInteraction: the pointer hits a World canvas through its ray, under the Screen canvases and nearest first", "UiSystem: a World canvas's button is hovered and clicked through the camera, and its slider drags along its plane", "RenderExtraction: World canvases draw in every world view at their render pose, Screen canvases only in game and camera views", "UiPass: a World canvas is depth-tested against the scene and drawn under the Screen canvases" (GPU), the loss test "UiPass: a device loss recreates the world canvases' pipeline and a World canvas draws the same image afterwards" (GPU) and "UiMethods: ui.tree reports a World canvas and its elements in the canvas's own pixels"; and the World-canvas rules of the review of C22.3: "RenderAnnotations: a World canvas's element is outlined and labelled where the camera projects it", "UiAuthoring: the UI preview picks no element of a World canvas", "SceneViewportPanel: the UI preview neither selects nor resizes an element of a World canvas" and "Ui: GetRect reports a World canvas's element in its canvas pixels, with its canvas".
- **Recording suites drive drags, the wheel and typing** (W22.2's review, decision 52): "TestBindings: InjectMouseMove, InjectScroll and InjectText queue their events for the next unapplied tick" and Python `test_script_tests.ScriptTestRunnerTests.test_a_recording_suite_moving_the_pointer_scrolling_and_typing_records_what_input_record_records`.
- **Scenes without UI keep their hash:** "PlaySession: a scene with Screen UI and no text input or World canvas keeps its state hash while text is typed" (live), and every committed replay at its hash through the determinism and games suites.
- **Coverage gates in every mode:** decision 55's scaffolds, through the feature and export suites at W22.3's integration.

## Review of C22.3

One review of the frozen interfaces (ADR 0028 decision 11), read-only, of the staged contract against `5a5da45b`, with every new file read in full and every non-vendored hunk with its context (upstream HarfBuzz and SheenBidi code was not reviewed; their pins and digests were recomputed from the staged blobs): 19 findings, 4 major, 13 minor and 2 nits. Each major was verified against the code before it was fixed, and so was every other finding: all 19 are confirmed and fixed in this checkpoint, none rejected. The review found sound, among others: the pins and digests (HarfBuzz's 366 files and 5,587,532 bytes, SheenBidi's 63 files and 609,024 bytes, their trees and licences); HarfBuzz's include closure (only its guarded `config.h` missing); `HB_NO_GETENV` and `HB_NO_SETLOCALE` read by no public header; SheenBidi built neither dllimport nor dllexport, its one global under `SB_CONFIG_LOG`; precise floating point for MSVC, clang-cl and GCC and Clang; the two fixtures' registration; the ABI families, private defines, Lint prefixes and `ModuleRules.json` entries, private to Renderer and AssetPipeline; no first-party warning setting changed; the notices rows; the ledger positions (TextLatch between ApplyInput and UiInput, FontData 2 and FontImporter 5, IdSeeds 2013 to 2017, the handles 0x01c2 to 0x01c4 left free, every registry appended); the 11 stubs and the skipped cases; the live hash guard; GPU recreation through SceneRendererPipelines with a loss case; no HarfBuzz or SheenBidi type in a public header; and the arithmetic of most skipped cases, checked by hand.

**The lead's decisions on the review** (binding):

1. M1 (`Input.GetText` could not reach the latched text): an optional script-host function that returns empty by default, as C22.2's `GetUi`, so no file outside the ledger's grants changes (`EditorMethodContext.cpp` keeps its one granted line).
2. M2 (the default features reach glyphs Inter's atlas lacks, and shaping moves default-font text): shape with HarfBuzz's default features (standard behaviour, which Arabic and Hebrew need); the atlas holds every glyph the font's ranges reach through them, so FontImporter 5 changes Inter's atlas deliberately; the frozen atlas test and risk 15 say so, and the stream that implements version 5 records the new atlas hash. Text measurements and the Text, Ui and UiWidgets goldens may move: W22.3's integration regenerates exactly the images that move, each reviewed and explained. Whether a replay hash can change is answered and frozen (decision 44: none can).
3. M3 (World canvases' pixels reach consumers of scene pixels): a rule for each (annotations, the editor's preview picking and overlay, `Ui.GetRect`), each given to stream W with a frozen case.
4. M4 (no cross-configuration identity case for shaped text): a frozen case shapes a fixed set of strings (Latin with ligatures and kerning, Arabic, Hebrew, mixed bidi, CJK through the fallback) and compares glyph ids, clusters and positions with a committed digest, so every configuration that runs it must match the same bytes.
5. HarfBuzz's global state (m6): find what HarfBuzz keeps process-wide and whether SheenBidi v3.0.0's state differs in kind; apply one rule to both, record HarfBuzz's in Architecture §3 rule 5 if it is acceptable, and reconsider v3.0.0 on the same rule, recording either way.
6. The LdrDepth amendment is granted on main (`1707cf47`): the five places that call it pending say so (m9).
7. TextLatch's phase case checks relative order, which holds whichever of M21 and M22 lands first (m5). Every other finding is fixed as proposed unless the code proves it wrong (none did), and the streams table gains the missing ownerships and dependencies (`SceneRendererOutput.cpp`'s Ui inputs and the pass's pipeline count; I's test needs T's widget; F's fallback case needs S's version 5), so stream E's scratch merge and the integration know the order (m10).

**Major**

- **M1. `Input.GetText` had no frozen path from the script bindings to the text latch** (`Scripting/ScriptHost.h`). Confirmed: the input bindings read only `IScriptHost::GetInput()` (the devices' `InputState`, which carries no text) and the frame state's phase; the frozen `PlayInput::GetText` was reachable only from the session, and stream T's row owned neither the host interface nor the hosts, so T would have had to change `EditScriptHost` in `EditorMethodContext.cpp` (one granted line in phase 2) or Platform's frozen `InputState`. Fixed (the lead's decision 1): `[[nodiscard]] virtual std::string_view GetText(InputPhase phase) const { return {}; }` in `IScriptHost`, implemented (empty), its comment stating that the play session's host returns its `PlayInput`'s and every other host reads no text; decision 46, decision 40's frozen list, the C22.3 interfaces table (a `ScriptHost.h` row) and stream T's row (`PlaySession.cpp`'s host override, `Bindings/Input.cpp` reading the host) say so; `InputTests.cpp`'s skipped case names the path. No live test: no binding reads it before stream T, whose skipped "InputBindings: GetText reads the tick's committed characters in the step and the frame's in OnUpdate" covers it.
- **M2. FontImporter 5's shaping closure adds glyphs to Inter, while the frozen case and risk 15 expected version 4's atlas** (`FontImporterTests.cpp`). Confirmed in the vendored HarfBuzz: `hb_ot_shape_glyphs_closure` collects every GSUB lookup of the shape plan (`hb-ot-shape.cc`), whose map holds `calt` as a global horizontal feature and adds `frac`, `numr` and `dnom`; the reviewer's approximate closure of Inter's GSUB from ASCII and Latin-1 found about 44 glyphs from `calt` and 35 from the fraction features. Version 5 would have failed the live "version 3 ..." case's whole-atlas hash and the frozen version 5 case's, and shaped every default-font UI text. Fixed (the lead's decision 2): decision 44's new bullet, decision 43, risk 15, `FontImporter.h`'s and `TextMeasurement.h`'s comments say that Inter's atlas changes deliberately, that default-font UI text is shaped, which goldens may move and that the integration regenerates exactly those, each reviewed and explained; and that no replay hash can change (UI layout is not hashed, text measurement is not on the simulation path, the Text component's layout is unshaped, and `Ui.replay`'s pointer hits follow element rectangles, which never follow content): every committed replay must verify at its hash, `Ui.replay` at `94b2457e1ed7567b` among them. The version 5 case now checks that Inter gains shaping-only glyphs, keeps its codepoint glyphs' fields and bakes the atlas hash stream S records (`InterTrueTypeShapingAtlasHash`); the live "FontImporter: version 3 rasterizes through the vendored stb_truetype.h with the glyph fields of version 2" (renamed from "... with the atlases of version 2") compares the codepoint glyphs' distance fields wherever they are packed (`HashGlyphFields`), so version 5 keeps it green without a new expectation; its two field digests were recorded in a run in which the whole atlases still hashed as version 2's (`0x66f83cc4b7cca3c8` and `0x1c5d3945b145bec0`), so they are version 2's fields.
- **M3. World canvases' elements, in canvas pixels, reached consumers of scene pixels that had no rule or owner** (`Scene/UiExtraction.h`). Confirmed: `AppendUiRenderAnnotations` copies every item's rectangle into scene-pixel annotations, `PickUiElement` and the UI preview's overlay draw and pick every node's rectangle as scene pixels, and `Ui.GetRect`'s registered description says scene pixels; no stream's row owned them. Fixed (the lead's decision 3), each rule in decision 51's new bullet and given to stream W with a frozen case: annotations bound the element's corners projected through its canvas's World matrix and the snapshot's camera, and none without a camera or behind it (`UiExtraction.h`; "RenderAnnotations: a World canvas's element is outlined and labelled where the camera projects it"); the UI preview leaves World canvases' elements out of its outlines, picking and rect tool, since the rect tool's scene-pixel drags have no meaning through a camera's projection (`UiAuthoring.h`'s `PickUiElement`; "UiAuthoring: the UI preview picks no element of a World canvas" and "SceneViewportPanel: the UI preview neither selects nor resizes an element of a World canvas"; Backlog §6); and `Ui.GetRect` reports canvas pixels and the element's canvas in a new `Canvas` member of `UiElementRect`, with decision 56's frozen text ("Ui: GetRect reports a World canvas's element in its canvas pixels, with its canvas"). W's row owns the four files and the four cases.
- **M4. No case pinned shaped text's bytes across configurations** (`TextMeasurementTests.cpp`). Confirmed: the frozen shaping cases checked relations only (visual orders, comparisons, flags), so a configuration-dependent advance or offset passed both unit runs, and the goldens run in Release only; the mixed case's name also claimed an alignment it did not check. Fixed (the lead's decision 4): the new skipped case "TextMeasurement: shaped Latin, Arabic, Hebrew, mixed-direction and fallback CJK text measures to the same bytes in every configuration" (streams S and F) bakes Inter and the three Noto fixtures, shapes five texts (Latin through Inter's default features, Arabic, Hebrew, a mixed-direction line and Japanese through the fallback chain) in both base directions, wrapped and centred at a size, spacing and width that round, and hashes every glyph's id (found through its font's `IndexedGlyphs`), position, atlas rectangle, font and byte, every cluster and every line into a digest stream S records from its Debug run, which the Release run must match; decision 43 names it. The mixed case, renamed "TextMeasurement: a mixed-direction line is reordered by the bidirectional algorithm in either base direction, and Alignment places it", now checks that Right ends a right-to-left line at the block's right edge and Left starts it at 0, both in visual order (alignment does not follow the direction).

**Minor**

- **m1. `RenderExtraction.h`'s frozen rule and decision 7 still said that the scene view draws no game UI.** Confirmed. Fixed: `RenderExtraction.h`'s M22 paragraph, `RenderSnapshot.h`'s `Ui` comment and decision 7 state decision 51's rule (World canvases in every view that draws the world, at their render pose; Screen canvases in the Primary and Entity views only), and the C22.3 interfaces table lists both headers (stream W).
- **m2. `ComputeUiPointerRay` ignored M21's PixelPerfectCamera projection.** Confirmed against `milestone/m21`'s `PixelPerfectCameraComponent.h` (orthographic, half height ReferenceHeight / (2 PixelsPerUnit), pixel-snapped). Fixed: the ray is defined as the inverse of the projection render extraction gives the primary camera's view, at its simulated pose (`UiInteraction.h`), and decision 50 and risk 11 record that the later lander of M21 and M22 routes it through PixelPerfectCamera's projection and snapped position, with a case.
- **m3. The caret's horizontal rule contradicted itself and never brought back a caret left of the rectangle** (`UiPass.h`). Confirmed: moving right cannot lower the block's left, and a right-aligned text wider than its field puts the caret at its start left of the rectangle. Fixed: the block moves by the least distance, left or right, that puts the caret's quad inside [Min.x, Max.x] (its left edge at Min.x when the quad is wider than the rectangle), in `UiPass.h` and decision 48; the caret case gains a right-aligned 50-pixel text in a 40-pixel field with the caret at 0 (caret and text at the field's left).
- **m4. An empty focused field's caret was placed vertically as a block of no height** (`UiPass.h`). Confirmed: an empty text measures no line and a height of 0, so Middle put the caret's top at the field's middle and End below it. Fixed: a caret item's block is aligned as at least one `LineHeight` high (`UiPass.h`, decision 48); the caret case gains an empty field at Middle (the caret over 55 to 65 in a 20-pixel field at 50) and at End (60 to 70).
- **m5. The TextLatch case pinned whole phase lists** (`PlaySessionTests.cpp`). Confirmed: M21's phases in every session would have failed it at the second lander's merge. Fixed (the lead's decision 7): it checks, in Play and Simulate, that TextLatch runs once, right after ApplyInput and right before UiInput, and that the step view holds the tick's text; stream T's row names the documented-sequence cases it extends (the two `PlaySessionTests.cpp` lists with their `begin() + 15` split, the Simulate list, the UiInput case's two lists, and `NativeWorldTests.cpp`'s NativeStep list).
- **m6. Decision 41 rejected SheenBidi v3.0.0 for process-level state HarfBuzz 15 also has, and Architecture §3 rule 5 was not amended.** Confirmed: HarfBuzz keeps lazily created statics, an interned language list, atomic lookup hints and a write sink for its null objects, none in §3 rule 5's list. Fixed (the lead's decision 5): §3 rule 5 states one rule for every vendored library (thread-safe, nothing the engine initializes, sets or tears down, and no result depends on it) and lists HarfBuzz's state under it with the reason (decision 41's new bullet, `Vendor/HarfBuzz/VENDOR.md`); v3.0.0's sources (its tag archive, downloaded from upstream for reading only, SHA-256 `86c56014034739ba39a24c23eb00323b0bf6f737354f665786015fca842af786`) show state of the same kind (one-time initializations, atomic reference counts, a pool of three scratch buffers held through a thread-local slot only while a paragraph or line is built, a default allocator only its API sets), so the rule rejects neither; reconsidered on that rule, v2.9.0 stays for what v3.0.0 adds and changes (its release notes list a text-editing API, an allocator abstraction, refactored queues and Unicode 17.0 data, and no fix to UAX #9 or the script locator; 75 source files against 47; a build that depends on the compiler's threading support), recorded in decision 41 and `Vendor/SheenBidi/VENDOR.md`. The review checklist's global-state item names the rule.
- **m7. The vendoring left Architecture, AGENTS.md and the review checklist wrong.** Confirmed. Fixed: Architecture §2.2's `CheckBuildConfig.py` paragraph lists the `HB_*` and `SB_CONFIG_*` families and HarfBuzz and SheenBidi in the precise floating-point rule; §3's Renderer and AssetPipeline rows name their private libraries; §3 rule 3, `AGENTS.md`'s two lists and `Docs/ReviewChecklist.md`'s public-header item name HarfBuzz and SheenBidi.
- **m8. The text input's limits disagreed with the script string limit and with Caret's registered range** (`UiTextInputComponent.h`). Confirmed: 16,384 codepoints reach 64 KiB of UTF-8 while a script's write of a component holds 4,096 bytes, and Caret was registered up to 16,384 although a longer Text from a scene or automation makes End store more. Fixed: `MaxUiTextInputLength` is 1,024 (four bytes a codepoint at most, so the longest typed text fits a script's write), MaxLength is registered up to it from `Scene/UiTextInput.h`'s constant (n1's duplicate removed), Caret is registered without an upper bound, the header's sentence says that a longer Text is kept and typing then inserts nothing until it is below the limit, and decision 45 and Backlog §6 say so; the references were regenerated. The new live case "UiTextInput: a Text of the longest MaxLength fits a script's write, and Caret is registered for any Text" failed before the fix (65,536 > 4,096; Caret's maximum present) and passes after it.
- **m9. The LdrDepth amendment was still described as requested and pending.** Confirmed: main granted it in `1707cf47`. Fixed (the lead's decision 6): decision 57 (its title and bullet), stream W's row, risk 13, `UiPass.h`'s file comment and the loss case's comment state the grant; `milestone/m22` does not merge main in this step, so decision 57 records that stream W builds on the granted text and the milestone's merge of main brings the ledger row.
- **m10. W22.3's ownership table missed files, and decision 58 two cross-stream dependencies.** Confirmed: `SceneRendererOutput.cpp` fills the new `UiPassInputs` members and the QuadBatcher declaration's `PipelineCount` lives in `RenderPassDeclaration.h`, in no row; I's Python test builds a TextInput widget, which only T's kind makes; F's fallback case reads `FontData::Fallbacks`, which only S's format 2 and version 5 carry. Fixed (the lead's decision 7): W's row owns both (with the startup count's cases), I's and F's rows name their dependencies, decision 58 lists the I and T and the F and S pairs among the two-stream cases and asks E's scratch merge and the integration to keep that order, and F's case comment names S.
- **m11. The refusal of a font that names itself as a fallback was untested** (`FontImporterTests.cpp`). Confirmed. Fixed: the fallback case imports a font whose second fallback is its own handle (the helper's handle now a named constant) and expects a Validation error located at `/Fallbacks/1`, and the null fallback's error at `/Fallbacks/0`.
- **m12. World Text in front of a World canvas is overdrawn, and the accepted limit did not say so** (`UiPass.h`). Confirmed: world Text is recorded in the Text row and World canvases afterwards in the Ui row, both depth-tested without depth writes. Fixed by the reviewer's first option: risk 16, decision 51, `UiPass.h`'s file comment and Backlog §6 record that a World canvas draws over world Text where they overlap, whichever is nearer.
- **m13. `FindTextCaret` left a byte offset on skipped bytes undefined** (`TextMeasurement.h`). Confirmed: the text input keeps ill-formed bytes, so the caret can stand on one that no cluster begins at, ends at or holds. Fixed: a last rule gives the trailing edge of its line's cluster that ends last before it in logical order, or its line's start when none does (`TextMeasurement.h`, decision 48); the caret case gains "A\377\377" at byte 2 (A's trailing edge, 5) and "\377\377A" at byte 1 (the line's start, 0).

**Nits**

- **n1. Stale comments and a duplicated constant.** Confirmed each. Fixed: `UiTextInput.h` names `UiDrawItem::CaretByte`; `UiDrawList.h`'s container paragraph names `WorldCanvases`; `UiLayout.h`'s node rectangle and Visible say a World canvas's pixels and rectangle, and `UiCanvasLayout::Scale` says canvas pixels per canvas unit; `UiPass.h` names the loss case exactly ("... a World canvas draws ..."); `UiSystemTests.cpp`'s comment says a keyboard Up; `UiRegistration.cpp` uses `Scene/UiTextInput.h`'s `MaxUiTextInputLength` instead of its own double.
- **n2. Decision 56 pointed to decision 49 for `UiCanvasRenderMode`'s text, which gave none.** Confirmed: the registry keeps an enum's and each entry's description. Fixed: decision 49 freezes the enum's description and its Screen and World entries'.

**Open for the lead** (the reviewer's notes, not findings): an MSVC Dist build of the two libraries was not part of the contract's runs (decision 58: the Debug build, and clang-cl's Release and Dist), which the milestone-end `CI.py` makes; and HarfBuzz's generated `hb-ot-shaper-use-table.hh` derives from the archive's `src/ms-use/` data, which the vendored tree removed (`Vendor/HarfBuzz/VENDOR.md`) together with that directory's own `COPYING`. Neither changes this checkpoint.

## Integration (W22.3)

W22.3's six stream branches were merged onto `milestone/m22` at `20868e31` (C22.3, `56cf8b57`, with main `0f795411` merged in: `1707cf47`, the ledger amendment for `QuadBatchTarget::LdrDepth`, and `0f795411`, the product owner's NVIDIA driver 621.14, which renamed the golden device class to `nvidia-62x`, ADR 0028 decision 9 amended, `Ui.png` and `UiWidgets.png` moving with it) in the order S (`27400437`), F (`556c6609`), T (`d6567f3f`), W (`d3f83f2f`), I (`918bd9e0`), E (`c50d15b6`), then staged as one change: S before F, since F's importer-fallback case reads S's format 2, and E last, as stream E's scratch merge (`scratch/m22/w22.3-e-merge`, `2e7159fe`, local) took them. Each merge's tree equals the scratch merge's at the same step plus main's changes, so E's verified resolutions are the ones below.

- **A bug stream T found, fixed here** (the lead's note 3): `Lua::PushString` (`Scripting/LuaHelpers.cpp`) handed `lua_pushlstring` a default-constructed `std::string_view`'s null data pointer, which Luau's `api_check` asserts on (a fatal assert in Debug and Release), so any caller pushing an empty view crashed; it now pushes `""` for an empty view. T's two call sites, which pushed the empty literal to avoid the trap, are kept.

## Review of W22.3

One review pass of the integrated wave diff (ADR 0028 decision 11), read-only, of the staged change against `20868e31`, in three lenses (correctness and robustness; determinism, formats and integration; tests, parity and documentation), each reading every new file in full and every hunk with its context: 17 findings, 16 once the lenses' duplicates are merged (decision 41's licence record was raised by two lenses), none blocker or major, 9 minor and 7 nits. Every finding was checked against the code before it was fixed: all 16 are confirmed and fixed in this checkpoint, none rejected (one part of m1, a canvas beyond a perspective camera's FarClip, is not a defect, with the reason below). The lenses found sound, among others: FontData format 2's bounds before allocation, format 1 still loading and shaping data whole or absent; HarfBuzz's and SheenBidi's object lifetimes and failure paths (an allocation failure falls back to unshaped clusters or the base level; `hb_buffer_t::clear` resets the buffer's random state); the cluster, line-break, kerning and caret logic; the ray in double with `DetMath::Tan` and square roots only, cast only with a World canvas; the text latch, `EditUiTextInput`'s integer operations and its atomic write; non-finite World matrices dropped everywhere they are read; the ledger positions (TextLatch, FontData 2 and FontImporter 5, UiTextInput v1, IdSeeds 2013 to 2017, LdrDepth on the granted amendment, every registry appended); the recorded hashes and the Noto pins; and every acceptance case under its exact name, un-skipped.

**The lead's decisions on the review** (binding):

1. Main is not merged in this step: the M22 landing merges it and does the landing work the determinism lens lists (LdrDepth after M21's pipeline targets, the World-canvas ray through PixelPerfectCamera, StringTable renumbered to 13).
2. A gamepad cannot type into a text input (m8): an accepted limit, not new scope. A gamepad focuses, submits and leaves a text input, which a test asserts by gamepad; typing needs a text source, and a gamepad on-screen keyboard is out of M22's scope. Recorded as a departure from Roadmap M22's "every widget works by keyboard and gamepad" (decision 47's amendment, with the reason), a Backlog §6 row, Architecture §5.7's UiInput step and the skills.
3. HarfBuzz's licences (m4): decision 41 and the Approvals section are amended in place (367 kept files, the "Old MIT" `COPYING` and Microsoft's MIT `src/ms-use/COPYING`, the MSVC Debug, Release and Dist builds), and the lead authorizes the matching update of `0001-approvals.md`'s HarfBuzz row (a factual record of a second permissive licence file of the already-approved upstream archive; no new approval). The notices must carry both.
4. The frozen-header changes in `FontData.h` (m5) are accepted: `DeserializeFontPayload`'s `formatVersion` parameter defaulting to format 2, `MinFontUnitsPerEm`, `MaxFontUnitsPerEm` and `MaxFontShapingSourceSize`, recorded in the C22.3 interfaces table beside `UiPassPlan::WorldCanvases` and `TextMeasurement::Alignment`, with the reason.
5. The integration's record (m9): "The lead's decisions" keeps only what the lead's notes decided, and the streams' own choices move to a list of stream choices accepted at the integration; the Python each-kind test takes TextInput if its mechanism allows (it does: its undo is checked by entity id).
6. The fuzz cases (m7): the existing cases are strengthened, and no fuzz target is added (the hardening stage's target count is at its bound for this phase): FontImporter's case seeds iterations from a committed font with layout tables, FontData's case seeds its shaped payload from a real version 5 bake, and one of them measures and shapes mutated text with an accepted font, each case's cost kept about where it was.
7. The World canvas's pointer (m1): the near and far clip range is checked (a hit outside the camera's clip range is not a hit), with a test; occlusion by scene geometry stays a recorded limit (decision 50 amended, a Backlog §6 row): no physics or depth query in UiInteraction.
8. `Ui.GetRect`'s canvas (m2) is read with `FindReadableEntityByID`, with a test of the pending-destruction case; `Ui.SetFocus`'s refusal of a hidden element (m3) names the real cause generically, tested by behaviour and code, not by message string.
9. The redundant empty-string guards (n4) are removed only where `Lua::PushString`'s fix covers the path and a test exercises it with an empty string.
10. The stale test name (n6) is renamed to what it asserts, keeping the test, and `EditorHostViewportsTests.cpp`'s comment is fixed; every nit is fixed now.
11. Load and R-GPUCRASH as the integration's note 9; scripted file edits write LF.

**Minor**

- **m1. A World canvas took the pointer outside the camera's clip range, and nothing recorded that scene geometry does not occlude it** (`Scene/UiInteraction.cpp`; correctness). Confirmed: `MapUiPointerToCanvas` accepted any ray parameter from 0, and a perspective ray starts at the camera's position, so a canvas closer than NearClip, which the projection clips away, still took hover, presses and drags; an orthographic camera's canvas beyond its far plane alike; and decision 50, `UiInteraction.h` and the Backlog said nothing of geometry in front of a canvas, which the draw's depth test hides. Fixed (the lead's decision 7): `UiPointerRay` gains `MinDistance` and `MaxDistance` (a frozen-header addition, now in the C22.3 interfaces table), the ray parameters from its origin between which the camera draws, inclusive, defaulting to 0 and infinity; `ComputeUiPointerRay` fills them as viewport.pick's ray interval (`ComputeViewPixelRayInterval`) does, a perspective ray's from NearClip / c (c the ray's cosine with the camera's -Z) and an orthographic ray's from 0 to FarClip - NearClip (its origin is on the near plane); `MapUiPointerToCanvas` maps no point outside them, so `HitTestUiPointer` passes a clipped canvas over and a farther one within the range still takes the pointer, and a slider dragged past a clipping plane holds its value as when its ray leaves the plane. Not a defect, the reason the perspective ray has no end: the reviewer's "a canvas beyond FarClip (not rendered)" holds for an orthographic camera only; a perspective projection's far plane is at infinity (Architecture §8.3; the Camera row's FarClip is the orthographic range and the meshes' culling distance) and nothing culls a World canvas there, so such a canvas is drawn and takes the pointer, which the case pins. Occlusion stays a limit (decision 50's amendment, `UiInteraction.h`'s file comment, Backlog §6's World-canvas row, the game-building `ui` page: a game casts the pointer's ray with `Physics.Raycast` before it acts). New case "UiInteraction: a World canvas takes the pointer only within the camera's clip range": the ranges of a centred and a corner ray, a canvas before a perspective camera's near plane missed, one beyond its FarClip hit, an orthographic camera's canvases before its near and beyond its far plane missed and those between hit, a hand-made ray's range applied by `MapUiPointerToCanvas`, and a farther canvas taking the pointer a nearer clipped one leaves. Without the fix it failed (the ranges unset, the clipped canvases hit). The ray's origin and direction are computed as before, so no recorded hash moves (`UiWorld.replay` verifies at its hash in the four runs, below).
- **m2. `Ui.GetRect` gave an element pending destruction a nil `Canvas`, though `UiElementRect` declares `Canvas: Entity`** (`Scripting/Bindings/Ui.cpp`; correctness). Confirmed: the element was validated as readable (pending until the flush) and its node read from the current layout, but its canvas was pushed through `PushUiElement`, which looks up live entities only, so a canvas destroyed with its element came back nil and a `--!strict` script indexing it raised. Fixed (the lead's decision 8): the canvas is looked up with `FindReadableEntityByID`, as the text events' dispatch reads its input, and pushed as an entity while it is readable; a canvas destroyed with its element stays readable until the same flush, and no script runs between a flush and the next layout, so a laid-out readable element's canvas is always readable (nil remains a guard only); `GetFocus`, `GetHovered` and `GetPressed` keep `PushUiElement` (typed `Entity?`). New case "Ui: GetRect reports the canvas of an element pending destruction" (a script destroys the canvas, then reads its button's rectangle with its canvas in the same evaluation); without the fix it failed (`rect.Canvas` nil).
- **m3. `Ui.SetFocus`'s refusal of a hidden element blamed an off toggle, though W22.3 hides a text input's Placeholder too** (`Scene/UiSystem.cpp`; correctness). Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 8): the refusal says that the element is hidden, in an off toggle's Graphic or in a text input's Placeholder while its Text is not empty, which names both rules that set `UiLayoutNode::Hidden`; `UiLayout.h`'s `Hidden` comment names the Placeholder rule too. Tested by behaviour, not by its words: "UiSystem: SetFocus focuses a focusable element and refuses any other" refuses a button in a text input's Placeholder while the input has text (`InvalidArgument`, `CheckFocus` alike) and focuses it once the Text is emptied (the Placeholder shows; the check lays the scene out at the call). The message changed and the refusal did not, so the case passes with and without the change.
- **m4. Decision 41 and the approvals record still gave HarfBuzz's kept files and licence as before stream S restored `src/ms-use/COPYING`** (`Docs/Decisions/0033-m22-contract.md`; determinism and tests). Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 3): decision 41 is amended in place (the licence cell names Microsoft's MIT licence of `src/ms-use/COPYING`; what is kept is `COPYING`, the 365 files of `src/` and `src/ms-use/COPYING`, 367 files and 5,588,673 bytes; MSVC builds both libraries without warnings in Debug, Release and Dist), the Approvals section names the second licence, and `0001-approvals.md`'s HarfBuzz row names both licence files (the lead's authorization). The notices carry both: `Tests/Automation/test_notices.py` asserts that HarfBuzz's notice lists `src/ms-use/COPYING` and that the generated Windows notices hold its "Copyright (c) Microsoft Corporation." line, which no other licence file of the repository holds.
- **m5. `FontData.h`, a C22.3 frozen header, gained `DeserializeFontPayload`'s `formatVersion` parameter and three public constants that no record listed** (determinism). Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 4): the C22.3 interfaces table's `FontData.h` row lists the parameter (default `FontData::FormatVersion`; 1 reads a format 1 payload, which decision 44 requires) and `MinFontUnitsPerEm`, `MaxFontUnitsPerEm` and `MaxFontShapingSourceSize` (the bounds `ValidateFontData` and the fuzz case read), added by stream S and accepted by the lead, with the reason; the integration's stream choices name them.
- **m6. `Tests/Data/LICENSES.md` called two copies of the OFL Inter font first-party** (tests). Confirmed: its paragraph listed only `Assets/Formats/Fonts/Inter-Regular.otf`, while `Assets/Ui/Fonts/` (W22.1) and `Assets/Fonts/GoldenUiScripts/` (W22.3) hold copies imported from `Scaffold/Sources/Fonts/`. Fixed: the paragraph covers every copy of the Inter and Noto font fixtures in `Scaffold/Sources/Fonts/` or imported from there into `Assets/`, naming today's three directories, and the audio fixtures with their `Scaffold/Sources/Audio/` counterparts.
- **m7. Version 5's shaping readers were fuzzed only in passing** (`FontImporterTests.cpp`, `FontDataTests.cpp`; tests). Confirmed: seven FontImporter iterations in eight used Glyphs.ttf, which has no layout tables, so HarfBuzz's closure and subset rarely saw a lookup; FontData's shaped seed carried 64 bytes of 0x07 as its subset; and no fuzz case measured text with an accepted font. Fixed (the lead's decision 6), with no new target: "FontImporter: 200 seeded mutations of TrueType and OpenType fonts never crash" takes the Noto Sans Hebrew fixture (its GSUB composes letters and points, its GPOS places marks) one iteration in eight with the Hebrew block's range, Glyphs.ttf six in eight and Inter one, and its sample check asks that a font of a seed with layout tables (Inter or the Hebrew fixture) was accepted, shaped and subset; "FontData: 10,000 seeded mutations of a payload never crash" seeds its shaped payload from the Hebrew fixture baked by FontImporter version 5 (its letters and points at 16 pixels per em, with two fallbacks), and measures every payload the reader accepts with `MeasureText` and `FindTextCaret` on a mutated mixed-direction text (pointed Hebrew, Latin, digits and mirrored parentheses) in each base direction and alignment, wrapped, checking that every glyph and cluster lies in the text and on a line. The case names, so the hardening stage's targets, are unchanged; the costs (Release, the whole count): FontImporter's case from 0.41 s to 0.40 s, FontData's from 0.03 s to 0.08 s, its one bake of the Hebrew fixture included.
- **m8. A gamepad cannot type into the fifth widget, and no record said so** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed by the lead's decision 2: decision 47's amendment records the departure from Roadmap M22's "every widget works by keyboard and gamepad" for the text input's typing, with its reason; Backlog §6 gains the row "A gamepad cannot type into a text input" (a gamepad on-screen keyboard is new scope); Architecture §5.7's UiInput step, the luau-gameplay skill's UI callbacks line and the game-building `ui` page's Typing bullet say it. New case "PlaySession: a gamepad focuses, submits and leaves a text input, and types nothing into it": through the session's Ui bindings, gamepad taps focus the canvas's default focus, move down to the field, submit it with South, press West and North into it (nothing typed) and leave it with DPadUp, the Focus and Submit events in order and the Text empty.
- **m9. The integration's record listed the streams' own choices as binding lead decisions, and the Python each-kind test left TextInput out** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 5): "Integration (W22.3)" keeps under "The lead's decisions" only what the lead's notes decided (W's frozen-header addition, rectangle rule, GPU case and pipeline count; S's frozen-header addition, Arabic case, `src/ms-use/COPYING` and four limits; T's and F's frozen-case fixes; E's Noto copies, edits and the Ui replay's hash), and a new list holds the stream choices accepted at the integration. `test_ui_authoring`'s "each kind" test now builds a TextInput (three entities, its widget `TextInput` in `ui.tree`) and checks its first undo by the Text widget's entity id, the text input's ids staying, so the name it shares with the text input's child no longer matters.

**Nits**

- **n1. `UiPass.h` said that a text's glyphs are planned in text order** (correctness). Confirmed. Fixed: in MeasureText's order, line by line, each line in visual order (right-to-left runs reversed, a shaped cluster one glyph or several).
- **n2. `PlannedChain::Fonts` stored raw `FontData` pointers without saying what keeps them alive** (`Renderer/UiPass.cpp`; correctness). Confirmed. Fixed: the struct's comment says that they borrow the fonts of the resolver's `m_Fonts`, whose `AssetRef`s keep them alive and which never erases an entry during the plan (a reallocation moves the references, not the fonts).
- **n3. `DragOrigin` was documented in scene pixels, though a World canvas's drag stores its canvas pixels** (`Scene/UiInteraction.h`; determinism). Confirmed. Fixed: the pixels of the dragged widget's canvas (scene pixels for a Screen canvas, the canvas's own for a World canvas).
- **n4. `Input.GetText` and the text events' dispatch kept empty-string guards that `Lua::PushString`'s fix made redundant** (`Bindings/Input.cpp`, `Private/ScriptInstances.cpp`; tests). Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 9): both push the view as it is (`Input.GetText` the host's view, the dispatch an empty view for an element without a `UiTextInput`), and the comments say that `PushString` pushes an empty view as "". Each path runs with an empty string: "InputBindings: physical and injected state obey independent phase latches" calls `Input.GetText()` in a host without a session (the default host's view, without a data pointer), and "UiEvents: OnUiTextChanged and OnUiSubmit receive the input's text and bubble, and their names are reserved" now dispatches a TextChanged to the field after its `UiTextInput` is removed, which both receivers hear with an empty text.
- **n5. Three edited comments lost their wrapping** (`Renderer/QuadBatcher.h`'s `Create`, `Tests/Source/Golden/GoldenTests.cpp`'s file comment, `Scripting/ScriptEngine.h`'s `DispatchUiEvents`; tests). Confirmed. Fixed: re-wrapped at the comments' width.
- **n6. A test name and a comment still said that the scene view draws no game UI** (`UiExtractionTests.cpp`, `EditorHostViewportsTests.cpp`; tests). Confirmed. Fixed (the lead's decision 10): the case is "RenderExtraction: the editor's scene view draws no Screen canvas, and a view without a primary camera draws it" (its assertions kept; "RenderExtraction: World canvases draw in every world view at their render pose, Screen canvases only in game and camera views" asserts decision 51's World canvases in the scene view, which its comment names), W22.1's acceptance list names it with its old name, and the editor test's comment says that the scene view draws no Screen canvas (decisions 7 and 51).
- **n7. Architecture's Ui pass row, `RenderSnapshot` listing and UiInput step missed parts of World canvases** (tests). Confirmed. Fixed: the Ui row's target says that World canvases test `SceneDepth` without writes; the listing's `Ui` comment says that a World canvas's items are in its own pixels; §5.7's step 1b gains decision 50's pointer (the ray cast only while the layout holds a World canvas, a Screen canvas's target first, then the nearest World canvas whose rectangle the ray meets within the camera's clip range, drags along its plane, navigation within one render space, no occlusion) and the gamepad's text-input limit.

**Runs** (a checkpoint's targeted runs, strict, ADR 0028 decision 11): `Build.py --jobs 12` of Debug, Release and Dist (20, 20 and 17 projects, 0 warnings); the two regression cases alone, in a Tests build with only their fixes' behaviour removed (the `UiPointerRay` members kept so the case compiles): m1's case failed (9 of its 21 assertions: the ranges unset, and the canvases before a near plane, beyond an orthographic far plane and outside a hand-made range hit) and m2's failed (`rect.Canvas` nil), then both passed with the fixes; `Test.py --suite unit --config Debug` on the touched suites (302 cases; the first run failed only the FontData fuzz case's setup, which required the Hebrew bake to reach glyphs no codepoint maps, which that fixture's GSUB does not from its letters and points, so it requires shaping data instead; then the font cases passed, 29), then the whole Debug unit suite (4,349 cases passed, the 36 child-process targets skipped); `Test.py --suite unit --config Release` on the same suites (302 passed, the shaped-text digest, Inter's atlas hash and the fuzz cases at their full counts among them: FontData's case accepted 813 payloads of 10,000, FontImporter's 22 fonts of 200); `Lint.py` strict (0 findings, after two Python lines it found too long were wrapped), `Format.py --check` (passed, after a nested conditional clang-format would wrap became a table), `GenerateDocs.py --check --config Debug` (21 references verified: no registered text changed) and `FetchAssets.py fixture --check` (passed); then, each with `--require-gpu`: gpu Debug on `UiPass*`, `EditorHostViewports*` and `QuadBatcher*` under both API caps (37 cases each), golden Release (31 matched on `nvidia-62x`), feature Debug and Release (253 cases, 73 suites and replays, the six editor-mode gates green, FeatureTest unchanged), export Release and Dist (66 suites and 7 replays in each mode, the six gates green in each, the coverage merge green), determinism Debug, Release and Dist (the simulation path changed: FeatureTest's 7 replays at their committed hashes in the four runs, `TextInput.replay` `76aecb0dd6e1153a`, `UiWorld.replay` `365eabca1abb20c0` and `Ui.replay` `14057c3734e9a42b` among them, each with input events failing without it), games Debug (Tetris, Breakout and NativeTest: the audit and the Debug editor half, the upgrade dry run clean, every replay at its committed hash and failing without its input) and automation (768 tests, method coverage 101/101), all passed. No load-only timeout and no Tests run that ended without a report, though the whole Debug unit run overlapped the GPU suites. Edited after the runs: this section only, which no check reads (ADR 0023).

## Landing

M22 lands after M21 in phase 2 (ADR 0028 decision 17's merge train: M21 and M22 in readiness order, then M23), so it is the later lander of the two: it merges main, appends its ledger entries after M21's, adapts its UI to M21's PixelPerfectCamera and adds the combined 2D/UI test (ADR 0028's phase-2 ledger).

**The merge** (`0f201a3c`, "Merge main into milestone/m22 (M21, the silent-exit evidence)"): `milestone/m22` at W22.3's checkpoint `66f6e1c9` merged main's `969341c2` (M21's landing, main's silent-exit evidence `f30f6cdd`; the golden rename `0f795411` was already merged). 159 files conflicted; every conflict kept both milestones' intent by the ledger, M21's entries first:

- **Registries and appended lists** (shared integration files and the ledger's append-only lists): `AssetType`, its name and parse, `AutomationTypes.cpp`'s entry, `Icons` (enumerator, glyph, mapping), the asset loader and importer registries, `RegisterAssetTypes`, `AssetCreateType`, the native-property kinds, `MakeCanonicalFile` and the upgraded extensions, the content browser's create list (Tilemap at 9, String table at 10), `ModuleRules.json`'s authored extensions, `RegisterSharedMethods.cpp` (physics2d, then ui), `RegisterMethods.cpp` (tilemap.paint, then ui.createWidget), the callbacks' declarations (`Bindings/Script.cpp`: the 2D physics callbacks, then the UI ones) and their reserved names (`ScriptData.cpp`), the dispatch (`ScriptInstances.cpp`: M21's `DeliverPhysics` and `PublishPhysicsDiagnostic` beside M22's `InvokeUi`), `RenderSnapshot`'s members and its allocator list (Sprites, Tilemaps, Lights2D, then Ui), `RenderExtractionRequest` (SortingLayers, then UiInteraction), `EditorViewportOptions`, `viewport.setOptions`' params and result (mode2D, then uiPreview), `Tools/MCP/tests/test_schemas.py`'s proxied tools, the reference generator's authored formats (alphabetical: SpriteSheet, StringTable, Tilemap) and schemas, the validation fixtures' component order and settings (`MakeValidationFixtures.py`, then regenerated), `Tests/Data/LICENSES.md`'s fixture paragraph, the golden-images skill and `Projects/FeatureTest/AGENTS.md`.
- **AssetType:** M21's `Tilemap` keeps 12; M22's `StringTable` is renumbered to 13 (the enumerator, `AssetTypeToString` and `AssetTypeFromString`, `AutomationTypes.cpp`, `Icons`, the registries, "AssetType: persisted values never change", Architecture §7.1); `CookedFormat.cpp` keeps M21's range that follows the enumeration (ADR 0032 decision 31). The slot's cooked caches (the `Library/` folders of FeatureTest, Tetris, Breakout and NativeTest, and the exports) were deleted before any editor or test read them, so no cooked StringTable stamped 12 is read.
- **Settings sections** after Testing: SortingLayers, Physics2D (M21), Localization, Ui (M22), in `ProjectSettings`, its registration, every canonical fixture (`AllSettings.eproj`, the coverage-gap fixture, the validation fixtures) and Architecture §6.1; `Scaffold/Baseline/FeatureTest.eproj` reconciled to hold all four (ADR 0032's observation).
- **Validation codes:** M21's block after `PREFAB_CYCLE` (`SPRITE_UNKNOWN_SORTING_LAYER`, `SPRITE_FRAME_OUT_OF_RANGE`, `SPRITE_UNKNOWN_CLIP`, `PHYSICS2D_MIXED_WORLDS`, `PHYSICS2D_OFF_PLANE`, `TILEMAP_FRAME_OUT_OF_RANGE`, `SPRITE_FRAME_OUTSIDE_TEXTURE`), then M22's (`UI_ELEMENT_WITHOUT_CANVAS`, `UI_IMAGE_FRAME_OUT_OF_RANGE`, `UI_WIDGET_PART_INVALID`), in both of `ProjectValidator.cpp`'s tables, kept in step, the fixtures, Architecture §13.7 and the game-debugging skill's validation-codes page in the same order; "ProjectValidator: GetCodes lists the codes the validator reports, each once" checks M21's chain and M22's block right after it (47 codes plus the physics ones).
- **BuiltinComponents categories:** Core, Rendering, Physics, Audio, Scripting, Rendering2D, Physics2D (M21), Ui (M22): 45 components.
- **PlaySessionPhase:** exactly the ledger's execution order: the fixed step InterpolationSnapshot, ApplyInput, TextLatch, UiInput, StartFlush, UiEvents, FixedUpdate, Tasks, NativeStep, SpriteAnimation, PreStepTransformUpdate, PhysicsPreStep, PhysicsStep, PhysicsPostStep, Physics2DPreStep, Physics2DStep, Physics2DPostStep, DestroyFlush, PostStepTransformUpdate, UiLayout; the frame phase LatchFrame, FrameStartFlush, Update, LateUpdate, FrameDestroyFlush, FrameTransformUpdate, FrameUiLayout, AudioUpdate, RenderExtraction (the enum, `FixedStep` and `RunFramePhase` merged without reordering; the phase-list cases of both milestones, `PlaySessionTests.cpp`, `PlaySession2DTests.cpp` and `NativeWorldTests.cpp`, list every phase of both). (Amended 2026-11-15: ADR 0038 decision 14 removed `FrameStartFlush`; the frame phase runs no start flush.)
- **State-hash blocks:** physics, native, physics2d (M21), ui (M22).
- **Render passes:** the ledger's list; `QuadBatchTarget::LdrDepth` appended after M21's `HdrLit` and `Normals` (ADR 0033 decision 57, main's ledger row), changing none of them: `QuadBatcher.{h,cpp}`, M21's file, taken from main with LdrDepth re-applied (its pipeline index 4, its description `QuadLdrDepth` on `PSLdr`, Ldr's binding layout and framebuffer, depth-tested without writes); `QuadBatcher::PipelineCount` 5 and the declaration's count 5, so 62 startup pipelines (M17's 54, M18's 4, M21's 3, M22's 1), asserted in "Pipelines: Create makes exactly the declared startup pipelines", "RenderPassDeclarations: the Lights2D pass declares one pipeline of its program and the QuadBatcher five" (renamed from "four": M21's case asserts the merged count) and the scene renderer's pipeline cases; 28 stats rows in the ledger's order (Sprites and Lights2D after Overdraw, Ui after Text).
- **ModuleRules.json:** `SnapshotHeaders` and Scene's `Headers` hold `UiDrawList.h`; HarfBuzz and SheenBidi's `ThirdParty`, private and Renderer and AssetPipeline lists; `Use<Lib>()`, `IncludeDir`, the premake includes, `VENDOR_ABI_FAMILIES` and Lint's ABI-macro prefixes merged without conflict, after main's.
- **M21's single-owner files:** `InspectorPanel.cpp` and `SceneHierarchyPanel` merged without conflict and hold exactly W22.2's calls (the UiRect drawer, the Create UI menus and the class comment); `EditorHostViewports.cpp`, `SceneViewportPanel.{h,cpp}` are main's with W22.2's "as built" calls re-applied (decision 33's list), where M21's 2D mode meets the UI preview: the preview turns off the scene view's debug view and M21's 2D grid together (`&& !uiPreview` on the block that sets both), hides the gizmo as an armed tile brush does, and its presses are the UI authoring overlay's, never a tile-brush stroke (the brush sees them as captured). `EditorApp`, `Autosave`, `EditorLayer` and `GizmoOverlay` M22 never changed; `EditorViewportHost.h` keeps M21's `EditorViewportImage::Alpha`.
- **Tests both milestones added at one place** (parallel test cases): both kept, main's first (the asset-type registration, content browser, asset and project methods, reference generator, scene viewport and editor host cases); counts that both sides raised from one base take both (the importer registry 14, the settings structs 15, the Behaviour callbacks 25, the golden scaffolds' source copies 16, the MCP tools 68 and the method list 105).
- **Generated and audited files:** `Docs/Reference/*`, `Resources/Scripting/Engine.d.luau` and `Tools/MCP/catalog.json`, the demos' `.eproj`, `BuildLog.jsonl` and `Provenance.json`, and FeatureTest's `FeatureTest.eproj` and `Provenance.json` took main's side and were regenerated in the next commit; `Scaffolds.json` lists main's scaffolds, then M22's, before `Suites.jsonl`; `Suites.jsonl`'s one `Testing.Suites` patch line is the union by hand (83 suites, each side's additions placed after the entries they followed); the editor-UI goldens took main's (regenerated below); every scene golden of both milestones is in `Tests/Golden/nvidia-62x/`.
- **Docs:** Architecture (21 conflicting hunks merged word by word against the merge base, then the counts, §6.8's two cooked formats and §13.7's two code blocks by hand), the Roadmap, Backlog (main's rows first) and the skills keep both sides.

The merge commit built in Debug (20 projects, 0 warnings) before the adaptations.

**Landing adaptations** (the next commit; each with a test that fails without it, run alone against a build with the fix removed):

- **a. One scene resolution for the session and extraction** (decision 3, risk 4): `GetCameraOutputSettings` (`Scene/RenderExtraction.h`) is the one place a camera's output settings are decided (its PixelPerfectCamera's when it holds one, else the defaults); extraction sets `RenderSnapshot::Output` from it, and `ComputeGameViewOutputMapping` gives the game view's mapping through the primary camera, which the session's UiLayout and FrameUiLayout phases (`GetUiSceneResolution`) lay out at. "PlaySession: under a PixelPerfectCamera the UI is laid out at its reference resolution and the pointer maps through its upscale" (a 200 x 120 camera in a 640 x 360 view: the session's layout is 200 x 120, the game view's Output and its UI items the same).
- **b. UiInput's pointer mapping** (decision 24): `GetUiOutputMapping` is `ComputeGameViewOutputMapping`, so a pointer at a view pixel hits the scene pixel the image shows there, and one in the letterbox hits nothing where no element reaches past the image's edge (the same case: view (170, 120) is over button B at scene (50, 40); view (10, 100), in the left bar, over nothing, since B starts at x = 0). The milestone review found that an element or a World canvas reaching past the edge still took a press from a bar, and made the claim hold for every target (decision 24's amendment, "PlaySession: under a PixelPerfectCamera a click in the letterbox reaches no UI, though an element or a World canvas reaches past the image").
- **c. The World-canvas pointer ray** (decision 50, the C22.3 review's m2, risk 11): `ComputeUiPointerRay` casts through a PixelPerfectCamera as extraction draws it: Orthographic with `ComputePixelPerfectView`'s half height, from the position it snaps the simulated position to, whatever the Camera's own projection. "UiInteraction: under a PixelPerfectCamera the pointer's ray is the inverse of its pixel-perfect projection" (a perspective Camera at (0.03, 0.02, 10) holding a 200 x 120 camera of 16 pixels per unit: rays along -Z from the snapped (0, 0) view's near plane, the corner at (6.25, 3.75), one scene pixel 1/16 unit).
- **d. The combined 2D/UI test** (ADR 0028's ledger "Golden scenes and goldens"; decision 14's IdSeed row 2090–2099): below.
- **e. The other later-lander items** found in ADR 0032 and this record: the ledger positions above (decisions 3, 24, 29, 30, 38, 57; ADR 0032 decisions 16, 23, 31, risk 7); the QuadBatcher target appended after M21's (risk 13, decision 57); M21's editor files re-applied (decision 33, risk 10); `StringTable` renumbered (risk 5); the baseline `.eproj` reconciled (ADR 0032's W21.1 review observation); ADR 0032's IdSeed row 1990–1999 amended (M22 took the combined test); and, beyond the ledger's list, the consumers of a scene resolution that would otherwise disagree with the game view under a PixelPerfectCamera: `ui.tree`'s default layouts (the play fallback and the edit scene at the project's window size; an explicit `width` and `height` stay the scene resolution asked for: "UiMethods: ui.tree lays the edit scene out at a PixelPerfectCamera's reference resolution by default"), and the editor's UI preview, whose overlay lays the UI out at the game frame's scene resolution and places, picks and drags it through the same mapping ("SceneViewportPanel: the UI preview places and picks the UI through a PixelPerfectCamera's upscale"); the UiRect drawer's anchor presets lay the UI out at the same scene resolution (it computes the layout the preview shows with the same function; the milestone review gave it a case of its own, "UiRectDrawer: under a PixelPerfectCamera a preset keeps the rectangle in the reference resolution's canvas", and the preview a case with letterbox bars, "SceneViewportPanel: the UI preview picks and drags through a PixelPerfectCamera's letterboxed upscale, and the letterbox picks nothing", each failing without its adaptation).
- **f. What the merged tree broke and the landing fixes:** `docs.get`'s first-page section list (ADR 0026, frictions S1-01 and S2-05) holds the deepest heading level whose list fits in 12 KB; the merged `ScriptAPI.md`'s second level (147 sections, about 12.8 KB; main's 123 took 10.7 KB, M22's 134 11.6 KB) no longer fits, so its first page listed the title alone. A list whose deepest fitting level is a lone entry now takes the next level's leading entries that fit (`ObserveMethods.cpp`'s `MakeSectionList`; `ObserveMethods.h`, the `sections` description and Architecture §13.10 say so): "ObserveMethods: docs.get returns every repository topic inline and the script API reference completely in pages" failed on the merged tree before the fix (the first page listed no `input` section) and passes after it. The counts both sides raised are merged as above ("ReferenceGenerator: the references list every component, field, enum value, method, extension and validation code": 22 references, both new schemas), and M21's 2D mode and M22's UI preview meet in `SceneViewportPanel.cpp` as the merge bullet says (the milestone review added the combination's case, "SceneViewportPanel: an armed tile brush paints nothing when a click lands in the UI preview": two guards keep the preview's presses from the brush, the overlay's item owning them and the brush's `Captured` flag, and the case fails only without both).
- **The departure in Roadmap M22's acceptance** (the lead's note after W22.3): "every widget works by keyboard and gamepad" now names the text input's recorded departure (decision 47, Backlog §6).

**The combined 2D/UI test.** Two scaffolds of M22's range, listed after W22.3's and before `Suites.jsonl`:

| IdSeed | Scaffold | Content |
|---|---|---|
| 2090 | `Combined2DUiGolden.jsonl` (a golden scaffold, `GoldenDirectories` `Assets/Scenes/Combined2DUiGolden` and `Assets/Scripts/Combined2DUiGolden`) | `Combined2DUi.scene`: a Camera holding a 200 x 120 PixelPerfectCamera of 16 pixels per unit, three sprites (the Ground strip along the bottom 16 rows, the Hero standing on it at (-3, -2.25), a Coin) and the Hud canvas with the Step button (scene pixels (8, 8) to (64, 26), its label) and the Counter label at the top right; the Hud's `Stepper` behaviour moves the Hero one unit right and updates the Counter on each click of Step |
| 2091 | `Combined2DUiReplay.jsonl` | `Assets/Tests/Replays/Combined2DUi.replay`, recorded as M20's and M21's are: `input.record` around a `play.step` of planned pointer input (never `Replays.jsonl`, no second `Testing.Suites` patch): two clicks of the button at view (256, 102), its centre at scene (36, 17) in the 1280 x 720 deterministic view (FeatureTest's window: the reference resolution upscaled 6 times with 40-pixel bars), and a press at view (20, 400), in the left bar, which hits nothing; its expectations at ticks 30 and 60 read the Hero at x = -1 after two steps and the Counter's "Steps 2" |

- **The golden** `Combined2DUi` ("Golden: Combined2DUi", `Tests/Source/Golden/Combined2DUiGoldenTests.cpp`): the scene at 640 x 360, the reference resolution upscaled 3 times into 600 x 360 between 20-pixel bars; the case also checks on every device the bars, the 3 x 3 blocks of the button, the sky, the Hero and the Ground, and the button's edge. `Tests/Golden/nvidia-62x/Combined2DUi.png` was written with `--update-golden --filter "Golden: Combined2DUi"` and reviewed by eye: the maroon letterbox bars, the slate sky, the green Ground strip, the orange Hero square standing on it, the yellow Coin, the light blue Step button with its white label at the top left and "Steps 0" at the top right, every pixel a 3 x 3 block. A first candidate, with a 10 x 10-unit bevelled Ground sprite that covered most of the view, was rejected at review and the scene reshaped before it was committed.
- **The replay:** `Assets/Tests/Replays/Combined2DUi.replay`, 61 ticks at 64 Hz, seed 1414: checkpoint at tick 60 `4568c68a164c5f05`, final state hash `bd51214863ea28c9` at tick 61 (the recording's expectations passed when `Scaffold.py` recorded it).
- **Coverage in every run mode:** the replay runs in the Editor, Release and Dist runs through the `Testing.Replays` glob (the feature and export suites), at the same hash, and fails without its input (the determinism and games suites' input check); the scene is a soak subject of the next hardened gate.
- **Textures for M23's `Compression: None`** (ADR 0028's ledger; stated by the milestone review): the landing's scaffolds import no texture (the combined scene's sprites draw M21's built-in procedural sprite textures, which are never compressed), and neither does the milestone review's `UiFallback.jsonl` (fonts only), so M22's complete list is decision 7's `Assets/Textures/GoldenUi/Rgba.png` (`UiGolden.jsonl`): W22.2 and W22.3 import none ("Integration (W22.2)"; "Integration (W22.3)", stream E's bullet), and no importer or creation path of M22 makes a texture (`StringTableImporter`, `asset.create {type: StringTable}` and `ui.createWidget` make none; a font's atlas is never compressed, the ledger).

**The editor-UI goldens** (ADR 0028's ledger: the later of M21 and M22 regenerates them on the merged tree, never merging them as files; main's were taken at the merge). `Test.py --suite golden --config Release --require-gpu --update-golden --filter` on the six cases (`EditorDefaultLayout` and the five `EditorDesign` images): four candidates (`EditorDefaultLayout`, `EditorDesignSelected1280x720`, `EditorDesignLauncher1280x720`, `EditorDesignSupporting1600x900`) are byte-identical to main's, which already hold M21's "2D" button (ADR 0032, "Integration (W21.1)"); M22's editor changes (the Overlays popup's UI preview option, the hierarchy's Create UI menus, a UiRect's anchor presets) appear in no capture. The other two differ from main's by 1/255 in one channel at 1 and 3 pixels, inside the scene view's rendered image, the same in every run: `EditorDesignSelected1600x900` at (816, 411) (214 to 215 in red), `EditorDesignSelected2560x1440-2x` at (1274, 661), (1010, 766) and (1018, 787) (box (1010, 661) to (1274, 787)). Neither M21's button nor an M22 editor change explains them: a build that draws the scene image over the panel's cursor rectangle as main does (W22.2 draws it over the measured rectangle) gives the same candidates, and main's goldens were renamed to `nvidia-62x` byte for byte without regeneration (`0f795411`: on driver 621.14 at most 1/255 in 23 images), so they are that driver's rounding. They are far within the comparison's thresholds, so main's images are kept, not regenerated, and the lead has the report; nothing in the editor-UI goldens changes at the landing.

**The audited projects.** Main's `.eproj` of Tetris, Breakout and NativeTest (taken at the merge) hold M21's SortingLayers and Physics2D sections but not M22's Localization and Ui, so their canonical form changes: the recorded `Editor --headless --renderer none --audio-device none --user-data-dir <scratch> --project <P> --upgrade` from this branch's Release editor rewrote only `Tetris.eproj`, `Breakout.eproj` and `NativeTest.eproj`, each gaining `Localization` and `Ui` after `Physics2D`, with their transcript and provenance lines; no scene, replay or hash changed, and `AuditProvenance.py --project <P> --files disk` passes for the three (74, 50 and 20 engine-consumed files). FeatureTest: `Scaffold.py` on the merged tree wrote the combined test's new files and `Automation/Provenance.json`; every other file, every committed replay among them, came back byte for byte, so every replay of both milestones keeps its hash.

feature Debug and Release (the six editor-mode gates green, FeatureTest unchanged; 94 suites with the 11 replays); export Release and Dist (the export suite's "83 suite(s), 11 replay(s)" counts the configured `Testing.Suites`: 77 of them ran in each exported mode, since the six suites of `Modes: ["Editor"]` do not run exported, with the 11 replays, 88 entries and 287 cases in `FeatureTest-Release.json` and `-Dist.json`; stated by the milestone review; the six gates green, the coverage merge green); the automation suite in full (790 tests, method coverage 105 of 105); determinism Debug, Release and Dist (the 11 replays at their committed hashes in the Editor Debug, Editor Release, exported Release and exported Dist runs, which agree, and each with input failing without it);
